   40: function toStringList(
   41:   value: unknown,
   42: ): string[] {
   43:   if (typeof value === "string") {
   44:     return value.trim()
   45:       ? [value.trim()]
   46:       : [];
   47:   }
   48: 
   49:   if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
   50:     return [];
   51:   }
   52: 
   53:   return value
   54:     .map((item) => {
   55:       if (
   56:         typeof item === "string"
   57:       ) {
   58:         return item.trim();
   59:       }
   60: 
   61:       if (
   62:         item &&
   63:         typeof item === "object"
   64:       ) {
   65:         const candidate =
   66:           item as Record<
   67:             string,
   68:             unknown
   69:           >;
   70: 
   71:         const text =
   72:           candidate.description ??
   73:           candidate.text ??
   74:           candidate.point;
   75: 
   76:         return typeof text ===
   77:           "string"
   78:           ? text.trim()
   79:           : "";
   80:       }
   81: 
   82:       return "";
   83:     })
   84:     .filter(Boolean);
   85: }
   86: 
   87: function getQuestionExtras(
   88:   question: unknown,
   89: ) {
   90:   const candidate =
   91:     question as Record<
   92:       string,
   93:       unknown
   94:     >;
   95: 
   96:   return {
   97:     examinerGuidance:
   98:       toStringList(
   99:         candidate.examinerGuidance,
  100:       ),
  101: 
  102:     misconceptions:
  103:       toStringList(
  104:         candidate.misconceptions,
  105:       ),
  106:   };
  107: }

  303:   async function logIncident(
  304:     type:
  305:       | "fullscreen_exit"
  306:       | "fullscreen_restored"
  307:       | "page_hidden"
  308:       | "page_visible",
  309:     detail: string,

  346:   async function terminateForIntegrity(
  347:     reason: string,
  348:   ) {
  349:     if (
  350:       !assignment ||
  351:       !user?.uid ||
  352:       finishingRef.current ||
  353:       locked
  354:     ) {
  355:       return;
  356:     }
  357: 
  358:     finishingRef.current = true;
  359: 
  360:     if (
  361:       countdownTimer.current
  362:     ) {
  363:       clearInterval(
  364:         countdownTimer.current,
  365:       );
  366:       countdownTimer.current =
  367:         null;
  368:     }
  369: 
  370:     setFullscreenCountdown(
  371:       null,
  372:     );
  373: 
  374:     setSaving(true);
  375: 
  376:     try {
  377:       await terminateExamForIntegrity(
  378:         {
  379:           assignmentId:
  380:             assignment.id,
  381: 
  382:           studentId:
  383:             user.uid,
  384: 
  385:           answers,
  386: 
  387:           questionNumber:
  388:             activeQuestionNumber,
  389: 
  390:           reason,
  391:         },
  392:       );
  393: 
  394:       setSubmission(
  395:         (current) =>
  396:           current
  397:             ? {
  398:                 ...current,
  399:                 status:
  400:                   "submitted",
  401: 
  402:                 answers,
  403: 
  404:                 integrityTerminated:
  405:                   true,
  406: 
  407:                 integrityTerminationReason:
  408:                   reason,
  409:               }
  410:             : current,
  411:       );
  412: 
  413:       if (
  414:         document.fullscreenElement
  415:       ) {
  416:         await document
  417:           .exitFullscreen()
  418:           .catch(() => undefined);
  419:       }
  420: 
  421:       toast.error(
  422:         "Exam terminated and submitted because the integrity rule was triggered.",
  423:       );
  424:     } catch (error) {
  425:       console.error(
  426:         "Integrity termination failed:",
  427:         error,
  428:       );
  429: 
  430:       finishingRef.current =
  431:         false;
  432: 
  433:       toast.error(
  434:         "The exam could not be submitted automatically. Please contact your teacher.",
  435:       );
  436:     } finally {
  437:       setSaving(false);
  438:     }
  439:   }
  440: 
  441:   function beginFullscreenCountdown() {
  442:     if (
  443:       fullscreenExitActiveRef.current ||
  444:       !assignment ||
  445:       locked ||
  446:       finishingRef.current
  447:     ) {
  448:       return;
  449:     }
  450: 
  451:     fullscreenExitActiveRef.current =
  452:       true;
  453: 
  454:     const seconds =
  455:       assignment.integrityPolicy
  456:         .fullscreenExitCountdownSeconds;
  457: 
  458:     setFullscreenCountdown(
  459:       seconds,
  460:     );
  461: 
  462:     void logIncident(
  463:       "fullscreen_exit",
  464:       `Fullscreen was exited. A ${seconds}-second return countdown started.`,
  465:     );
  466: 
  467:     let remaining = seconds;
  468: 
  469:     countdownTimer.current =
  470:       setInterval(() => {
  471:         remaining -= 1;
  472: 
  473:         setFullscreenCountdown(
  474:           Math.max(
  475:             remaining,
  476:             0,
  477:           ),
  478:         );
  479: 
  480:         if (remaining <= 0) {
  481:           if (
  482:             countdownTimer.current
  483:           ) {
  484:             clearInterval(
  485:               countdownTimer.current,
  486:             );
  487:             countdownTimer.current =
  488:               null;
  489:           }
  490: 
  491:           void terminateForIntegrity(
  492:             "The learner exited fullscreen and did not return within 5 seconds.",
  493:           );
  494:         }
  495:       }, 1000);
  496:   }
  497: 
  498:   function resolveFullscreenExit() {
  499:     if (
  500:       !fullscreenExitActiveRef.current
  501:     ) {
  502:       return;
  503:     }
  504: 
  505:     fullscreenExitActiveRef.current =
  506:       false;
  507: 
  508:     if (
  509:       countdownTimer.current
  510:     ) {
  511:       clearInterval(
  512:         countdownTimer.current,
  513:       );
  514: 
  515:       countdownTimer.current =
  516:         null;
  517:     }
  518: 
  519:     setFullscreenCountdown(
  520:       null,
  521:     );
  522: 
  523:     void logIncident(
  524:       "fullscreen_restored",
  525:       "Fullscreen was restored before the five-second termination countdown expired.",
  526:     );
  527:   }
  528: 
  529:   const handleExamFullscreenChange = useEffectEvent(() => {
  530:     if (
  531:       finishingRef.current
  532:     ) {
  533:       return;
  534:     }
  535: 
  536:     if (
  537:       document.fullscreenElement ===
  538:       examRootRef.current
  539:     ) {
  540:       resolveFullscreenExit();
  541:     } else {
  542:       beginFullscreenCountdown();
  543:     }
  544:   });
  545: 
  546:   const handleExamVisibilityChange = useEffectEvent(() => {
  547:     if (
  548:       finishingRef.current ||
  549:       !assignment
  550:         ?.integrityPolicy
  551:         .monitorPageVisibility
  552:     ) {
  553:       return;
  554:     }
  555: 
  556:     if (
  557:       document.visibilityState ===
  558:       "hidden"
  559:     ) {
  560:       void logIncident(
  561:         "page_hidden",
  562:         "The exam page became hidden.",
  563:       );
  564: 
  565:       const action =
  566:         assignment
  567:           .integrityPolicy
  568:           .visibilityAction;
  569: 
  570:       if (
  571:         action ===
  572:         "auto_submit"
  573:       ) {
  574:         void terminateForIntegrity(
  575:           "The exam page became hidden and the teacher configured immediate automatic submission.",
  576:         );
  577: 
  578:         return;
  579:       }
  580: 
  581:       if (action === "pause") {
  582:         setIntegrityPaused(
  583:           true,
  584:         );
  585:       } else {
  586:         setIntegrityWarning(
  587:           "The exam page was hidden. This incident has been recorded.",
  588:         );
  589:       }
  590:     } else {
  591:       void logIncident(
  592:         "page_visible",
  593:         "The exam page became visible again.",
  594:       );
  595:     }
  596:   });
  597: 
  598:   useEffect(() => {
  599:     if (
  600:       !assignment ||
  601:       !user?.uid ||
  602:       locked ||
  603:       !integrityStarted ||
  604:       !assignment.integrityPolicy
  605:         .enabled
  606:     ) {
  607:       return;
  608:     }
  609: 
  610:     function onFullscreenChange() {
  611:       handleExamFullscreenChange();
  612:     }
  613: 
  614:     function onVisibilityChange() {
  615:       handleExamVisibilityChange();
  616:     }
  617: 
  618:     document.addEventListener(
  619:       "fullscreenchange",
  620:       onFullscreenChange,
  621:     );
  622: 
  623:     document.addEventListener(
  624:       "visibilitychange",
  625:       onVisibilityChange,
  626:     );
  627: 
  628:     return () => {
  629:       document.removeEventListener(
  630:         "fullscreenchange",
  631:         onFullscreenChange,
  632:       );
  633: 
  634:       document.removeEventListener(
  635:         "visibilitychange",
  636:         onVisibilityChange,
  637:       );
  638:     };
  639:   }, [
  640:     assignment,
  641:     integrityStarted,
  642:     locked,
  643:     user?.uid,
  644:   ]);
  645: 
  646:   async function enterExamMode() {
  647:     if (
  648:       !assignment ||
  649:       !user?.uid ||
  650:       !examRootRef.current
  651:     ) {
  652:       return;
  653:     }
  654: 
  655:     if (
  656:       !assignment.integrityPolicy
  657:         .enabled
  658:     ) {
  659:       setIntegrityStarted(
  660:         true,
  661:       );
  662:       return;
  663:     }
  664: 
  665:     if (
  666:       !document.fullscreenEnabled
  667:     ) {
  668:       toast.error(
  669:         "Fullscreen is not available in this browser. Ask your teacher for support.",
  670:       );
  671:       return;
  672:     }
  673: 
  674:     try {
  675:       await examRootRef.current.requestFullscreen();
  676: 
  677:       await startExamIntegritySession(
  678:         {
  679:           assignment,
  680:           studentId:
  681:             user.uid,
  682:         },
  683:       );
  684: 
  685:       setIntegrityStarted(
  686:         true,
  687:       );
  688: 
  689:       setIntegrityPaused(
  690:         false,
  691:       );
  692: 
  693:       setIntegrityWarning(
  694:         "",
  695:       );
  696: 
  697:       finishingRef.current =
  698:         false;
  699: 
  700:       toast.success(
  701:         "Exam Mode started.",
  702:       );
  703:     } catch (error) {
  704:       console.error(
  705:         "Unable to enter Exam Mode:",
  706:         error,
  707:       );
  708: 
  709:       toast.error(
  710:         "Fullscreen Exam Mode could not be started.",
  711:       );
  712:     }
  713:   }
  714: 
  715:   async function resumePausedExam() {
  716:     if (
  717:       !examRootRef.current
  718:     ) {
  719:       return;
  720:     }
  721: 
  722:     try {
  723:       if (
  724:         document.fullscreenElement !==
  725:         examRootRef.current
  726:       ) {
  727:         await examRootRef.current.requestFullscreen();
  728:       }
  729: 
  730:       setIntegrityPaused(
  731:         false,
  732:       );
  733: 
  734:       setIntegrityWarning(
  735:         "",
  736:       );
  737:     } catch {
  738:       toast.error(
  739:         "Return to fullscreen to resume the exam.",
  740:       );
  741:     }
  742:   }
  743: 
  744:   function updateResponse(
  745:     questionId: string,
  746:     response: string,
  747:   ) {
  748:     if (
  749:       !assignment ||
  750:       !user?.uid ||
  751:       locked ||
  752:       integrityPaused
  753:     ) {
  754:       return;
  755:     }
  756: 
  757:     const nextAnswers =
  758:       answers.map((answer) =>
  759:         answer.questionId ===
  760:         questionId
  761:           ? {
  762:               ...answer,
  763:               response,
  764:             }
  765:           : answer,
  766:       );
  767: 
  768:     setAnswers(
  769:       nextAnswers,
  770:     );
  771: 
  772:     if (saveTimer.current) {
  773:       clearTimeout(
  774:         saveTimer.current,
  775:       );
  776:     }
  777: 
  778:     saveTimer.current =
  779:       setTimeout(() => {
  780:         void autosaveExamAnswers(
  781:           assignment.id,
  782:           user.uid,
  783:           nextAnswers,
  784:         ).catch((error) => {
  785:           console.error(error);
  786: 
  787:           toast.error(
  788:             "Autosave failed.",
  789:           );
  790:         });
  791:       }, 900);
  792:   }
  793: 
  794:   function focusQuestion(
  795:     questionNumber: number,
  796:   ) {
  797:     setActiveQuestionNumber(
  798:       questionNumber,
  799:     );
  800: 
  801:     if (
  802:       assignment &&
  803:       user?.uid
  804:     ) {
  805:       void updateExamCurrentQuestion(
  806:         {
  807:           assignmentId:
  808:             assignment.id,
  809: 
  810:           studentId: user.uid,
  811: 
  812:           questionNumber,
  813:         },
  814:       ).catch(
  815:         (error) =>
  816:           console.error(
  817:             "Unable to store current question:",
  818:             error,
  819:           ),
  820:       );
  821:     }
  822:   }
  823: 
  824:   async function saveNow() {
  825:     if (
  826:       !assignment ||
  827:       !user?.uid ||
  828:       locked
  829:     ) {
  830:       return;
  831:     }
  832: 
  833:     setSaving(true);
  834: 
  835:     try {
  836:       await autosaveExamAnswers(
  837:         assignment.id,
  838:         user.uid,
  839:         answers,
  840:       );
  841: 
  842:       toast.success(
  843:         "Answers saved.",
  844:       );
  845:     } catch (error) {
  846:       console.error(error);
  847: 
  848:       toast.error(
  849:         "Answers could not be saved.",
  850:       );
  851:     } finally {
  852:       setSaving(false);
  853:     }
  854:   }
  855: 
  856:   async function submit() {
  857:     if (
  858:       !assignment ||
  859:       !user?.uid ||
  860:       locked
  861:     ) {
  862:       return;
  863:     }
  864: 
  865:     if (
  866:       !window.confirm(
  867:         "Submit this assessment? You will not be able to edit it afterwards.",
  868:       )
  869:     ) {
  870:       return;
  871:     }
  872: 
  873:     finishingRef.current =
  874:       true;
  875: 
  876:     setSaving(true);
  877: 
  878:     try {

FILE: app\assignments\exam\page.tsx
==============================================================================================================
    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import Link from "next/link";
    4: import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
    5: 
    6: import Card from "@/components/ui/Card";
    7: import Skeleton from "@/components/ui/Skeleton";
    8: import { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";
    9: import { getStudentExamAssignments } from "@/services/examAssignmentService";
   10: import { getExamSubmission } from "@/services/examSubmissionService";
   11: import type { ExamAssignment, ExamSubmission } from "@/types/examAssignment";
   12: 
   13: type Row = {
   14:   assignment: ExamAssignment;
   15:   submission: ExamSubmission | null;
   16: };
   17: 
   18: export default function StudentExamAssignmentsPage() {
   19:   const { user } = useAuth();
   20: 
   21:   const [rows, setRows] = useState<Row[]>([]);
   22: 
   23:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   24: 
   25:   useEffect(() => {
   26:     async function load() {
   27:       if (!user?.uid) {
   28:         return;
   29:       }
   30: 
   31:       const assignments = await getStudentExamAssignments(user.uid);
   32: 
   33:       const loadedRows = await Promise.all(
   34:         assignments.map(async (assignment) => ({
   35:           assignment,
   36:           submission: await getExamSubmission(assignment.id, user.uid),
   37:         })),
   38:       );
   39: 
   40:       setRows(loadedRows);
   41:       setLoading(false);
   42:     }
   43: 
   44:     void load();
   45:   }, [user?.uid]);
   46: 
   47:   if (loading) {
   48:     return <Skeleton className="h-96" />;
   49:   }
   50: 
   51:   return (
   52:     <div className="space-y-8">
   53:       <Card className="border-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-800 to-violet-800 text-white">
   54:         <h1 className="text-4xl font-black">Written Assessments</h1>
   55: 
   56:         <p className="mt-3 text-indigo-100">
   57:           Complete assigned exam-style papers and review marked feedback.
   58:         </p>
   59:       </Card>
   60: 
   61:       {rows.length === 0 ? (
   62:         <Card>No written assessments have been assigned.</Card>
   63:       ) : (
   64:         <div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2">
   65:           {rows.map(({ assignment, submission }) => (
   66:             <Card key={assignment.id}>
   67:               <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-indigo-600">
   68:                 {assignment.className}
   69:               </p>
   70: 
   71:               <h2 className="mt-2 text-xl font-black">{assignment.title}</h2>
   72: 
   73:               <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-slate-600">
   74:                 {assignment.questionCount} questions Â· {assignment.totalMarks}{" "}
   75:                 marks
   76:               </p>
   77: 
   78:               <p className="mt-3 font-bold">
   79:                 Status: {submission?.status || "not_started"}
   80:               </p>
   81: 
   82:               {submission?.status === "marked" ? (
   83:                 <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 p-4">
   84:                   <p className="text-2xl font-black text-emerald-900">
   85:                     {submission.totalAwardedMarks}/
   86:                     {submission.totalAvailableMarks} ({submission.percentage}
   87:                     %)
   88:                   </p>
   89: 
   90:                   <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-emerald-800">
   91:                     {submission.overallFeedback}
   92:                   </p>
   93:                 </div>
   94:               ) : null}
   95: 
   96:               <Link
   97:                 href={`/assignments/exam/${assignment.id}`}
   98:                 className="mt-6 inline-flex rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 px-5 py-3 font-bold text-white"
   99:               >
  100:                 {submission?.status === "marked"
  101:                   ? "Review Feedback"
  102:                   : submission?.status === "submitted"
  103:                     ? "View Submission"
  104:                     : "Open Assessment"}
  105:               </Link>
  106:             </Card>
  107:           ))}
  108:         </div>
  109:       )}
  110:     </div>
  111:   );
  112: }

   29: function formatDate(value: Date | null): string {

   33:     day: "numeric",
   34:     month: "short",
   35:     year: "numeric",
   36:   }).format(value);

   38: 
   39: function getExamStatus(
   40:   assignment: UnifiedAssignment,
   41: ): "not_started" | "in_progress" | "submitted" | "marked" {
   42:   if (assignment.status === "marked") {
   43:     return "marked";
   44:   }
   45: 
   46:   if (
   47:     assignment.status === "submitted" ||
   48:     assignment.status === "marking"
   49:   ) {
   50:     return "submitted";
   51:   }
   52: 
   53:   if (assignment.status === "in_progress") {
   54:     return "in_progress";
   55:   }
   56: 
   57:   return "not_started";
   58: }

   60: function statusLabel(
   61:   assignment: UnifiedAssignment,

   65:       return "Marked";
   66:     case "submitted":
   67:       return assignment.status === "marking"
   68:         ? "Marking"
   69:         : "Submitted";
   70:     case "in_progress":
   71:       return "In progress";
   72:     default:
   73:       return "Not started";
   74:   }
   75: }

  112:         {assignment.status === "marking"
  113:           ? "Marking"
  114:           : "Submitted"}

   40: };
   41: 
   42: function normaliseErrorCode(error: unknown): string {
   43:   if (
   44:     typeof error === "object" &&
   45:     error !== null &&
   46:     "code" in error &&
   47:     typeof (error as FirestoreLikeError).code === "string"
   48:   ) {
   49:     return ((error as FirestoreLikeError).code as string).toLowerCase();
   50:   }
   51: 
   52:   return "";
   53: }
   54: 
   55: function normaliseErrorMessage(error: unknown): string {
   56:   if (error instanceof Error) {
   57:     return error.message.trim();
   58:   }
   59: 
   60:   if (
   61:     typeof error === "object" &&
   62:     error !== null &&
   63:     "message" in error &&
   64:     typeof (error as FirestoreLikeError).message === "string"
   65:   ) {
   66:     return ((error as FirestoreLikeError).message as string).trim();
   67:   }
   68: 
   69:   return "";
   70: }
   71: 
   72: function isExpectedUnavailableError(error: unknown): boolean {
   73:   const code = normaliseErrorCode(error);
   74:   const message = normaliseErrorMessage(error).toLowerCase();
   75: 
   76:   return (
   77:     code === "permission-denied" ||
   78:     code === "firestore/permission-denied" ||
   79:     code === "not-found" ||
   80:     code === "firestore/not-found" ||
   81:     message.includes("missing or insufficient permissions") ||
   82:     message.includes("do not have permission") ||
   83:     message.includes("not found")
   84:   );
   85: }
   86: 
   87: function getMarkbookErrorMessage(error: unknown): string {
   88:   if (isExpectedUnavailableError(error)) {
   89:     return "This assignment could not be found or you do not have access to it.";
   90:   }
   91: 
   92:   return "The exam markbook could not be loaded. Please try again.";
   93: }
   94: 
   95: function reportUnexpectedLoadError(
   96:   context: string,
   97:   error: unknown,
   98: ): void {
   99:   if (
  100:     process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production" &&
  101:     !isExpectedUnavailableError(error)
  102:   ) {
  103:     console.warn(context, error);
  104:   }
  105: }
  106: 
  107: async function getStudentIdentity(studentId: string): Promise<StudentIdentity> {
  108:   try {
  109:     const snapshot = await getDoc(doc(db, "users", studentId));
  110: 
  111:     if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  112:       return {
  113:         uid: studentId,
  114:         name: "Unnamed Student",
  115:         email: "",
  116:       };
  117:     }
  118: 
  119:     const profile = snapshot.data() as StudentProfileDocument;
  120: 
  121:     const name =
  122:       normaliseString(profile.name) ||
  123:       normaliseString(profile.displayName) ||
  124:       normaliseString(profile.fullName) ||
  125:       "Unnamed Student";
  126: 
  127:     const email = normaliseString(profile.email).toLowerCase();
  128: 
  129:     return {
  130:       uid: studentId,
  131:       name,
  132:       email,
  133:     };
  134:   } catch (error) {
  135:     reportUnexpectedLoadError(
  136:       `Unable to load student profile ${studentId}:`,
  137:       error,
  138:     );
  139: 
  140:     return {
  141:       uid: studentId,
  142:       name: "Unnamed Student",
  143:       email: "",
  144:     };
  145:   }
  146: }
  147: 
  148: function formatStatus(
  149:   status: ExamSubmission["status"] | "not_started",
  150: ): string {
  151:   switch (status) {
  152:     case "in_progress":
  153:       return "In progress";
  154: 
  155:     case "submitted":
  156:       return "Submitted";
  157: 
  158:     case "marking":
  159:       return "Being marked";
  160: 
  161:     case "marked":
  162:       return "Marked";
  163: 
  164:     default:
  165:       return "Not started";
  166:   }
  167: }
  168: 
  169: function getStatusClass(
  170:   status: ExamSubmission["status"] | "not_started",
  171: ): string {
  172:   switch (status) {
  173:     case "marked":
  174:       return "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700";
  175: 
  176:     case "submitted":
  177:       return "bg-cyan-100 text-cyan-700";
  178: 
  179:     case "marking":
  180:       return "bg-amber-100 text-amber-700";
  181: 
  182:     case "in_progress":
  183:       return "bg-blue-100 text-blue-700";
  184: 
  185:     default:
  186:       return "bg-slate-100 text-slate-600";
  187:   }
  188: }
  189: 
  190: export default function ExamAssignmentMarkbookPage() {
  191:   const params = useParams<{
  192:     assignmentId: string;
  193:   }>();
  194: 
  195:   const {
  196:     user,
  197:     loading: authLoading,
  198:     profileReady,
  199:   } = useAuth();
  200: 
  201:   const [assignment, setAssignment] = useState<ExamAssignment | null>(null);
  202: 
  203:   const [submissions, setSubmissions] = useState<ExamSubmission[]>([]);
  204: 
  205:   const [students, setStudents] = useState<StudentIdentity[]>([]);
  206: 
  207:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  208: 
  209:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
  210: 
  211:   useEffect(() => {
  212:     let cancelled = false;
  213: 
  214:     async function loadMarkbook() {
  215:       /*
  216:        * Do not query protected Firestore data until Firebase Auth has finished
  217:        * restoring the signed-in teacher. This also prevents hard-refresh
  218:        * permission errors.
  219:        */
  220:       if (authLoading || !profileReady) {
  221:         return;
  222:       }
  223: 
  224:       if (!user?.uid) {
  225:         if (!cancelled) {
  226:           setAssignment(null);
  227:           setSubmissions([]);
  228:           setStudents([]);
  229:           setLoading(false);
  230:         }
  231: 
  232:         return;
  233:       }
  234: 
  235:       try {
  236:         setLoading(true);
  237:         setError("");
  238: 
  239:         const loadedAssignment = await getExamAssignmentById(
  240:           params.assignmentId,
  241:         );
  242: 
  243:         if (cancelled) {
  244:           return;
  245:         }
  246: 
  247:         if (!loadedAssignment) {
  248:           setAssignment(null);
  249:           return;
  250:         }
  251: 
  252:         /*
  253:          * Only the teacher who owns the exam should open its markbook or edit
  254:          * its integrity policy.
  255:          */
  256:         if (loadedAssignment.teacherId !== user.uid) {
  257:           throw new Error(
  258:             "You do not have permission to view this exam markbook.",
  259:           );
  260:         }
  261: 
  262:         const [loadedSubmissions, loadedStudents] = await Promise.all([
  263:           getAssignmentSubmissions(
  264:             loadedAssignment.id,
  265:             user.uid,
  266:           ),
  267: 
  268:           Promise.all(
  269:             loadedAssignment.studentIds.map((studentId: string) =>
  270:               getStudentIdentity(studentId),
  271:             ),
  272:           ),
  273:         ]);
  274: 
  275:         if (cancelled) {
  276:           return;
  277:         }
  278: 
  279:         setAssignment(loadedAssignment);
  280:         setSubmissions(loadedSubmissions);
  281: 
  282:         setStudents(
  283:           loadedStudents.sort(
  284:             (firstStudent: StudentIdentity, secondStudent: StudentIdentity) =>
  285:               firstStudent.name.localeCompare(secondStudent.name, "en-GB", {
  286:                 sensitivity: "base",
  287:               }),
  288:           ),
  289:         );
  290:       } catch (caughtError) {
  291:         reportUnexpectedLoadError(
  292:           "Unable to load exam markbook:",
  293:           caughtError,
  294:         );
  295: 
  296:         if (!cancelled) {
  297:           setAssignment(null);
  298:           setSubmissions([]);
  299:           setStudents([]);
  300:           setError(getMarkbookErrorMessage(caughtError));
  301:         }
  302:       } finally {
  303:         if (!cancelled) {
  304:           setLoading(false);
  305:         }
  306:       }
  307:     }
  308: 
  309:     void loadMarkbook();
  310: 
  311:     return () => {
  312:       cancelled = true;
  313:     };
  314:   }, [
  315:     authLoading,
  316:     params.assignmentId,
  317:     profileReady,
  318:     user?.uid,
  319:   ]);
  320: 
  321:   const submissionsByStudent = useMemo(
  322:     () =>
  323:       new Map<string, ExamSubmission>(
  324:         submissions.map((submission: ExamSubmission) => [
  325:           submission.studentId,
  326:           submission,
  327:         ]),
  328:       ),
  329:     [submissions],
  330:   );
  331: 
  332:   const summary = useMemo(() => {
  333:     const submitted = submissions.filter((submission: ExamSubmission) =>
  334:       ["submitted", "marking", "marked"].includes(submission.status),
  335:     ).length;
  336: 
  337:     const markedSubmissions = submissions.filter(
  338:       (submission: ExamSubmission) => submission.status === "marked",
  339:     );
  340: 
  341:     const average =
  342:       markedSubmissions.length > 0
  343:         ? Math.round(
  344:             markedSubmissions.reduce(
  345:               (total: number, submission: ExamSubmission) =>
  346:                 total + submission.percentage,
  347:               0,
  348:             ) / markedSubmissions.length,
  349:           )
  350:         : 0;
  351: 
  352:     return {
  353:       total: assignment?.studentIds.length ?? 0,
  354: 
  355:       submitted,
  356: 
  357:       marked: markedSubmissions.length,
  358: 
  359:       average,
  360:     };
  361:   }, [assignment?.studentIds.length, submissions]);
  362: 
  363:   if (authLoading || !profileReady || loading) {
  364:     return <Skeleton className="h-96" />;
  365:   }
  366: 
  367:   if (!assignment) {
  368:     return (
  369:       <Card>
  370:         <h1 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
  371:           Assignment unavailable
  372:         </h1>
  373: 
  374:         <p className="mt-3 text-slate-600">
  375:           {error ||
  376:             "This assignment could not be found or you do not have access to it."}
  377:         </p>
  378: 
  379:         <Link
  380:           href="/teacher/exam-assignments"
  381:           className="mt-5 inline-flex rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 px-5 py-3 font-bold text-white"
  382:         >
  383:           Back to Exam Assignments
  384:         </Link>
  385:       </Card>
  386:     );
  387:   }
  388: 
  389:   return (
  390:     <div className="space-y-8">
  391:       <Card className="border-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-950 to-indigo-950 text-white">
  392:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between">
  393:           <div>
  394:             <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-indigo-200">
  395:               {assignment.className}
  396:             </p>
  397: 
  398:             <h1 className="mt-2 text-4xl font-black">{assignment.title}</h1>
  399: 
  400:             <p className="mt-3 text-indigo-100">
  401:               {assignment.questionCount} questions Â· {assignment.totalMarks}{" "}
  402:               marks
  403:             </p>
  404:           </div>
  405: 
  406:           <Link
  407:             href="/teacher/exam-assignments"
  408:             className="rounded-xl border border-white/20 px-5 py-3 text-center font-bold text-white"
  409:           >
  410:             All Exam Assignments
  411:           </Link>
  412:         </div>
  413:       </Card>
  414: 
  415:       <section className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
  416:         <SummaryCard label="Students" value={summary.total} />
  417: 
  418:         <SummaryCard label="Submitted" value={summary.submitted} />
  419: 
  420:         <SummaryCard label="Marked" value={summary.marked} />
  421: 
  422:         <SummaryCard label="Class average" value={`${summary.average}%`} />
  423:       </section>
  424: 
  425:       {user?.uid && (
  426:         <ExamIntegrityPolicyCard
  427:           assignment={assignment}
  428:           teacherId={user.uid}
  429:           onSaved={(policy) =>

  431:               current
  432:                 ? {
  433:                     ...current,
  434:                     integrityPolicy: policy,
  435:                   }
  436:                 : current,
  437:             )
  438:           }
  439:         />
  440:       )}
  441: 
  442:       <ExamAssignmentIntelligencePanel
  443:         assignment={assignment}
  444:         submissions={submissions}
  445:       />
  446: 
  447:       <Card>
  448:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between">
  449:           <div>
  450:             <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-indigo-600">
  451:               Student results
  452:             </p>
  453: 
  454:             <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
  455:               Class Markbook
  456:             </h2>
  457: 
  458:             <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
  459:               Track progress, open submitted papers and review marked results.
  460:             </p>
  461:           </div>

  464:             {summary.submitted}/{summary.total} submitted
  465:           </p>
  466:         </div>

  539:                         ? `${submission.totalAwardedMarks}/${assignment.totalMarks}`
  540:                         : "â€”"}

  543:                     <td className="p-4 font-semibold text-slate-700">
  544:                       {submission?.status === "marked"
  545:                         ? `${submission.percentage}%`
  546:                         : "â€”"}
  547:                     </td>
  548:

  569:                     </td>

  572:               })}
  573:             </tbody>
  574:           </table>
  575:         </div>

  577:     </div>
  578:   );

  580: 
  581: function SummaryCard({
  582:   label,
  583:   value,
  584: }: {
  585:   label: string;
  586:   value: number | string;
  587: }) {
  588:   return (

  591: 
  592:       <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-black text-slate-950">{value}</p>
  593:     </Card>
  594:   );
  595: }

   28: function toStringList(value: unknown): string[] {
   29:   if (typeof value === "string") {
   30:     return value.trim() ? [value.trim()] : [];
   31:   }
   32: 
   33:   if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
   34:     return [];
   35:   }
   36: 
   37:   return value
   38:     .map((item) => {
   39:       if (typeof item === "string") {
   40:         return item.trim();
   41:       }
   42: 
   43:       if (item && typeof item === "object") {
   44:         const candidate = item as Record<string, unknown>;
   45: 
   46:         const text = candidate.description ?? candidate.text ?? candidate.point;
   47: 
   48:         return typeof text === "string" ? text.trim() : "";
   49:       }
   50: 
   51:       return "";
   52:     })
   53:     .filter(Boolean);
   54: }
   55: 
   56: function getQuestionExtras(question: unknown) {
   57:   const candidate = question as Record<string, unknown>;
   58: 
   59:   return {
   60:     examinerGuidance: toStringList(candidate.examinerGuidance),
   61:     misconceptions: toStringList(candidate.misconceptions),
   62:   };
   63: }
   64: 
   65: function confidenceClass(value: "high" | "medium" | "low") {
   66:   if (value === "high") {
   67:     return "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700";
   68:   }
   69: 
   70:   if (value === "medium") {
   71:     return "bg-amber-100 text-amber-700";
   72:   }
   73: 
   74:   return "bg-red-100 text-red-700";
   75: }

  138:           suggestion,
  139:         ]),
  140:       ),
  141:     [aiResult],
  142:   );
  143: 
  144:   function updateAnswer(
  145:     questionId: string,
  146:     updates: Partial<StudentExamAnswer>,
  147:   ) {
  148:     setAnswers((current) =>
  149:       current.map((answer) =>
  150:         answer.questionId === questionId
  151:           ? {
  152:               ...answer,
  153:               ...updates,
  154:             }
  155:           : answer,
  156:       ),
  157:     );
  158:   }
  159: 
  160:   async function runAiMarking() {
  161:     if (!assignment || !submission) {
  162:       return;
  163:     }
  164: 
  165:     setAiMarking(true);
  166: 
  167:     try {
  168:       const response = await fetch("/api/ai/mark-exam-submission", {
  169:         method: "POST",
  170:         headers: {
  171:           "Content-Type": "application/json",
  172:         },
  173:         body: JSON.stringify({
  174:           assignmentTitle: assignment.title,
  175:           topic:
  176:             assignment.questionSetSnapshot.topic || assignment.questionSetTitle,
  177:           questions: assignment.questionSetSnapshot.questions,
  178:           answers: answers.map((answer) => ({
  179:             questionId: answer.questionId,
  180:             response: answer.response,
  181:           })),
  182:         }),
  183:       });
  184: 
  185:       const result = (await response.json()) as
  186:         | AIExamMarkingResult
  187:         | {
  188:             error?: string;
  189:           };
  190: 
  191:       if (!response.ok) {
  192:         throw new Error(
  193:           "error" in result && result.error
  194:             ? result.error
  195:             : "AI marking failed.",
  196:         );
  197:       }
  198: 
  199:       setAiResult(result as AIExamMarkingResult);
  200: 
  201:       toast.success("AI marking suggestions are ready for teacher review.");
  202:     } catch (error) {
  203:       console.error("AI marking error:", error);
  204: 
  205:       toast.error(
  206:         error instanceof Error ? error.message : "AI marking failed.",
  207:       );
  208:     } finally {
  209:       setAiMarking(false);
  210:     }
  211:   }
  212: 
  213:   function applySuggestion(suggestion: AIQuestionMarkingSuggestion) {
  214:     updateAnswer(suggestion.questionId, {
  215:       awardedMarks: suggestion.suggestedMarks,
  216:       teacherFeedback: suggestion.feedback,
  217:     });
  218:   }
  219: 
  220:   function applyAllSuggestions() {
  221:     if (!aiResult) {
  222:       return;
  223:     }
  224: 
  225:     setAnswers((current) =>
  226:       current.map((answer) => {
  227:         const suggestion = aiResult.suggestions.find(
  228:           (item) => item.questionId === answer.questionId,
  229:         );
  230: 
  231:         if (!suggestion) {
  232:           return answer;
  233:         }
  234: 
  235:         return {
  236:           ...answer,
  237:           awardedMarks: suggestion.suggestedMarks,
  238:           teacherFeedback: suggestion.feedback,
  239:         };
  240:       }),
  241:     );
  242: 
  243:     if (aiResult.overallFeedback) {
  244:       setOverallFeedback(aiResult.overallFeedback);
  245:     }
  246: 
  247:     toast.success(
  248:       "AI suggestions applied. Review and edit them before finalising.",
  249:     );
  250:   }
  251: 
  252:   async function saveDraft() {
  253:     if (!assignment) {
  254:       return;
  255:     }
  256: 
  257:     setSaving(true);
  258: 
  259:     try {
  260:       await saveDraftMarking({
  261:         assignmentId: assignment.id,
  262:         studentId: params.studentId,
  263:         answers,
  264:         overallFeedback,
  265:       });
  266: 
  267:       toast.success("Draft marking saved.");
  268:     } catch (error) {
  269:       console.error(error);
  270: 
  271:       toast.error("Draft marking could not be saved.");
  272:     } finally {
  273:       setSaving(false);
  274:     }
  275:   }
  276: 
  277:   async function finalise() {
  278:     if (!assignment) {
  279:       return;
  280:     }
  281: 
  282:     const incomplete = answers.some((answer) => answer.awardedMarks === null);
  283: 
  284:     if (incomplete) {
  285:       toast.error("Award marks for every question before finalising.");
  286:       return;
  287:     }
  288: 
  289:     if (
  290:       !window.confirm(
  291:         "Finalise these marks and release the result to the student?",
  292:       )
  293:     ) {
  294:       return;
  295:     }
  296: 
  297:     setSaving(true);
  298: 
  299:     try {
  300:       await finaliseExamMarking({
  301:         assignmentId: assignment.id,
  302:         studentId: params.studentId,
  303:         answers,
  304:         overallFeedback,
  305:         totalAvailableMarks: assignment.totalMarks,
  306:       });
  307: 
  308:       toast.success("Marking finalised.");
  309: 
  310:       router.push(`/teacher/exam-assignments/${assignment.id}`);
  311:     } catch (error) {
  312:       console.error(error);
  313: 
  314:       toast.error(
  315:         error instanceof Error
  316:           ? error.message
  317:           : "Marking could not be finalised.",
  318:       );
  319:     } finally {
  320:       setSaving(false);
  321:     }
  322:   }
  323: 
  324:   if (loading) {
  325:     return <Skeleton className="h-96" />;
  326:   }
  327: 
  328:   if (!assignment || !submission) {
  329:     return (
  330:       <Card>
  331:         <h1 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
  332:           Submission not found
  333:         </h1>
  334: 
  335:         <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
  336:           <Link
  337:             href={`/teacher/exam-assignments/${params.assignmentId}`}
  338:             className="rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 px-5 py-3 font-bold text-white"
  339:           >
  340:             Back to Markbook
  341:           </Link>
  342: 
  343:           <Link
  344:             href="/teacher"
  345:             className="rounded-xl border px-5 py-3 font-bold"
  346:           >
  347:             Teacher Dashboard
  348:           </Link>
  349:         </div>
  350:       </Card>
  351:     );
  352:   }
  353: 
  354:   return (
  355:     <div className="space-y-8">
  356:       <Card className="border-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-950 to-indigo-950 text-white">
  357:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between">
  358:           <div>
  359:             <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-indigo-200">
  360:               Marking workspace
  361:             </p>
  362: 
  363:             <h1 className="mt-2 text-4xl font-black">
  364:               {submission.studentName}
  365:             </h1>
  366: 
  367:             <p className="mt-3 text-indigo-100">{assignment.title}</p>
  368: 
  369:             <p className="mt-3 text-xl font-black">
  370:               {totalAwarded}/{assignment.totalMarks}
  371:             </p>
  372:           </div>
  373: 
  374:           <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
  375:             <Link
  376:               href={`/teacher/exam-assignments/${assignment.id}`}
  377:               className="rounded-xl border border-white/20 px-5 py-3 font-bold text-white"
  378:             >
  379:               Ã¢â€ Â Markbook
  380:             </Link>
  381: 
  382:             <Link
  383:               href="/teacher"
  384:               className="rounded-xl border border-white/20 px-5 py-3 font-bold text-white"
  385:             >
  386:               Teacher Dashboard
  387:             </Link>
  388:           </div>
  389:         </div>
  390:       </Card>
  391: 
  392:       <ExamIntegrityReportCard submission={submission} />
  393: 
  394:       <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200">
  395:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between">
  396:           <div>
  397:             <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-indigo-600">
  398:               Intelligence handoff
  399:             </p>
  400: 
  401:             <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
  402:               Connect this exam to the learner record
  403:             </h2>
  404: 
  405:             <p className="mt-2 max-w-3xl text-sm leading-6 text-slate-600">
  406:               Review this written paper alongside the student&apos;s wider analytics before deciding whether to reteach, assign targeted work or create an intervention.
  407:             </p>

  439:           <div className="mt-5 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl bg-amber-50 p-4 text-sm leading-6 text-amber-950">

  456: 
  457:             <p className="mt-2 max-w-3xl text-sm leading-6 text-violet-800">
  458:               AI suggestions are provisional. Review every mark and feedback
  459:               comment before finalising.
  460:             </p>
  461:           </div>
  462:

  466:               onClick={() => {

  468:               }}
  469:               disabled={aiMarking || saving}
  470:               className="rounded-xl bg-violet-700 px-5 py-3 font-bold text-white disabled:bg-slate-300"
  471:             >

  473:             </button>
  474:

  476:               <button

  478:                 onClick={applyAllSuggestions}

  504:

  506:                   {aiResult.strengths.map((item) => (
  507:                     <li key={item}>Ã¢â‚¬Â¢ {item}</li>
  508:                   ))}
  509:                 </ul>
  510:               </div>
  511: 
  512:               <div>
  513:                 <p className="font-black text-amber-800">
  514:                   Suggested priorities
  515:                 </p>
  516: 
  517:                 <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1 text-sm text-slate-700">
  518:                   {aiResult.priorities.map((item) => (
  519:                     <li key={item}>Ã¢â‚¬Â¢ {item}</li>
  520:                   ))}
  521:                 </ul>
  522:               </div>
  523:             </div>
  524:           </div>
  525:         )}
  526:       </Card>
  527: 
  528:       {assignment.questionSetSnapshot.questions.map((question) => {
  529:         const answer = answers.find((item) => item.questionId === question.id);
  530: 
  531:         const suggestion = suggestionsByQuestion.get(question.id);
  532: 
  533:         const extras = getQuestionExtras(question);
  534: 
  535:         return (

  540:               </h2>
  541: 
  542:               <span className="rounded-full bg-indigo-100 px-3 py-1 font-bold text-indigo-800">
  543:                 {question.marks} marks
  544:               </span>
  545:             </div>
  546: 
  547:             {question.context && (

  549:                 {question.context}
  550:               </div>
  551:             )}
  552:

  554:               {question.question}
  555:             </p>

  558:               <h3 className="font-black">Student response</h3>
  559:

  561:                 {answer?.response || "No response submitted."}
  562:               </p>
  563:             </div>
  564: 
  565:             <div className="mt-5 grid gap-5 xl:grid-cols-2">
  566:               <div className="rounded-2xl bg-emerald-50 p-5">
  567:                 <h3 className="font-black text-emerald-950">Mark scheme</h3>
  568: 
  569:                 <ul className="mt-3 space-y-2 text-sm text-emerald-900">
  570:                   {question.markScheme.map((point) => (
  571:                     <li key={point.id}>
  572:                       Ã¢â‚¬Â¢ {point.description} ({point.marks})
  573:                     </li>
  574:                   ))}
  575:                 </ul>
  576:               </div>
  577: 
  578:               <div className="rounded-2xl bg-blue-50 p-5">
  579:                 <h3 className="font-black text-blue-950">Model answer</h3>
  580: 
  581:                 <p className="mt-3 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm leading-6 text-blue-900">
  582:                   {question.modelAnswer}
  583:                 </p>
  584:               </div>
  585:             </div>
  586: 
  587:             {extras.examinerGuidance.length > 0 && (
  588:               <div className="mt-5 rounded-2xl bg-amber-50 p-5">

  590: 
  591:                 <ul className="mt-3 space-y-2 text-sm text-amber-900">
  592:                   {extras.examinerGuidance.map((item) => (
  593:                     <li key={item}>Ã¢â‚¬Â¢ {item}</li>
  594:                   ))}
  595:                 </ul>
  596:               </div>
  597:             )}
  598: 
  599:             {suggestion && (
  600:               <div className="mt-5 rounded-2xl border border-violet-200 bg-violet-50 p-5">

  613:                   </span>

  624:                     emptyMessage="No marking points were confidently matched."

  626:

  628:                     title="Missed points"
  629:                     items={suggestion.missedMarkPoints}
  630:                     emptyMessage="No missed marking points were identified."
  631:                   />
  632:                 </div>
  633:

  635:                   <p className="mt-4 rounded-xl bg-white p-3 text-sm font-bold text-red-700">
  636:                     Teacher review is required before this suggestion is
  637:                     accepted.
  638:                   </p>
  639:                 )}
  640:

  642:                   type="button"
  643:                   onClick={() => applySuggestion(suggestion)}
  644:                   className="mt-4 rounded-xl bg-violet-700 px-4 py-2 font-bold text-white"
  645:                 >
  646:                   Apply this suggestion
  647:                 </button>
  648:               </div>
  649:             )}
  650: 
  651:             <div className="mt-5 grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-[180px_1fr]">
  652:               <label>
  653:                 <span className="text-sm font-bold">Awarded marks</span>
  654:

  656:                   type="number"
  657:                   min={0}
  658:                   max={question.marks}
  659:                   value={answer?.awardedMarks ?? ""}
  660:                   onChange={(event) =>
  661:                     updateAnswer(question.id, {
  662:                       awardedMarks:
  663:                         event.target.value === ""
  664:                           ? null
  665:                           : Math.min(
  666:                               question.marks,
  667:                               Math.max(0, Number(event.target.value)),
  668:                             ),
  669:                     })
  670:                   }
  671:                   className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border px-4 py-3"
  672:                 />
  673:               </label>
  674: 
  675:               <label>
  676:                 <span className="text-sm font-bold">Question feedback</span>
  677: 
  678:                 <textarea
  679:                   rows={5}
  680:                   value={answer?.teacherFeedback || ""}
  681:                   onChange={(event) =>
  682:                     updateAnswer(question.id, {
  683:                       teacherFeedback: event.target.value,
  684:                     })
  685:                   }
  686:                   className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border px-4 py-3"
  687:                 />
  688:               </label>
  689:             </div>
  690:           </Card>
  691:         );
  692:       })}
  693: 
  694:       <Card>

  702:             className="mt-3 w-full rounded-xl border px-4 py-3"
  703:           />
  704:         </label>
  705: 
  706:         <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
  707:           <button
  708:             type="button"
  709:             onClick={() => {
  710:               void saveDraft();
  711:             }}
  712:             disabled={saving}
  713:             className="rounded-xl border px-5 py-3 font-bold"
  714:           >
  715:             Save Draft
  716:           </button>
  717: 
  718:           <button
  719:             type="button"
  720:             onClick={() => {
  721:               void finalise();
  722:             }}
  723:             disabled={saving}
  724:             className="rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 px-5 py-3 font-bold text-white"
  725:           >
  726:             Finalise Marking
  727:           </button>
  728: 
  729:           <Link
  730:             href={`/teacher/exam-assignments/${assignment.id}`}
  731:             className="rounded-xl border px-5 py-3 font-bold"
  732:           >
  733:             Back to Markbook
  734:           </Link>
  735: 
  736:           <Link
  737:             href="/teacher"
  738:             className="rounded-xl border px-5 py-3 font-bold"
  739:           >
  740:             Teacher Dashboard
  741:           </Link>
  742:         </div>
  743:       </Card>
  744:     </div>
  745:   );
  746: }
  747: 
  748: function SuggestionList({
  749:   title,
  750:   items,
  751:   emptyMessage,
  752: }: {
  753:   title: string;
  754:   items: string[];
  755:   emptyMessage: string;
  756: }) {
  757:   return (
  758:     <div className="rounded-xl bg-white p-4">
  759:       <p className="font-black text-slate-900">{title}</p>
  760: 
  761:       {items.length > 0 ? (
  762:         <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1 text-sm text-slate-700">
  763:           {items.map((item) => (
  764:             <li key={item}>Ã¢â‚¬Â¢ {item}</li>
  765:           ))}
  766:         </ul>
  767:       ) : (
  768:         <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">{emptyMessage}</p>
  769:       )}
  770:     </div>
  771:   );
  772: }

   12: function formatDate(value: Date | null) {
   13:   return value
   14:     ? value.toLocaleDateString("en-GB", {
   15:         day: "numeric",
   16:         month: "short",
   17:         year: "numeric",
   18:       })
   19:     : "No due date";
   20: }
   21: 
   22: export default function TeacherExamAssignmentsPage() {
   23:   const { user } = useAuth();
   24: 
   25:   const [assignments, setAssignments] = useState<ExamAssignment[]>([]);
   26: 
   27:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   28: 
   29:   const [error, setError] = useState("");

   32:     async function load() {
   33:       if (!user?.uid) {
   34:         return;
   35:       }

   16: export default function QuestionCard({ question }: QuestionCardProps) {
   17:   const [selected, setSelected] = useState("");
   18:   const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);
   19: 
   20:   const isCorrect = selected === question.answer;
   21: 
   22:   return (
   23:     <Card>
   24:       <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">{question.question}</h2>
   25: 
   26:       <div className="mt-5 space-y-3">
   27:         {question.options.map((option) => (
   28:           <button
   29:             key={option}
   30:             onClick={() => setSelected(option)}
   31:             className={`w-full rounded-xl border p-3 text-left transition ${
   32:               selected === option
   33:                 ? "border-blue-600 bg-blue-50"
   34:                 : "border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50"
   35:             }`}
   36:           >
   37:             {option}
   38:           </button>
   39:         ))}
   40:       </div>
   41: 
   42:       <div className="mt-6">
   43:         <Button onClick={() => setSubmitted(true)}>Check Answer</Button>
   44:       </div>
   45: 
   46:       {submitted && (
   47:         <div
   48:           className={`mt-5 rounded-xl p-4 ${
   49:             isCorrect ? "bg-green-50 text-green-700" : "bg-red-50 text-red-700"
   50:           }`}
   51:         >
   52:           <p className="font-semibold">
   53:             {isCorrect ? "âœ… Correct" : "âŒ Not quite"}
   54:           </p>
   55:           <p className="mt-2 text-sm">{question.explanation}</p>
   56:         </div>
   57:       )}
   58:     </Card>
   59:   );
   60: }

   28:   id: string;

   35: const DEFAULT_QUIZ_DURATION_SECONDS = 8 * 60;
   36: const FULLSCREEN_EXIT_COUNTDOWN_SECONDS = 5;
   37: 
   38: function parseQuizDurationSeconds(value: string): number {
   39:   const normalised = value.trim().toLowerCase();
   40: 
   41:   if (!normalised) {
   42:     return DEFAULT_QUIZ_DURATION_SECONDS;
   43:   }
   44: 
   45:   const hoursMatch = normalised.match(
   46:     /(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:hours?|hrs?|hr|h)\b/,
   47:   );
   48: 
   49:   const minutesMatch = normalised.match(
   50:     /(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:minutes?|mins?|min|m)\b/,
   51:   );
   52: 
   53:   const secondsMatch = normalised.match(
   54:     /(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:seconds?|secs?|sec|s)\b/,
   55:   );
   56: 
   57:   const hours = hoursMatch ? Number(hoursMatch[1]) : 0;
   58:   const minutes = minutesMatch ? Number(minutesMatch[1]) : 0;
   59:   const seconds = secondsMatch ? Number(secondsMatch[1]) : 0;
   60: 
   61:   const totalSeconds = Math.round(
   62:     hours * 60 * 60 +
   63:       minutes * 60 +
   64:       seconds,
   65:   );
   66: 
   67:   return totalSeconds > 0
   68:     ? totalSeconds
   69:     : DEFAULT_QUIZ_DURATION_SECONDS;
   70: }
   71: 
   72: function getGrade(scorePercent: number) {
   73:   if (scorePercent >= 90) return "Grade 9";
   74:   if (scorePercent >= 80) return "Grade 8";
   75:   if (scorePercent >= 70) return "Grade 7";
   76:   if (scorePercent >= 60) return "Grade 6";
   77:   if (scorePercent >= 50) return "Grade 5";
   78:   if (scorePercent >= 40) return "Grade 4";
   79:   if (scorePercent >= 30) return "Grade 3";
   80:   if (scorePercent >= 20) return "Grade 2";
   81:   return "Grade 1";
   82: }
   83: 
   84: function getMessage(scorePercent: number) {
   85:   if (scorePercent >= 90) return "Outstanding performance!";
   86:   if (scorePercent >= 75) return "Excellent work!";
   87:   if (scorePercent >= 60) return "Good progress!";
   88:   if (scorePercent >= 40) return "You are getting there.";
   89:   return "Keep practising. You can improve this.";
   90: }

   92: function createIncident(
   93:   type: QuizIntegrityIncident["type"],
   94:   questionNumber: number | null,
   95:   detail: string,
   96: ): QuizIntegrityIncident {
   97:   return {
   98:     id: `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9)}`,
   99:     type,
  100:     occurredAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  101:     questionNumber,
  102:     detail,
  103:   };
  104: }

  115:   );

  119:   const assignmentSaveInProgress = useRef(false);

  121: 
  122:   /*
  123:    * Synchronous integrity termination flag.
  124:    *
  125:    * React state updates are asynchronous. This ref makes the zero-XP rule

  127:    */
  128:   const integrityTerminatedRef = useRef(false);
  129: 
  130:   const fullscreenExitActiveRef = useRef(false);

  132:   const integrityIncidentsRef = useRef<QuizIntegrityIncident[]>([]);

  137:   const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = useState(() => quizDurationSeconds);
  138:   const [resultSaved, setResultSaved] = useState(false);
  139:   const [assignmentResultSaved, setAssignmentResultSaved] = useState(false);

  142:     useState<LinkedAssignment | null>(null);

  144:   const [assignmentError, setAssignmentError] = useState("");

  148:     useState<string | null>(null);

  150:     useState<number | null>(null);

  154:     useState("");

  157:     linkedAssignment?.deliveryMode ?? "practice";

  159:     Boolean(assignmentId) && deliveryMode === "assessment";
  160: 
  161:   const currentQuestion = quiz.questions[currentIndex];
  162:   const selectedAnswer = currentQuestion
  163:     ? answers[currentQuestion.id] || ""
  164:     : "";
  165: 
  166:   const currentQuestionNumber = currentIndex + 1;
  167: 
  168:   const progress = Math.round(
  169:     ((currentIndex + 1) / Math.max(1, quiz.questions.length)) * 100,
  170:   );
  171: 
  172:   const totalXP = useMemo(() => {
  173:     return quiz.questions.reduce(
  174:       (total, question) => total + question.xpReward,
  175:       0,
  176:     );
  177:   }, [quiz.questions]);
  178: 
  179:   const correctCount = quiz.questions.filter((question) => {
  180:     const userAnswer = answers[question.id]?.trim().toLowerCase();
  181:     const correctAnswer = question.correctAnswer.trim().toLowerCase();
  182:     return userAnswer === correctAnswer;
  183:   }).length;
  184: 
  185:   const scorePercent = Math.round(
  186:     (correctCount / Math.max(1, quiz.questions.length)) * 100,
  187:   );
  188: 
  189:   const earnedXP = quiz.questions.reduce((total, question) => {
  190:     const userAnswer = answers[question.id]?.trim().toLowerCase();
  191:     const correctAnswer = question.correctAnswer.trim().toLowerCase();
  192: 
  193:     return userAnswer === correctAnswer
  194:       ? total + question.xpReward
  195:       : total;
  196:   }, 0);
  197: 
  198:   /*
  199:    * Integrity-terminated assessment attempts retain their raw score for
  200:    * teacher evidence, but do not award XP.
  201:    */
  202:   const awardedXP = integrityTerminated ? 0 : earnedXP;
  203: 
  204:   const strengths = quiz.questions
  205:     .filter((question) => {
  206:       const userAnswer = answers[question.id]?.trim().toLowerCase();
  207:       const correctAnswer = question.correctAnswer.trim().toLowerCase();
  208:       return userAnswer === correctAnswer;
  209:     })
  210:     .slice(0, 3);
  211:

  215:       const correctAnswer = question.correctAnswer.trim().toLowerCase();
  216:       return userAnswer !== correctAnswer;
  217:     })
  218:     .slice(0, 3);
  219: 
  220:   useEffect(() => {
  221:     let cancelled = false;
  222: 
  223:     async function loadLinkedAssignment() {
  224:       if (!assignmentId) {
  225:         setLinkedAssignment(null);
  226:         setAssignmentLoading(false);
  227:         return;
  228:       }
  229: 
  230:       if (!user?.uid) {
  231:         setLinkedAssignment(null);
  232:         setAssignmentError(
  233:           "Sign in with the student account that received this assignment.",
  234:         );
  235:         setAssignmentLoading(false);
  236:         return;
  237:       }
  238: 
  239:       try {
  240:         setAssignmentLoading(true);
  241:         setAssignmentError("");
  242: 
  243:         const snapshot = await getDoc(
  244:           doc(db, "assignments", assignmentId),
  245:         );
  246: 
  247:         if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  248:           throw new Error(
  249:             "The linked quiz assignment could not be found.",
  250:           );
  251:         }
  252: 
  253:         const data = snapshot.data();
  254: 
  255:         if (data.type !== "quiz") {
  256:           throw new Error(
  257:             "This assignment is not a quiz assignment.",
  258:           );
  259:         }
  260: 
  261:         if (!data.classId || !data.teacherId) {
  262:           throw new Error(
  263:             "The assignment is missing its class or teacher information.",
  264:           );
  265:         }
  266: 
  267:         if (data.status === "cancelled") {
  268:           throw new Error(
  269:             "This quiz assignment has been cancelled.",
  270:           );
  271:         }
  272: 
  273:         const assignedResourceId =
  274:           typeof data.resourceId === "string"
  275:             ? data.resourceId.trim()
  276:             : "";
  277: 
  278:         if (
  279:           !assignedResourceId ||
  280:           ![
  281:             quiz.id,
  282:             quiz.topicId,
  283:           ].includes(assignedResourceId)
  284:         ) {
  285:           throw new Error(
  286:             "This quiz does not match the linked assignment.",
  287:           );
  288:         }
  289: 
  290:         const classSnapshot = await getDoc(
  291:           doc(
  292:             db,
  293:             "classes",
  294:             data.classId,
  295:           ),
  296:         );
  297: 
  298:         if (!classSnapshot.exists()) {
  299:           throw new Error(
  300:             "The class linked to this assignment could not be found.",
  301:           );
  302:         }
  303: 
  304:         const classData =
  305:           classSnapshot.data();
  306: 
  307:         const studentIds =
  308:           Array.isArray(
  309:             classData.studentIds,
  310:           )
  311:             ? classData.studentIds.filter(
  312:                 (
  313:                   value,
  314:                 ): value is string =>
  315:                   typeof value ===
  316:                     "string" &&
  317:                   Boolean(
  318:                     value.trim(),
  319:                   ),
  320:               )
  321:             : [];
  322: 
  323:         if (
  324:           !studentIds.includes(
  325:             user.uid,
  326:           )
  327:         ) {
  328:           throw new Error(
  329:             "You do not have access to this quiz assignment.",
  330:           );
  331:         }
  332: 
  333:         if (!cancelled) {
  334:           setLinkedAssignment({
  335:             id: snapshot.id,
  336:             classId: data.classId,
  337:             teacherId: data.teacherId,
  338:             resourceId:
  339:               assignedResourceId,
  340:             deliveryMode:
  341:               data.deliveryMode ===
  342:               "assessment"
  343:                 ? "assessment"
  344:                 : "practice",
  345:           });
  346:         }
  347:       } catch (error) {
  348:         console.error(
  349:           "Quiz assignment load error:",
  350:           error,
  351:         );
  352: 
  353:         if (!cancelled) {
  354:           setLinkedAssignment(null);
  355:           setAssignmentError(
  356:             error instanceof Error
  357:               ? error.message
  358:               : "The linked quiz assignment could not be loaded.",
  359:           );
  360:         }
  361:       } finally {
  362:         if (!cancelled) {
  363:           setAssignmentLoading(false);
  364:         }
  365:       }
  366:     }
  367: 
  368:     void loadLinkedAssignment();
  369: 
  370:     return () => {

  394:   function appendIntegrityIncident(
