  410:   async function leaveFullscreenSafely() {
  411:     if (document.fullscreenElement) {
  412:       await document.exitFullscreen().catch(() => undefined);
  413:     }
  414:   }
  415: 
  416:   async function finishQuiz({
  417:     terminated = false,
  418:     reason = "",
  419:   }: {
  420:     terminated?: boolean;
  421:     reason?: string;
  422:   } = {}) {
  423:     if (finishingRef.current || showResults) {
  424:       return;
  425:     }
  426: 
  427:     finishingRef.current = true;
  428: 
  429:     if (countdownTimerRef.current) {
  430:       clearInterval(countdownTimerRef.current);
  431:       countdownTimerRef.current = null;
  432:     }
  433: 
  434:     fullscreenExitActiveRef.current = false;
  435:     setFullscreenCountdown(null);
  436: 
  437:     if (terminated) {
  438:       /*
  439:        * Set the ref first so save effects see termination synchronously,
  440:        * before React finishes applying the state update.
  441:        */
  442:       integrityTerminatedRef.current = true;
  443:       setIntegrityTerminated(true);
  444:       setIntegrityTerminationReason(reason);
  445: 
  446:       appendIntegrityIncident(
  447:         "auto_submit",
  448:         reason || "The assessment was automatically submitted.",
  449:       );
  450:     }
  451: 
  452:     await leaveFullscreenSafely();
  453:     setShowResults(true);
  454:   }
  455: 
  456:   const finishQuizFromTimer = useEffectEvent(
  457:     (options: { terminated?: boolean; reason?: string }) => {
  458:       void finishQuiz(options);
  459:     },
  460:   );
  461: 
  462:   useEffect(() => {
  463:     if (
  464:       showResults ||
  465:       assignmentLoading ||
  466:       (assessmentMode && !integrityStarted)
  467:     ) {
  468:       return;
  469:     }
  470: 
  471:     const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
  472:       setTimeLeft((previousTime) => {
  473:         if (previousTime <= 1) {
  474:           window.clearInterval(timer);
  475: 
  476:           finishQuizFromTimer({
  477:             terminated: assessmentMode,
  478:             reason: assessmentMode
  479:               ? "The assessment timer expired and the quiz was automatically submitted."
  480:               : "",
  481:           });
  482: 
  483:           return 0;
  484:         }
  485: 
  486:         return previousTime - 1;
  487:       });
  488:     }, 1000);
  489: 
  490:     return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  491:   }, [
  492:     showResults,
  493:     assignmentLoading,
  494:     assessmentMode,
  495:     integrityStarted,
  496:   ]);
  497: 
  498:   function beginFullscreenCountdown() {
  499:     if (
  500:       !assessmentMode ||
  501:       !integrityStarted ||
  502:       showResults ||
  503:       finishingRef.current ||
  504:       fullscreenExitActiveRef.current
  505:     ) {
  506:       return;
  507:     }
  508: 
  509:     fullscreenExitActiveRef.current = true;
  510: 
  511:     setFullscreenCountdown(FULLSCREEN_EXIT_COUNTDOWN_SECONDS);
  512: 
  513:     appendIntegrityIncident(
  514:       "fullscreen_exit",
  515:       `Fullscreen was exited. A ${FULLSCREEN_EXIT_COUNTDOWN_SECONDS}-second return countdown started.`,
  516:     );
  517: 
  518:     let remaining = FULLSCREEN_EXIT_COUNTDOWN_SECONDS;
  519: 
  520:     countdownTimerRef.current = setInterval(() => {
  521:       remaining -= 1;
  522:       setFullscreenCountdown(Math.max(remaining, 0));
  523: 
  524:       if (remaining <= 0) {
  525:         if (countdownTimerRef.current) {
  526:           clearInterval(countdownTimerRef.current);
  527:           countdownTimerRef.current = null;
  528:         }
  529: 
  530:         void finishQuiz({
  531:           terminated: true,
  532:           reason:
  533:             "The learner exited fullscreen and did not return within 5 seconds.",
  534:         });
  535:       }
  536:     }, 1000);
  537:   }
  538: 
  539:   function resolveFullscreenExit() {
  540:     if (!fullscreenExitActiveRef.current) {
  541:       return;
  542:     }
  543: 
  544:     fullscreenExitActiveRef.current = false;
  545: 
  546:     if (countdownTimerRef.current) {
  547:       clearInterval(countdownTimerRef.current);
  548:       countdownTimerRef.current = null;
  549:     }
  550: 
  551:     setFullscreenCountdown(null);
  552: 
  553:     appendIntegrityIncident(
  554:       "fullscreen_restored",
  555:       "Fullscreen was restored before the five-second termination countdown expired.",
  556:     );
  557:   }
  558: 
  559:   const handleFullscreenChange = useEffectEvent(() => {
  560:     if (finishingRef.current) {
  561:       return;
  562:     }
  563: 
  564:     if (
  565:       document.fullscreenElement ===
  566:       assessmentRootRef.current
  567:     ) {
  568:       resolveFullscreenExit();
  569:     } else {
  570:       beginFullscreenCountdown();
  571:     }
  572:   });
  573: 
  574:   const handleVisibilityChange = useEffectEvent(() => {
  575:     if (finishingRef.current) {
  576:       return;
  577:     }
  578: 
  579:     if (document.visibilityState === "hidden") {
  580:       appendIntegrityIncident(
  581:         "page_hidden",
  582:         "The assessment page became hidden.",
  583:       );
  584: 
  585:       setIntegrityWarning(
  586:         "The assessment page was hidden. This incident has been recorded.",
  587:       );
  588:     } else {
  589:       appendIntegrityIncident(
  590:         "page_visible",
  591:         "The assessment page became visible again.",
  592:       );
  593:     }
  594:   });
  595: 
  596:   useEffect(() => {
  597:     if (
  598:       !assessmentMode ||
  599:       !integrityStarted ||
  600:       showResults
  601:     ) {
  602:       return;
  603:     }
  604: 
  605:     function onFullscreenChange() {
  606:       handleFullscreenChange();
  607:     }
  608: 
  609:     function onVisibilityChange() {
  610:       handleVisibilityChange();
  611:     }
  612: 
  613:     document.addEventListener(
  614:       "fullscreenchange",
  615:       onFullscreenChange,
  616:     );
  617:     document.addEventListener(
  618:       "visibilitychange",
  619:       onVisibilityChange,
  620:     );
  621: 
  622:     return () => {
  623:       document.removeEventListener(
  624:         "fullscreenchange",
  625:         onFullscreenChange,
  626:       );
  627:       document.removeEventListener(
  628:         "visibilitychange",
  629:         onVisibilityChange,
  630:       );
  631:     };
  632:   }, [
  633:     assessmentMode,
  634:     integrityStarted,
  635:     showResults,
  636:   ]);
  637: 
  638:   async function enterAssessmentMode() {
  639:     if (!assessmentRootRef.current) {
  640:       return;
  641:     }
  642: 
  643:     if (!document.fullscreenEnabled) {
  644:       toast.error(
  645:         "Fullscreen is not available in this browser. Ask your teacher for support.",
  646:       );
  647:       return;
  648:     }
  649: 
  650:     try {
  651:       await assessmentRootRef.current.requestFullscreen();
  652: 
  653:       const startedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  654:       setIntegritySessionStartedAt(startedAt);
  655:       setIntegrityStarted(true);
  656:       setIntegrityWarning("");
  657:       integrityTerminatedRef.current = false;
  658:       setIntegrityTerminated(false);
  659:       setIntegrityTerminationReason("");
  660:       finishingRef.current = false;
  661:       integrityIncidentsRef.current = [];
  662: 
  663:       toast.success("Monitored assessment started.");
  664:     } catch (error) {
  665:       console.error(
  666:         "Unable to enter monitored assessment mode:",
  667:         error,
  668:       );
  669: 
  670:       toast.error(
  671:         "Fullscreen assessment mode could not be started.",
  672:       );
  673:     }
  674:   }
  675: 
  676:   async function returnToFullscreen() {
  677:     if (!assessmentRootRef.current) {
  678:       return;
  679:     }
  680: 
  681:     try {
  682:       await assessmentRootRef.current.requestFullscreen();
  683:     } catch {
  684:       toast.error(
  685:         "Fullscreen could not be restored. Return before the countdown reaches zero.",
  686:       );
  687:     }
  688:   }
  689: 
  690:   useEffect(() => {
  691:     async function saveNormalQuizResult() {
  692:       if (
  693:         !showResults ||
  694:         resultSaved ||
  695:         !user ||
  696:         quizSaveInProgress.current
  697:       ) {
  698:         return;
  699:       }
  700: 
  701:       quizSaveInProgress.current = true;
  702: 
  703:       try {
  704:         await saveQuizResult({
  705:           uid: user.uid,
  706:           quizId: quiz.id,
  707:           topicId: quiz.topicId,
  708:           title: quiz.title,
  709:           scorePercent,
  710:           correctCount,
  711:           totalQuestions: quiz.questions.length,
  712:           earnedXP:
  713:             integrityTerminatedRef.current
  714:               ? 0
  715:               : awardedXP,
  716:         });
  717: 
  718:         setResultSaved(true);
  719: 
  720:         toast.success(
  721:           integrityTerminated
  722:             ? "Assessment result saved. No XP was awarded."
  723:             : `Quiz result saved! +${awardedXP} XP`,
  724:         );
  725:       } catch (error) {
  726:         console.error("Quiz save error:", error);
  727:         toast.error("Could not save quiz result.");
  728:       } finally {
  729:         quizSaveInProgress.current = false;
  730:       }
  731:     }
  732: 
  733:     void saveNormalQuizResult();
  734:   }, [
  735:     showResults,
  736:     resultSaved,
  737:     user,
  738:     quiz.id,
  739:     quiz.topicId,
  740:     quiz.title,
  741:     quiz.questions.length,
  742:     scorePercent,
  743:     correctCount,
  744:     awardedXP,
  745:     integrityTerminated,
  746:   ]);
  747: 
  748:   useEffect(() => {
  749:     async function saveLinkedAssignmentResult() {
  750:       if (
  751:         !showResults ||
  752:         !assignmentId ||
  753:         !linkedAssignment ||
  754:         assignmentResultSaved ||
  755:         !user ||
  756:         assignmentSaveInProgress.current
  757:       ) {
  758:         return;
  759:       }
  760: 
  761:       assignmentSaveInProgress.current = true;
  762: 
  763:       try {
  764:         await saveAssignmentResult({
  765:           assignmentId,
  766:           studentId: user.uid,
  767:           classId: linkedAssignment.classId,
  768:           teacherId: linkedAssignment.teacherId,
  769:           assignmentType: "quiz",
  770:           resourceId: linkedAssignment.resourceId || quiz.topicId,
  771:           score: correctCount,
  772:           totalQuestions: quiz.questions.length,
  773:           percentage: scorePercent,
  774:           earnedXP:
  775:             integrityTerminatedRef.current
  776:               ? 0
  777:               : awardedXP,
  778:           timeTakenSeconds: Math.max(0, quizDurationSeconds - timeLeft),
  779: 
  780:           ...(assessmentMode
  781:             ? {
  782:                 deliveryMode: "assessment" as const,
  783:                 integritySessionStartedAt,
  784:                 integrityIncidents: integrityIncidentsRef.current,
  785:                 integrityTerminated,
  786:                 integrityTerminationReason,
  787:               }
  788:             : {
  789:                 deliveryMode: "practice" as const,
  790:               }),
  791:         });
  792: 
  793:         setAssignmentResultSaved(true);
  794: 
  795:         toast.success(
  796:           integrityTerminated
  797:             ? "Assessment automatically submitted."
  798:             : "Assignment marked as completed.",
  799:         );
  800:       } catch (error) {
  801:         console.error("Assignment result save error:", error);
  802: 
  803:         toast.error(
  804:           error instanceof Error
  805:             ? error.message
  806:             : "Could not mark the assignment as completed.",
  807:         );
  808:       } finally {
  809:         assignmentSaveInProgress.current = false;
  810:       }
  811:     }
  812: 
  813:     void saveLinkedAssignmentResult();
  814:   }, [
  815:     showResults,
  816:     assignmentId,
  817:     linkedAssignment,
  818:     assignmentResultSaved,
  819:     user,
  820:     quiz.topicId,
  821:     quiz.questions.length,
  822:     correctCount,
  823:     scorePercent,
  824:     awardedXP,
  825:     timeLeft,
  826:     quizDurationSeconds,
  827:     assessmentMode,
  828:     integritySessionStartedAt,
  829:     integrityTerminated,
  830:     integrityTerminationReason,
  831:   ]);
  832: 
  833:   function formatTime(seconds: number) {
  834:     const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
  835:     const remainingSeconds = seconds % 60;
  836: 
  837:     return `${minutes}:${remainingSeconds
  838:       .toString()
  839:       .padStart(2, "0")}`;
  840:   }
  841: 
  842:   function saveAnswer(answer: string) {
  843:     if (assessmentMode && !integrityStarted) {
  844:       return;
  845:     }
  846: 
  847:     setAnswers((previousAnswers) => ({
  848:       ...previousAnswers,
  849:       [currentQuestion.id]: answer,
  850:     }));
  851:   }
  852: 
  853:   function goNext() {
  854:     if (currentIndex < quiz.questions.length - 1) {
  855:       setCurrentIndex((previousIndex) => previousIndex + 1);
  856:       setIntegrityWarning("");
  857:       return;
  858:     }
  859: 
  860:     void finishQuiz();
  861:   }
  862: 
  863:   function goPrevious() {
  864:     if (currentIndex > 0) {
  865:       setCurrentIndex((previousIndex) => previousIndex - 1);
  866:       setIntegrityWarning("");
  867:     }
  868:   }
  869: 
  870:   if (assignmentLoading) {
  871:     return (
  872:       <Card>
  873:         <p className="font-bold text-slate-700">
  874:           Preparing your assigned quiz...

  878:   }

  888:           {assignmentError}
  889:         </p>
  890:       </Card>
  891:     );
  892:   }
  893:

  895:     assessmentMode &&
  896:     !showResults &&
  897:     !integrityStarted
  898:   ) {
  899:     return (
  900:       <div
  901:         ref={assessmentRootRef}
  902:         className="min-h-screen bg-slate-100 p-6"
  903:       >
  904:         <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl">
  905:           <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border-0 p-0">
  906:             <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-950 via-indigo-950 to-violet-950 p-8 text-white">
  907:               <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-indigo-200">
  908:                 <ShieldCheck className="h-5 w-5" />
  909: 
  910:                 <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.16em]">
  911:                   Monitored Quiz Assessment
  912:                 </p>
  913:               </div>
  914: 
  915:               <h1 className="mt-4 text-4xl font-black">
  916:                 {quiz.title}
  917:               </h1>
  918: 
  919:               <p className="mt-3 max-w-2xl text-indigo-100">
  920:                 {quiz.questions.length} questions Â· {quiz.estimatedTime}
  921:               </p>
  922:             </div>
  923: 
  924:             <div className="space-y-6 p-8">
  925:               <div className="rounded-2xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-5">
  926:                 <p className="font-black text-amber-950">
  927:                   Integrity monitoring

  929: 
  930:                 <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-amber-900">
  931:                   This is integrity monitoring, not a guaranteed lockdown

  982:               <div className="flex justify-center">

  985:                 </div>

  987: 
  988:               <p className="mt-5 text-center text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-red-200">
  989:                 Monitored assessment

  997:                 Automatically submitted due to an integrity event

 1000: 
 1001:             <div className="space-y-6 p-8">

FILE: components\teacher\exam-assignments\AssignExamModal.tsx

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
    4: import toast from "react-hot-toast";
    5: 
    6: import { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";
    7: import { getTeacherClasses, type TeacherClass } from "@/services/classService";
    8: import { createExamAssignment } from "@/services/examAssignmentService";
    9: import type { SavedExamQuestionSet } from "@/services/examQuestionService";
   10: 
   11: type Props = {
   12:   questionSet: SavedExamQuestionSet;
   13:   onClose: () => void;
   14:   onAssigned?: (assignmentId: string) => void;
   15: };
   16: 
   17: export default function AssignExamModal({
   18:   questionSet,
   19:   onClose,
   20:   onAssigned,
   21: }: Props) {
   22:   const { user } = useAuth();
   23: 
   24:   const [classes, setClasses] = useState<TeacherClass[]>([]);
   25:   const [classId, setClassId] = useState("");
   26:   const [title, setTitle] = useState(questionSet.title);
   27:   const [instructions, setInstructions] = useState(
   28:     "Answer every question. Show working where required.",
   29:   );
   30:   const [dueDate, setDueDate] = useState("");
   31:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   32:   const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
   33: 
   34:   useEffect(() => {
   35:     async function load() {
   36:       if (!user?.uid) {
   37:         return;
   38:       }
   39: 
   40:       try {
   41:         const loaded = await getTeacherClasses(user.uid);
   42: 
   43:         setClasses(loaded.filter((item) => item.status === "active"));
   44:       } catch (error) {
   45:         console.error(error);
   46:         toast.error("Classes could not be loaded.");
   47:       } finally {
   48:         setLoading(false);
   49:       }
   50:     }
   51: 
   52:     void load();
   53:   }, [user?.uid]);
   54: 
   55:   async function assign() {
   56:     if (!user?.uid) {
   57:       toast.error("You must be logged in.");
   58:       return;
   59:     }
   60: 
   61:     const selectedClass = classes.find((item) => item.id === classId);
   62: 
   63:     if (!selectedClass) {
   64:       toast.error("Select a class.");
   65:       return;
   66:     }
   67: 
   68:     if (!dueDate) {
   69:       toast.error("Select a due date.");
   70:       return;
   71:     }
   72: 
   73:     setSaving(true);
   74: 
   75:     try {
   76:       const assignmentId = await createExamAssignment({
   77:         teacherId: user.uid,
   78:         teacherName: user.displayName || "Teacher",
   79:         classId: selectedClass.id,
   80:         className: selectedClass.name,
   81:         studentIds: selectedClass.studentIds,
   82:         questionSetId: questionSet.id,
   83:         questionSetTitle: questionSet.title,
   84:         questionSetSnapshot: questionSet.content,
   85:         title,
   86:         instructions,
   87:         dueDate: new Date(`${dueDate}T23:59:59`),
   88:       });
   89: 
   90:       toast.success("Exam assignment created.");
   91: 
   92:       onAssigned?.(assignmentId);
   93:     } catch (error) {
   94:       console.error(error);
   95:       toast.error(
   96:         error instanceof Error
   97:           ? error.message
   98:           : "Assignment could not be created.",
   99:       );
  100:     } finally {
  101:       setSaving(false);
  102:     }
  103:   }
  104: 
  105:   return (
  106:     <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-950/60 p-4">
  107:       <div className="w-full max-w-2xl rounded-3xl bg-white p-6 shadow-2xl">
  108:         <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
  109:           <div>
  110:             <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-indigo-600">
  111:               Assign assessment
  112:             </p>
  113: 
  114:             <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
  115:               {questionSet.title}
  116:             </h2>
  117:           </div>
  118: 
  119:           <button
  120:             type="button"
  121:             onClick={onClose}
  122:             className="rounded-lg border px-3 py-2 font-bold"
  123:           >
  124:             Close
  125:           </button>
  126:         </div>
  127: 
  128:         <div className="mt-6 space-y-5">
  129:           <label className="block">
  130:             <span className="text-sm font-bold">Class</span>
  131: 
  132:             <select
  133:               value={classId}
  134:               onChange={(event) => setClassId(event.target.value)}
  135:               disabled={loading}
  136:               className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border px-4 py-3"
  137:             >
  138:               <option value="">Select a class</option>
  139: 
  140:               {classes.map((item) => (
  141:                 <option key={item.id} value={item.id}>
  142:                   {item.name} ({item.studentIds.length} students)
  143:                 </option>
  144:               ))}
  145:             </select>
  146:           </label>
  147: 
  148:           <label className="block">
  149:             <span className="text-sm font-bold">Assignment title</span>
  150: 
  151:             <input
  152:               value={title}
  153:               onChange={(event) => setTitle(event.target.value)}
  154:               className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border px-4 py-3"
  155:             />
  156:           </label>
  157: 
  158:           <label className="block">
  159:             <span className="text-sm font-bold">Instructions</span>
  160: 
  161:             <textarea
  162:               rows={4}
  163:               value={instructions}
  164:               onChange={(event) => setInstructions(event.target.value)}
  165:               className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border px-4 py-3"
  166:             />
  167:           </label>
  168: 
  169:           <label className="block">
  170:             <span className="text-sm font-bold">Due date</span>
  171: 
  172:             <input
  173:               type="date"
  174:               value={dueDate}
  175:               onChange={(event) => setDueDate(event.target.value)}
  176:               className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border px-4 py-3"
  177:             />
  178:           </label>
  179:         </div>
  180: 
  181:         <div className="mt-7 flex justify-end gap-3">
  182:           <button
  183:             type="button"
  184:             onClick={onClose}
  185:             className="rounded-xl border px-5 py-3 font-bold"
  186:           >
  187:             Cancel
  188:           </button>
  189: 
  190:           <button
  191:             type="button"
  192:             onClick={() => {
  193:               void assign();
  194:             }}
  195:             disabled={saving}
  196:             className="rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 px-5 py-3 font-bold text-white disabled:bg-slate-300"
  197:           >
  198:             {saving ? "Assigning..." : "Assign to Class"}
  199:           </button>
  200:         </div>
  201:       </div>
  202:     </div>
  203:   );
  204: }

  665:                         </Link>
  666: 
  667:                         <Link
  668:                           href={interventionHref}
  669:                           className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg bg-teal-600 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-black text-white"
  670:                         >
  671:                           Intervention
  672:                           <ChevronRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
  673:                         </Link>
  674:                       </div>
  675:                     </div>
  676: 
  677:                     {student.reasons.length > 0 && (
  678:                       <ul className="mt-3 space-y-1 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-600">
  679:                         {student.reasons.map((reasonItem) => (
  680:                           <li
  681:                             key={reasonItem}
  682:                             className="flex items-start gap-2"
  683:                           >
  684:                             <AlertTriangle className="mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-amber-600" />
  685:                             {reasonItem}
  686:                           </li>
  687:                         ))}
  688:                       </ul>
  689:                     )}
  690:                   </div>
  691:                 );
  692:               })}
  693:             </div>
  694:           )}
  695:         </Card>
  696:       </div>
  697:     </div>
  698:   );
  699: }
  700: 
  701: function Metric({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  702:   return (
  703:     <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-4">
  704:       <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-400">{label}</p>
  705:       <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">{value}</p>
  706:     </div>
  707:   );
  708: }
  709: 
  710: function SmallMetric({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  711:   return (
  712:     <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-4 shadow-sm">
  713:       <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-400">{label}</p>
  714:       <p className="mt-2 text-xl font-black text-slate-950">{value}</p>
  715:     </div>
  716:   );
  717: }
  718: 
  719: function MiniMetric({ label, value }: { label: string; value: number }) {
  720:   return (
  721:     <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-4">
  722:       <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-400">{label}</p>
  723:       <p className="mt-1 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">{value}</p>
  724:     </div>
  725:   );
  726: }

   30: export default function ExamGradeBoundaryCard({
   31:   assignment,
   32:   gradeIntelligence,
   33: }: {
   34:   assignment: ExamAssignment;
   35:   gradeIntelligence: ExamGradeIntelligence;
   36: }) {
   37:   const [source, setSource] =
   38:     useState<AnalyticsBoundarySource>(
   39:       gradeIntelligence.boundarySource,
   40:     );
   41: 
   42:   const [title, setTitle] =
   43:     useState(
   44:       gradeIntelligence.boundarySetTitle,
   45:     );
   46: 
   47:   const [academicYear, setAcademicYear] =
   48:     useState(
   49:       gradeIntelligence.boundaryAcademicYear ||
   50:         "",
   51:     );
   52: 
   53:   const [
   54:     assessmentTitle,

   57:     gradeIntelligence.boundaryAssessmentTitle ||
   58:       assignment.title,
   59:   );

   62:     useState(
   63:       gradeIntelligence.boundarySourceNote ||
   64:         "",
   65:     );
   66: 
   67:   const [saving, setSaving] =
   68:     useState(false);
   69: 
   70:   const initialRows = useMemo(
   71:     () =>
   72:       gradeIntelligence.boundaries
   73:         .filter(
   74:           (boundary) =>
   75:             boundary.grade !== "U",
   76:         )
   77:         .map((boundary) => ({
   78:           grade: boundary.grade,
   79:           minimumMark:
   80:             String(
   81:               boundary.minimumMark,
   82:             ),
   83:         })),
   84:     [gradeIntelligence.boundaries],
   85:   );
   86: 
   87:   const [rows, setRows] =
   88:     useState(initialRows);
   89: 
   90:   function updateRow(
   91:     grade: GradeLabel,
   92:     value: string,
   93:   ) {
   94:     setRows((current) =>
   95:       current.map((row) =>
   96:         row.grade === grade
   97:           ? {
   98:               ...row,
   99:               minimumMark: value,
  100:             }
  101:           : row,
  102:       ),
  103:     );
  104:   }
  105: 
  106:   async function save() {
  107:     try {
  108:       setSaving(true);
  109: 
  110:       await saveExamBoundaryConfiguration({
  111:         assignmentId:
  112:           assignment.id,
  113:         teacherId:
  114:           assignment.teacherId,
  115:         qualification:
  116:           gradeIntelligence.qualification,

  118:           assignment.questionSetSnapshot

  123:         source,

  126:           gradeIntelligence.totalMarks,

  128:           rows.map((row) => ({
  129:             grade: row.grade,
  130:             minimumMark:
  131:               Number(row.minimumMark),
  132:           })),
  133:       });
  134: 
  135:       toast.success(
  136:         "Assessment grade boundaries saved.",
  137:       );
  138: 
  139:       window.location.reload();
  140:     } catch (error) {
  141:       toast.error(
  142:         error instanceof Error
  143:           ? error.message
  144:           : "Grade boundaries could not be saved.",
  145:       );
  146:     } finally {
  147:       setSaving(false);
  148:     }
  149:   }
  150: 
  151:   async function reset() {
  152:     try {
  153:       setSaving(true);
  154: 
  155:       await clearExamBoundaryConfiguration({
  156:         assignmentId:
  157:           assignment.id,
  158:         teacherId:
  159:           assignment.teacherId,
  160:       });
  161: 
  162:       toast.success(
  163:         "Paper returned to the CS Master indicative fallback.",
  164:       );
  165: 
  166:       window.location.reload();
  167:     } catch (error) {
  168:       toast.error(
  169:         error instanceof Error
  170:           ? error.message
  171:           : "The boundary configuration could not be cleared.",
  172:       );
  173:     } finally {
  174:       setSaving(false);
  175:     }
  176:   }
  177: 
  178:   return (
  179:     <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-indigo-200 p-0">
  180:       <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-950 to-violet-800 p-6 text-white">
  181:         <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-indigo-200">
  182:           <ShieldCheck className="h-5 w-5" />
  183:

  185:             Grade boundary provenance
  186:           </p>
  187:         </div>
  188: 
  189:         <h3 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black">
  190:           Configure this assessment&apos;s grade boundaries
  191:         </h3>
  192: 
  193:         <p className="mt-2 max-w-4xl text-sm leading-6 text-white/75">
  194:           Use verified official boundaries when this is a known exam paper,
  195:           teacher boundaries for internal assessments, or keep the CS Master
  196:           indicative fallback. The source is carried into QLA and CSV exports.
  197:         </p>
  198:       </div>
  199: 
  200:       <div className="p-6">
  201:         <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-2">

  204:               Boundary source
  205:             </span>
  206: 
  207:             <select
  208:               value={source}
  209:               onChange={(event) =>
  210:                 setSource(
  211:                   event.target.value as AnalyticsBoundarySource,
  212:                 )
  213:               }
  214:               className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 py-3"
  215:             >
  216:               <option value="indicative">
  217:                 Indicative
  218:               </option>
  219:               <option value="teacher">
  220:                 Teacher
  221:               </option>

  223:                 Official

    1: "use client";

    5:   Save,

    7: } from "lucide-react";

    9:

   13:   ExamAssignment,
   14:   ExamIntegrityPolicy,
   15:   ExamVisibilityAction,
   16: } from "@/types/examAssignment";
   17: 
   18: export default function ExamIntegrityPolicyCard({
   19:   assignment,
   20:   teacherId,
   21:   onSaved,
   22: }: {
   23:   assignment: ExamAssignment;
   24:   teacherId: string;
   25:   onSaved: (
   26:     policy: ExamIntegrityPolicy,
   27:   ) => void;
   28: }) {
   29:   const [policy, setPolicy] =
   30:     useState<ExamIntegrityPolicy>(
   31:       assignment.integrityPolicy,
   32:     );
   33: 
   34:   const [saving, setSaving] =
   35:     useState(false);
   36: 
   37:   async function save() {
   38:     try {
   39:       setSaving(true);
   40: 
   41:       const updated =
   42:         await updateExamIntegrityPolicy({
   43:           assignmentId:
   44:             assignment.id,
   45:           teacherId,
   46:           policy,
   47:         });
   48: 
   49:       setPolicy(updated);
   50:       onSaved(updated);
   51: 
   52:       toast.success(
   53:         "Exam integrity settings saved.",
   54:       );
   55:     } catch (error) {
   56:       console.error(
   57:         "Unable to save exam integrity settings:",
   58:         error,
   59:       );
   60: 
   61:       toast.error(
   62:         error instanceof Error
   63:           ? error.message
   64:           : "Integrity settings could not be saved.",
   65:       );
   66:     } finally {
   67:       setSaving(false);
   68:     }
   69:   }
   70: 
   71:   return (
   72:     <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-indigo-200">
   73:       <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-950 to-violet-900 p-6 text-white">
   74:         <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-indigo-200">
   75:           <ShieldCheck className="h-5 w-5" />
   76:           <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.16em]">
   77:             Exam integrity monitoring
   78:           </p>

   86:           CS Master records fullscreen and page-visibility events to support
   87:           teacher review. This is integrity monitoring, not a guaranteed
   88:           lockdown browser.
   89:         </p>
   90:       </div>
   91: 
   92:       <div className="grid gap-5 p-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
   93:         <label className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-4">
   94:           <input
   95:             type="checkbox"
   96:             checked={policy.enabled}
   97:             onChange={(event) =>
   98:               setPolicy(
   99:                 (current) => ({
  100:                   ...current,
  101:                   enabled:
  102:                     event.target.checked,
  103:                 }),
  104:               )
  105:             }
  106:             className="mt-1 h-4 w-4"
  107:           />
  108: 
  109:           <div>
  110:             <p className="font-black text-slate-900">

  112:             </p>
  113:

  115:               Require the monitored exam-entry workflow for this assignment.
  116:             </p>
  117:           </div>
  118:         </label>
  119:

  211:             ? "Saving..."

FILE: components\teacher\exam-assignments\ExamIntegrityReportCard.tsx
==============================================================================================================
    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import {
    4:   AlertTriangle,
    5:   CheckCircle2,
    6:   ShieldAlert,
    7: } from "lucide-react";
    8: 
    9: import Card from "@/components/ui/Card";
   10: import type {
   11:   ExamIntegrityIncident,
   12:   ExamSubmission,
   13: } from "@/types/examAssignment";
   14: 
   15: function formatDate(
   16:   value: Date | null,
   17: ): string {
   18:   if (!value) {
   19:     return "Time unavailable";
   20:   }
   21: 
   22:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(
   23:     "en-GB",
   24:     {
   25:       day: "2-digit",
   26:       month: "short",
   27:       year: "numeric",
   28:       hour: "2-digit",
   29:       minute: "2-digit",
   30:       second: "2-digit",
   31:     },
   32:   ).format(value);
   33: }
   34: 
   35: function incidentLabel(
   36:   incident: ExamIntegrityIncident,
   37: ): string {
   38:   switch (incident.type) {
   39:     case "fullscreen_exit":
   40:       return "Exited fullscreen";
   41: 
   42:     case "fullscreen_restored":
   43:       return "Returned to fullscreen";
   44: 
   45:     case "page_hidden":
   46:       return "Exam page hidden";
   47: 
   48:     case "page_visible":
   49:       return "Exam page visible again";
   50: 
   51:     case "integrity_termination":
   52:       return "Exam terminated";
   53: 
   54:     default:
   55:       return incident.type;
   56:   }
   57: }
   58: 
   59: export default function ExamIntegrityReportCard({
   60:   submission,
   61: }: {
   62:   submission: ExamSubmission;
   63: }) {
   64:   const incidents =
   65:     submission.integrityIncidents || [];
   66: 
   67:   const violationCount =
   68:     incidents.filter(
   69:       (incident) =>
   70:         incident.type ===
   71:           "fullscreen_exit" ||
   72:         incident.type ===
   73:           "page_hidden" ||
   74:         incident.type ===
   75:           "integrity_termination",
   76:     ).length;
   77: 
   78:   return (
   79:     <Card
   80:       className={`overflow-hidden rounded-3xl ${
   81:         submission.integrityTerminated
   82:           ? "border border-red-300"
   83:           : "border border-slate-200"
   84:       }`}
   85:     >
   86:       <div
   87:         className={`p-6 ${
   88:           submission.integrityTerminated
   89:             ? "bg-red-950 text-white"
   90:             : "bg-slate-950 text-white"
   91:         }`}
   92:       >
   93:         <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   94:           <ShieldAlert className="h-5 w-5" />
   95: 
   96:           <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-white/70">
   97:             Integrity review
   98:           </p>
   99:         </div>
  100: 
  101:         <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between">
  102:           <div>
  103:             <h2 className="text-2xl font-black">
  104:               Exam integrity incidents
  105:             </h2>
  106: 
  107:             <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-white/70">
  108:               {violationCount} recorded violation
  109:               {violationCount === 1
  110:                 ? ""
  111:                 : "s"}
  112:             </p>
  113:           </div>
  114: 
  115:           {submission.integrityTerminated ? (
  116:             <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-red-100 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-black text-red-800">
  117:               <AlertTriangle className="h-4 w-4" />
  118:               Auto-terminated
  119:             </span>
  120:           ) : (
  121:             <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-100 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-black text-emerald-800">
  122:               <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4" />
  123:               Not auto-terminated
  124:             </span>
  125:           )}
  126:         </div>
  127:       </div>
  128: 
  129:       {submission.integrityTerminated && (
  130:         <div className="border-b border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-5">
  131:           <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-red-600">
  132:             Termination reason
  133:           </p>
  134: 
  135:           <p className="mt-2 font-bold text-red-950">
  136:             {submission.integrityTerminationReason ||
  137:               "Integrity policy triggered automatic submission."}
  138:           </p>
  139:         </div>
  140:       )}
  141: 
  142:       <div className="p-6">
  143:         {incidents.length === 0 ? (
  144:           <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
  145:             No integrity incidents were recorded for this submission.
  146:           </p>
  147:         ) : (
  148:           <div className="space-y-3">
  149:             {incidents.map(
  150:               (incident) => (
  151:                 <div
  152:                   key={incident.id}
  153:                   className="grid gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4 md:grid-cols-[1fr_auto]"
  154:                 >
  155:                   <div>
  156:                     <p className="font-black text-slate-900">
  157:                       {incidentLabel(
  158:                         incident,
  159:                       )}
  160:                     </p>
  161: 
  162:                     <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
  163:                       {formatDate(
  164:                         incident.occurredAt,
  165:                       )}
  166:                       {incident.questionNumber
  167:                         ? ` Â· Question ${incident.questionNumber}`
  168:                         : ""}
  169:                     </p>
  170: 
  171:                     {incident.detail && (
  172:                       <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-700">
  173:                         {incident.detail}
  174:                       </p>
  175:                     )}
  176:                   </div>
  177:                 </div>
  178:               ),
  179:             )}
  180:           </div>
  181:         )}
  182: 
  183:         <p className="mt-5 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
  184:           Integrity events are contextual evidence for teacher review. They do
  185:           not prove misconduct and should not be treated as a secure-browser
  186:           guarantee.
  187:         </p>
  188:       </div>
  189:     </Card>
  190:   );
  191: }

    1: import {
    2:   deleteField,
    3:   doc,
    4:   getDoc,
    5:   serverTimestamp,
    6:   updateDoc,
    7: } from "firebase/firestore";
    8: 
    9: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
   10: import type {
   11:   ExamBoundaryConfigurationInput,
   12: } from "@/types/examBoundaryConfiguration";
   13: 
   14: function roundPercentage(
   15:   value: number,
   16: ): number {
   17:   return Math.round(value * 1000000) / 1000000;
   18: }
   19: 
   20: function validateInput(
   21:   input: ExamBoundaryConfigurationInput,
   22: ): void {
   23:   if (!input.assignmentId.trim()) {
   24:     throw new Error("A valid exam assignment is required.");
   25:   }
   26: 
   27:   if (!input.teacherId.trim()) {
   28:     throw new Error("A signed-in teacher is required.");
   29:   }
   30: 
   31:   if (!input.title.trim()) {
   32:     throw new Error("Enter a boundary-set title.");
   33:   }
   34: 
   35:   if (
   36:     !Number.isFinite(input.totalMarks) ||
   37:     input.totalMarks <= 0
   38:   ) {
   39:     throw new Error("The assessment must have a valid total mark.");
   40:   }
   41: 
   42:   if (!input.boundaries.length) {
   43:     throw new Error("Enter at least one grade boundary.");
   44:   }
   45: 
   46:   const seenGrades = new Set<string>();
   47: 
   48:   for (const boundary of input.boundaries) {
   49:     if (seenGrades.has(boundary.grade)) {
   50:       throw new Error(`Duplicate boundary for grade ${boundary.grade}.`);
   51:     }
   52: 
   53:     seenGrades.add(boundary.grade);
   54: 
   55:     if (
   56:       !Number.isFinite(boundary.minimumMark) ||
   57:       boundary.minimumMark < 0 ||
   58:       boundary.minimumMark > input.totalMarks
   59:     ) {
   60:       throw new Error(
   61:         `The minimum mark for grade ${boundary.grade} must be between 0 and ${input.totalMarks}.`,
   62:       );
   63:     }
   64:   }
   65: 
   66:   if (
   67:     input.source === "official" &&
   68:     (!input.examBoard.trim() ||
   69:       !input.academicYear.trim() ||
   70:       !input.assessmentTitle.trim())
   71:   ) {
   72:     throw new Error(
   73:       "Official boundaries require an exam board, academic year/series and assessment title.",
   74:     );
   75:   }
   76: }
   77: 
   78: async function assertTeacherOwnsAssignment({
   79:   assignmentId,
   80:   teacherId,
   81: }: {
   82:   assignmentId: string;
   83:   teacherId: string;
   84: }): Promise<void> {
   85:   const reference = doc(
   86:     db,
   87:     "examAssignments",
   88:     assignmentId,
   89:   );
   90: 
   91:   const snapshot = await getDoc(reference);
   92: 
   93:   if (!snapshot.exists()) {
   94:     throw new Error("Exam assignment not found.");
   95:   }
   96: 
   97:   if (
   98:     snapshot.data().teacherId !==
   99:     teacherId.trim()
  100:   ) {
  101:     throw new Error(
  102:       "You do not have permission to change this assessment.",
  103:     );
  104:   }
  105: }
  106: 
  107: export async function saveExamBoundaryConfiguration(
  108:   input: ExamBoundaryConfigurationInput,
  109: ): Promise<void> {
  110:   validateInput(input);
  111: 
  112:   await assertTeacherOwnsAssignment({
  113:     assignmentId: input.assignmentId,
  114:     teacherId: input.teacherId,
  115:   });
  116: 
  117:   const embeddedBoundarySet = {
  118:     id: `assignment-${input.assignmentId}-boundaries`,
  119:     title: input.title.trim(),
  120:     qualification: input.qualification,
  121:     examBoard: input.examBoard.trim(),
  122:     academicYear: input.academicYear.trim(),
  123:     assessmentTitle: input.assessmentTitle.trim(),
  124:     source: input.source,
  125:     sourceNote: input.sourceNote.trim(),
  126:     verifiedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  127:     boundaries: input.boundaries.map((boundary) => ({
  128:       grade: boundary.grade,
  129:       minimumMark: boundary.minimumMark,
  130:       minimumPercentage: roundPercentage(
  131:         (boundary.minimumMark / input.totalMarks) * 100,
  132:       ),
  133:     })),
  134:   };
  135: 
  136:   await updateDoc(
  137:     doc(
  138:       db,
  139:       "examAssignments",
  140:       input.assignmentId,
  141:     ),
  142:     {
  143:       "questionSetSnapshot.gradeBoundarySet":
  144:         embeddedBoundarySet,
  145:       updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  146:     },
  147:   );
  148: }
  149: 
  150: export async function clearExamBoundaryConfiguration({
  151:   assignmentId,
  152:   teacherId,
  153: }: {
  154:   assignmentId: string;
  155:   teacherId: string;
  156: }): Promise<void> {
  157:   await assertTeacherOwnsAssignment({
  158:     assignmentId,
  159:     teacherId,
  160:   });
  161: 
  162:   await updateDoc(
  163:     doc(
  164:       db,
  165:       "examAssignments",
  166:       assignmentId,
  167:     ),
  168:     {
  169:       "questionSetSnapshot.gradeBoundarySet":
  170:         deleteField(),
  171:       updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  172:     },
  173:   );
  174: }

==============================================================================================================
FILE: services\analytics\examGradeIntelligenceService.ts
==============================================================================================================
    1: import {
    2:   getDefaultBoundarySet,
    3:   getGradeOrder,
    4: } from "@/data/analytics/gradeBoundaries";
    5: import type {
    6:   ExamAssignment,
    7:   ExamSubmission,
    8: } from "@/types/examAssignment";
    9: import type {
   10:   AnalyticsBoundarySource,
   11:   AnalyticsQualification,
   12:   GradeBoundary,
   13:   GradeBoundarySet,
   14:   GradeLabel,
   15: } from "@/types/analytics";
   16: import type {
   17:   ExamGradeBoundaryRow,
   18:   ExamGradeDistributionItem,
   19:   ExamGradeIntelligence,
   20:   ExamStudentGradeOutcome,
   21: } from "@/types/examIntelligence";
   22: 
   23: type ResolvedBoundarySet = GradeBoundarySet & {
   24:   sourceNote?: string;
   25:   verifiedAt?: string;
   26: };
   27: 
   28: function round(value: number): number {
   29:   return Math.round(value * 10) / 10;
   30: }
   31: 
   32: function normaliseQualification(
   33:   value: unknown,
   34: ): AnalyticsQualification {
   35:   const text =
   36:     typeof value === "string"
   37:       ? value.trim().toUpperCase()
   38:       : "";
   39: 
   40:   return text === "A_LEVEL" ||
   41:     text === "A LEVEL" ||
   42:     text === "A-LEVEL"
   43:     ? "A_LEVEL"
   44:     : "GCSE";
   45: }
   46: 
   47: function validBoundarySource(
   48:   value: unknown,
   49: ): AnalyticsBoundarySource | null {
   50:   return value === "official" ||
   51:     value === "teacher" ||
   52:     value === "indicative"
   53:     ? value
   54:     : null;
   55: }
   56: 
   57: function validGrade(
   58:   value: unknown,
   59:   qualification: AnalyticsQualification,
   60: ): GradeLabel | null {
   61:   if (typeof value !== "string") {
   62:     return null;
   63:   }
   64: 
   65:   const grade = value.trim().toUpperCase();
   66: 
   67:   const valid =
   68:     qualification === "A_LEVEL"
   69:       ? ["A*", "A", "B", "C", "D", "E", "U"]
   70:       : ["9", "8", "7", "6", "5", "4", "3", "2", "1", "U"];
   71: 
   72:   return valid.includes(grade)
   73:     ? (grade as GradeLabel)
   74:     : null;
   75: }
   76: 
   77: function boundarySetFromSnapshot(
   78:   assignment: ExamAssignment,
   79:   qualification: AnalyticsQualification,
   80: ): ResolvedBoundarySet | null {
   81:   const snapshot =
   82:     assignment.questionSetSnapshot as unknown as
   83:       Record<string, unknown>;
   84: 
   85:   const candidate =
   86:     snapshot.gradeBoundarySet;
   87: 
   88:   if (
   89:     !candidate ||
   90:     typeof candidate !== "object"
   91:   ) {
   92:     return null;
   93:   }
   94: 
   95:   const data =
   96:     candidate as Record<string, unknown>;
   97: 
   98:   if (!Array.isArray(data.boundaries)) {
   99:     return null;
  100:   }
  101: 
  102:   const totalMarks =
  103:     assignment.totalMarks > 0
  104:       ? assignment.totalMarks
  105:       : assignment.questionSetSnapshot.totalMarks;
  106: 
  107:   const boundaries: GradeBoundary[] =
  108:     data.boundaries
  109:       .map((item) => {
  110:         if (
  111:           !item ||
  112:           typeof item !== "object"
  113:         ) {
  114:           return null;
  115:         }
  116: 
  117:         const row =
  118:           item as Record<string, unknown>;
  119: 
  120:         const grade =
  121:           validGrade(
  122:             row.grade,
  123:             qualification,
  124:           );
  125: 
  126:         let minimumPercentage =
  127:           typeof row.minimumPercentage === "number" &&
  128:           Number.isFinite(row.minimumPercentage)
  129:             ? row.minimumPercentage
  130:             : null;
  131: 
  132:         if (
  133:           minimumPercentage === null &&
  134:           typeof row.minimumMark === "number" &&
  135:           Number.isFinite(row.minimumMark) &&
  136:           totalMarks > 0
  137:         ) {
  138:           minimumPercentage =
  139:             (row.minimumMark / totalMarks) * 100;
  140:         }
  141: 
  142:         if (
  143:           !grade ||
  144:           minimumPercentage === null ||
  145:           minimumPercentage < 0 ||
  146:           minimumPercentage > 100
  147:         ) {
  148:           return null;
  149:         }
  150: 
  151:         return {
  152:           grade,
  153:           minimumPercentage,
  154:         };
  155:       })
  156:       .filter(
  157:         (
  158:           item,
  159:         ): item is GradeBoundary =>
  160:           Boolean(item),
  161:       );
  162: 
  163:   if (!boundaries.length) {
  164:     return null;
  165:   }
  166: 
  167:   return {
  168:     id:
  169:       typeof data.id === "string" &&
  170:       data.id.trim()
  171:         ? data.id.trim()
  172:         : "embedded-exam-boundaries",
  173:     title:
  174:       typeof data.title === "string" &&
  175:       data.title.trim()
  176:         ? data.title.trim()
  177:         : "Embedded exam grade boundaries",
  178:     qualification,
  179:     examBoard:
  180:       typeof data.examBoard === "string"
  181:         ? data.examBoard
  182:         : undefined,
  183:     academicYear:
  184:       typeof data.academicYear === "string"
  185:         ? data.academicYear
  186:         : undefined,
  187:     assessmentTitle:
  188:       typeof data.assessmentTitle === "string"
  189:         ? data.assessmentTitle
  190:         : undefined,
  191:     source:
  192:       validBoundarySource(
  193:         data.source,
  194:       ) || "teacher",
  195:     boundaries,
  196:     sourceNote:
  197:       typeof data.sourceNote === "string"
  198:         ? data.sourceNote
  199:         : undefined,
  200:     verifiedAt:
  201:       typeof data.verifiedAt === "string"
  202:         ? data.verifiedAt
  203:         : undefined,
  204:   };
  205: }
  206: 
  207: function resolveBoundarySet(
  208:   assignment: ExamAssignment,
  209:   qualification: AnalyticsQualification,
  210: ): ResolvedBoundarySet {
  211:   return (
  212:     boundarySetFromSnapshot(
  213:       assignment,
  214:       qualification,
  215:     ) ||
  216:     getDefaultBoundarySet(
  217:       qualification,
  218:     )
  219:   );
  220: }
  221: 
  222: function orderedBoundaries(
  223:   boundarySet: GradeBoundarySet,
  224:   qualification: AnalyticsQualification,
  225: ): GradeBoundary[] {
  226:   const order =
  227:     getGradeOrder(qualification);
  228: 
  229:   return [...boundarySet.boundaries].sort(
  230:     (first, second) =>
  231:       order.indexOf(second.grade) -
  232:       order.indexOf(first.grade),
  233:   );
  234: }
  235: 
  236: function gradeFromPercentage(
  237:   percentage: number,
  238:   boundarySet: GradeBoundarySet,
  239:   qualification: AnalyticsQualification,
  240: ): GradeLabel {
  241:   const boundaries =
  242:     orderedBoundaries(
  243:       boundarySet,
  244:       qualification,
  245:     );
  246: 
  247:   return (
  248:     boundaries.find(
  249:       (boundary) =>
  250:         percentage >=
  251:         boundary.minimumPercentage,
  252:     )?.grade || "U"
  253:   );
  254: }
  255: 
  256: function nextGradeInformation({
  257:   grade,
  258:   awardedMarks,
  259:   totalMarks,
  260:   boundarySet,
  261:   qualification,
  262: }: {
  263:   grade: GradeLabel;
  264:   awardedMarks: number;
  265:   totalMarks: number;
  266:   boundarySet: GradeBoundarySet;
  267:   qualification: AnalyticsQualification;
  268: }): {
  269:   nextGrade: GradeLabel | null;
  270:   nextGradeMinimumMark: number | null;
  271:   marksToNextGrade: number | null;
  272:   percentagePointsToNextGrade: number | null;
  273: } {
  274:   const order =
  275:     getGradeOrder(qualification);
  276: 
  277:   const gradeIndex =
  278:     order.indexOf(grade);
  279: 
  280:   if (
  281:     gradeIndex < 0 ||
  282:     gradeIndex >=
  283:       order.length - 1
  284:   ) {
  285:     return {
  286:       nextGrade: null,
  287:       nextGradeMinimumMark: null,
  288:       marksToNextGrade: null,
  289:       percentagePointsToNextGrade: null,
  290:     };
  291:   }
  292: 
  293:   const nextGrade =
  294:     order[gradeIndex + 1];
  295: 
  296:   const nextBoundary =
  297:     boundarySet.boundaries.find(
  298:       (boundary) =>
  299:         boundary.grade === nextGrade,
  300:     );
  301: 
  302:   if (!nextBoundary) {
  303:     return {
  304:       nextGrade,
  305:       nextGradeMinimumMark: null,
  306:       marksToNextGrade: null,
  307:       percentagePointsToNextGrade: null,
  308:     };
  309:   }
  310: 
  311:   const nextGradeMinimumMark =
  312:     Math.ceil(
  313:       (nextBoundary.minimumPercentage /
  314:         100) *
  315:         totalMarks -
  316:         1e-9,
  317:     );
  318: 
  319:   const currentPercentage =
  320:     totalMarks > 0
  321:       ? (awardedMarks / totalMarks) * 100
  322:       : 0;
  323: 
  324:   return {
  325:     nextGrade,
  326:     nextGradeMinimumMark,
  327:     marksToNextGrade:
  328:       Math.max(
  329:         0,
  330:         nextGradeMinimumMark -
  331:           awardedMarks,
  332:       ),
  333:     percentagePointsToNextGrade:
  334:       round(
  335:         Math.max(
  336:           0,
  337:           nextBoundary.minimumPercentage -
  338:             currentPercentage,
  339:         ),
  340:       ),
  341:   };
  342: }
  343: 
  344: function scoreForSubmission(
  345:   submission: ExamSubmission,
  346:   assignment: ExamAssignment,
  347: ): {
  348:   awardedMarks: number;
  349:   availableMarks: number;
  350:   percentage: number;
  351: } {
  352:   const availableMarks =
  353:     submission.totalAvailableMarks > 0
  354:       ? submission.totalAvailableMarks
  355:       : assignment.totalMarks;
  356: 
  357:   const awardedMarks =
  358:     submission.totalAwardedMarks;
  359: 
  360:   return {
  361:     awardedMarks,
  362:     availableMarks,
  363:     percentage:
  364:       availableMarks > 0
  365:         ? round(
  366:             (awardedMarks /
  367:               availableMarks) *
  368:               100,
  369:           )
  370:         : submission.percentage,
  371:   };
  372: }
  373: 
  374: export function buildExamGradeIntelligence(
  375:   assignment: ExamAssignment,
  376:   submissions: ExamSubmission[],
  377: ): ExamGradeIntelligence {
  378:   const qualification =
  379:     normaliseQualification(
  380:       assignment.questionSetSnapshot
  381:         .qualification,
  382:     );
  383: 
  384:   const boundarySet =
  385:     resolveBoundarySet(
  386:       assignment,
  387:       qualification,
  388:     );
  389: 
  390:   const totalMarks =
  391:     assignment.totalMarks > 0
  392:       ? assignment.totalMarks
  393:       : assignment.questionSetSnapshot
  394:           .totalMarks;
  395: 
  396:   const marked =
  397:     submissions.filter(
  398:       (submission) =>
  399:         submission.status === "marked",
  400:     );
  401: 
  402:   const provisionalOutcomes =
  403:     marked.map((submission) => {
  404:       const score =
  405:         scoreForSubmission(
  406:           submission,
  407:           assignment,
  408:         );
  409: 
  410:       const grade =
  411:         gradeFromPercentage(
  412:           score.percentage,
  413:           boundarySet,
  414:           qualification,
  415:         );
  416: 
  417:       const next =
  418:         nextGradeInformation({
  419:           grade,
  420:           awardedMarks:
  421:             score.awardedMarks,
  422:           totalMarks:
  423:             score.availableMarks,
  424:           boundarySet,
  425:           qualification,
  426:         });
  427: 
  428:       return {
  429:         submission,
  430:         score,
  431:         grade,
  432:         next,
  433:       };
  434:     });
  435: 
  436:   const classAveragePercentage =
  437:     provisionalOutcomes.length
  438:       ? round(
  439:           provisionalOutcomes.reduce(
  440:             (sum, item) =>
  441:               sum +
  442:               item.score.percentage,
  443:             0,
  444:           ) /
  445:             provisionalOutcomes.length,
  446:         )
  447:       : null;
  448: 
  449:   const classAverageMark =
  450:     provisionalOutcomes.length
  451:       ? round(
  452:           provisionalOutcomes.reduce(
  453:             (sum, item) =>
  454:               sum +
  455:               item.score.awardedMarks,
  456:             0,
  457:           ) /
  458:             provisionalOutcomes.length,
  459:         )
  460:       : null;
  461: 
  462:   const studentOutcomes: ExamStudentGradeOutcome[] =
  463:     provisionalOutcomes.map(
  464:       ({
  465:         submission,
  466:         score,
  467:         grade,
  468:         next,
  469:       }) => ({
  470:         studentId:
  471:           submission.studentId,
  472:         studentName:
  473:           submission.studentName,
  474:         studentEmail:
  475:           submission.studentEmail,
  476:         awardedMarks:
  477:           score.awardedMarks,
  478:         availableMarks:
  479:           score.availableMarks,
  480:         percentage:
  481:           score.percentage,
  482:         grade,
  483:         nextGrade:
  484:           next.nextGrade,
  485:         nextGradeMinimumMark:
  486:           next.nextGradeMinimumMark,
  487:         marksToNextGrade:
  488:           next.marksToNextGrade,
  489:         percentagePointsToNextGrade:
  490:           next.percentagePointsToNextGrade,
  491:         differenceFromClassAverage:
  492:           classAveragePercentage === null
  493:             ? null
  494:             : round(
  495:                 score.percentage -
  496:                   classAveragePercentage,
  497:               ),
  498:         nearNextGradeBoundary:
  499:           next.marksToNextGrade !== null &&
  500:           next.marksToNextGrade > 0 &&
  501:           next.marksToNextGrade <= 2,
  502:       }),
  503:     );
  504: 
  505:   const classAverageGrade =
  506:     classAveragePercentage === null
  507:       ? null
  508:       : gradeFromPercentage(
  509:           classAveragePercentage,
  510:           boundarySet,
  511:           qualification,
  512:         );
  513: 
  514:   const classNext =
  515:     classAverageGrade === null ||
  516:     classAverageMark === null
  517:       ? {
  518:           nextGrade: null,
  519:           nextGradeMinimumMark: null,
  520:           marksToNextGrade: null,
  521:           percentagePointsToNextGrade: null,
  522:         }
  523:       : nextGradeInformation({
  524:           grade: classAverageGrade,
  525:           awardedMarks:
  526:             Math.round(
  527:               classAverageMark,
  528:             ),
  529:           totalMarks,
  530:           boundarySet,
  531:           qualification,
  532:         });
  533: 
  534:   const gradeOrder =
  535:     getGradeOrder(
  536:       qualification,
  537:     ).reverse();
  538: 
  539:   const gradeDistribution: ExamGradeDistributionItem[] =
  540:     gradeOrder
  541:       .map((grade) => {
  542:         const count =
  543:           studentOutcomes.filter(
  544:             (student) =>
  545:               student.grade === grade,
  546:           ).length;
  547: 
  548:         return {
  549:           grade,
  550:           count,
  551:           percentage:
  552:             studentOutcomes.length > 0
  553:               ? round(
  554:                   (count /
  555:                     studentOutcomes.length) *
  556:                     100,
  557:                 )
  558:               : 0,
  559:         };
  560:       })
  561:       .filter(
  562:         (item) =>
  563:           item.count > 0,
  564:       );
  565: 
  566:   const boundaries: ExamGradeBoundaryRow[] =
  567:     orderedBoundaries(
  568:       boundarySet,
  569:       qualification,
  570:     ).map((boundary) => ({
  571:       grade: boundary.grade,
  572:       minimumPercentage:
  573:         round(boundary.minimumPercentage),
  574:       minimumMark:
  575:         Math.ceil(
  576:           (boundary.minimumPercentage /
  577:             100) *
  578:             totalMarks -
  579:             1e-9,
  580:         ),
  581:     }));
  582: 
  583:   return {
  584:     qualification,
  585:     examBoard:
  586:       assignment.questionSetSnapshot
  587:         .examBoard || null,
  588: 
  589:     boundarySetId:
  590:       boundarySet.id,
  591:     boundarySetTitle:
  592:       boundarySet.title,
  593:     boundarySource:
  594:       boundarySet.source,
  595:     boundarySourceNote:
  596:       boundarySet.sourceNote || null,
  597:     boundaryVerifiedAt:
  598:       boundarySet.verifiedAt || null,
  599:     boundaryAcademicYear:
  600:       boundarySet.academicYear ||
  601:       null,
  602:     boundaryAssessmentTitle:
  603:       boundarySet.assessmentTitle ||
  604:       null,
  605:     isOfficialBoundarySet:
  606:       boundarySet.source === "official",
  607: 
  608:     totalMarks,
  609:     boundaries,
  610: 
  611:     classAverageMark,
  612:     classAveragePercentage,
  613:     classAverageGrade,
  614:     classNextGrade:
  615:       classNext.nextGrade,
  616:     classNextGradeMinimumMark:
  617:       classNext.nextGradeMinimumMark,
  618:     classMarksToNextGrade:
  619:       classNext.marksToNextGrade,
  620:     classPercentagePointsToNextGrade:
  621:       classNext.percentagePointsToNextGrade,
  622: 
  623:     studentOutcomes,
  624:     nearBoundaryStudents:
  625:       studentOutcomes.filter(
  626:         (student) =>
  627:           student.nearNextGradeBoundary,
  628:       ),
  629:     gradeDistribution,
  630:   };
  631: }

   10:   ExamAnalysisConfidence,

   20: const INCIDENT_TYPES: ExamIntegrityIncidentType[] = [
   21:   "fullscreen_exit",
   22:   "fullscreen_restored",
   23:   "page_hidden",
   24:   "page_visible",
   25:   "integrity_termination",

   27: 
   28: function round(value: number): number {
   29:   return Math.round(value * 10) / 10;
   30: }
   31: 
   32: function round2(value: number): number {
   33:   return Math.round(value * 100) / 100;
   34: }
   35: 
   36: function median(
   37:   values: number[],
   38: ): number | null {
   39:   if (!values.length) return null;
   40: 
   41:   const sorted = [...values].sort(
   42:     (a, b) => a - b,
   43:   );
   44: 
   45:   const middle =
   46:     Math.floor(sorted.length / 2);
   47: 
   48:   return sorted.length % 2 === 0
   49:     ? round(
   50:         (sorted[middle - 1] +
   51:           sorted[middle]) /
   52:           2,
   53:       )
   54:     : sorted[middle];
   55: }
   56: 
   57: function questionRecord(
   58:   question: unknown,
   59: ): Record<string, unknown> {
   60:   return question &&
   61:     typeof question === "object"
   62:     ? (question as Record<string, unknown>)
   63:     : {};
   64: }
   65: 
   66: function questionTopic(
   67:   question: unknown,
   68:   fallback: string,
   69: ): string {
   70:   const data = questionRecord(question);
   71: 
   72:   for (const value of [
   73:     data.topic,
   74:     data.topicFocus,
   75:     data.subtopic,
   76:     data.curriculumTopic,
   77:   ]) {
   78:     if (
   79:       typeof value === "string" &&
   80:       value.trim()
   81:     ) {
   82:       return value.trim();
   83:     }
   84:   }
   85: 
   86:   return fallback;
   87: }
   88: 
   89: function assessmentObjective(
   90:   question: unknown,
   91: ): "AO1" | "AO2" | "AO3" | null {
   92:   const value =
   93:     questionRecord(
   94:       question,
   95:     ).assessmentObjective;
   96: 
   97:   return value === "AO1" ||
   98:     value === "AO2" ||
   99:     value === "AO3"
  100:     ? value
  101:     : null;
  102: }
  103: 
  104: function commandWord(
  105:   question: unknown,
