  107:   const value =
  108:     questionRecord(
  109:       question,
  110:     ).commandWord;
  111: 
  112:   return typeof value === "string" &&
  113:     value.trim()
  114:     ? value.trim()
  115:     : null;
  116: }
  117: 
  118: function priorityFromPercentage(
  119:   successPercentage: number | null,
  120: ): "high" | "medium" | "low" {
  121:   if (successPercentage === null) return "low";
  122:   if (successPercentage < 50) return "high";
  123:   if (successPercentage < 70) return "medium";
  124:   return "low";
  125: }
  126: 
  127: function questionDifficulty(
  128:   successPercentage: number | null,
  129:   markedStudents: number,
  130: ): ExamQuestionDifficulty {
  131:   if (
  132:     markedStudents === 0 ||
  133:     successPercentage === null
  134:   ) {
  135:     return "insufficient";
  136:   }
  137: 
  138:   if (successPercentage >= 70) return "secure";
  139:   if (successPercentage >= 50) return "developing";
  140:   return "priority";
  141: }
  142: 
  143: function discriminationLabel(
  144:   value: number | null,
  145: ): ExamDiscriminationLabel {
  146:   if (value === null) return "insufficient";
  147:   if (value < 0) return "negative";
  148:   if (value < 0.2) return "weak";
  149:   if (value < 0.3) return "moderate";
  150:   return "strong";
  151: }
  152: 
  153: function questionDiscrimination({
  154:   questionId,
  155:   availableMarks,
  156:   marked,
  157: }: {
  158:   questionId: string;
  159:   availableMarks: number;
  160:   marked: ExamSubmission[];
  161: }): number | null {
  162:   /*
  163:    * Small cohorts produce unstable discrimination statistics.
  164:    * Wait until at least 10 marked submissions are available.
  165:    */
  166:   if (
  167:     marked.length < 10 ||
  168:     availableMarks <= 0
  169:   ) {
  170:     return null;
  171:   }
  172: 
  173:   const sorted = [...marked].sort(
  174:     (first, second) =>
  175:       second.percentage -
  176:       first.percentage,
  177:   );
  178: 
  179:   const groupSize =
  180:     Math.max(
  181:       2,
  182:       Math.floor(
  183:         sorted.length * 0.27,
  184:       ),
  185:     );
  186: 
  187:   const top = sorted.slice(
  188:     0,
  189:     groupSize,
  190:   );
  191: 
  192:   const bottom = sorted.slice(
  193:     -groupSize,
  194:   );
  195: 
  196:   function groupFacility(
  197:     group: ExamSubmission[],
  198:   ): number {
  199:     const awarded =
  200:       group.reduce(
  201:         (sum, submission) => {
  202:           const answer =
  203:             submission.answers.find(
  204:               (item) =>
  205:                 item.questionId ===
  206:                 questionId,
  207:             );
  208: 
  209:           return (
  210:             sum +
  211:             (answer?.awardedMarks ?? 0)
  212:           );
  213:         },
  214:         0,
  215:       );
  216: 
  217:     return (
  218:       awarded /
  219:       (group.length *
  220:         availableMarks)
  221:     );
  222:   }
  223: 
  224:   return round2(
  225:     groupFacility(top) -
  226:       groupFacility(bottom),
  227:   );
  228: }
  229: 
  230: function analysisConfidence(
  231:   markedCount: number,
  232: ): {
  233:   confidence: ExamAnalysisConfidence;
  234:   warnings: string[];
  235: } {
  236:   if (markedCount === 0) {
  237:     return {
  238:       confidence: "insufficient",
  239:       warnings: [
  240:         "No marked submissions are available, so class-level QLA interpretation is not yet possible.",
  241:       ],
  242:     };
  243:   }
  244: 
  245:   if (markedCount < 5) {
  246:     return {
  247:       confidence: "limited",
  248:       warnings: [
  249:         `Only ${markedCount} marked submission${markedCount === 1 ? " is" : "s are"} available. Treat class-level percentages and priorities cautiously.`,
  250:         "Question discrimination is withheld until at least 10 marked submissions are available.",
  251:       ],
  252:     };
  253:   }
  254: 
  255:   if (markedCount < 10) {
  256:     return {
  257:       confidence: "developing",
  258:       warnings: [
  259:         `${markedCount} marked submissions provide useful early class evidence, but question discrimination is withheld until the sample reaches 10.`,
  260:       ],
  261:     };
  262:   }
  263: 
  264:   return {
  265:     confidence: "robust",
  266:     warnings: [],
  267:   };
  268: }
  269: 
  270: type StudentExamWeakness = {
  271:   topic: string | null;
  272:   questionNumber: number | null;
  273:   successPercentage: number | null;
  274: };
  275: 
  276: function getStudentExamWeakness(
  277:   assignment: ExamAssignment,
  278:   submission: ExamSubmission,
  279:   fallbackTopic: string,
  280: ): StudentExamWeakness {
  281:   if (
  282:     submission.status !== "marked"
  283:   ) {
  284:     return {
  285:       topic: null,
  286:       questionNumber: null,
  287:       successPercentage: null,
  288:     };
  289:   }
  290: 
  291:   const scored =
  292:     assignment.questionSetSnapshot
  293:       .questions
  294:       .map((question) => {
  295:         const answer =
  296:           submission.answers.find(
  297:             (item) =>
  298:               item.questionId ===
  299:               question.id,
  300:           );
  301: 
  302:         if (
  303:           !answer ||
  304:           answer.awardedMarks === null ||
  305:           question.marks <= 0
  306:         ) {
  307:           return null;
  308:         }
  309: 
  310:         return {
  311:           topic: questionTopic(
  312:             question,
  313:             fallbackTopic,
  314:           ),
  315:           questionNumber:
  316:             question.questionNumber,
  317:           successPercentage: round(
  318:             (answer.awardedMarks /
  319:               question.marks) *
  320:               100,
  321:           ),
  322:         };
  323:       })
  324:       .filter(
  325:         (
  326:           value,
  327:         ): value is NonNullable<
  328:           typeof value
  329:         > => Boolean(value),
  330:       )
  331:       .sort(
  332:         (first, second) =>
  333:           first.successPercentage -
  334:           second.successPercentage,
  335:       );
  336: 
  337:   const weakest = scored[0];
  338: 
  339:   if (!weakest) {
  340:     return {
  341:       topic: null,
  342:       questionNumber: null,
  343:       successPercentage: null,
  344:     };
  345:   }
  346: 
  347:   return {
  348:     topic: weakest.topic,
  349:     questionNumber:
  350:       weakest.questionNumber,
  351:     successPercentage:
  352:       weakest.successPercentage,
  353:   };
  354: }
  355: 
  356: function buildStudentPriority(
  357:   assignment: ExamAssignment,
  358:   submission: ExamSubmission,
  359:   classAverage: number | null,
  360:   fallbackTopic: string,
  361: ): ExamStudentPriority {
  362:   const reasons: string[] = [];
  363:   let priority: ExamStudentPriority["priority"] =
  364:     "none";
  365: 
  366:   if (submission.integrityTerminated) {
  367:     priority = "high";
  368:     reasons.push(
  369:       "The attempt was automatically submitted by an integrity rule.",
  370:     );
  371:   }
  372: 
  373:   if (
  374:     submission.integrityIncidents.length >= 3
  375:   ) {
  376:     if (priority !== "high") {
  377:       priority = "medium";
  378:     }
  379: 
  380:     reasons.push(
  381:       `${submission.integrityIncidents.length} integrity-monitoring events were recorded for teacher review.`,
  382:     );
  383:   } else if (
  384:     submission.integrityIncidents.length > 0 &&
  385:     priority === "none"
  386:   ) {
  387:     priority = "monitor";
  388: 
  389:     reasons.push(
  390:       `${submission.integrityIncidents.length} integrity-monitoring event${submission.integrityIncidents.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} recorded.`,
  391:     );
  392:   }
  393: 
  394:   const percentage =
  395:     submission.status === "marked"
  396:       ? submission.percentage
  397:       : null;
  398: 
  399:   if (
  400:     percentage !== null &&
  401:     percentage < 40
  402:   ) {
  403:     priority = "high";
  404: 
  405:     reasons.push(
  406:       `Marked result is ${percentage}%, below the 40% intervention threshold.`,
  407:     );
  408:   } else if (
  409:     percentage !== null &&
  410:     classAverage !== null &&
  411:     percentage <=
  412:       classAverage - 15
  413:   ) {
  414:     if (priority !== "high") {
  415:       priority = "medium";
  416:     }
  417: 
  418:     reasons.push(
  419:       `Result is ${round(classAverage - percentage)} percentage points below the current class average.`,
  420:     );
  421:   }
  422: 
  423:   if (
  424:     [
  425:       "submitted",
  426:       "marking",
  427:     ].includes(
  428:       submission.status,
  429:     ) &&
  430:     priority === "none"
  431:   ) {
  432:     priority = "monitor";
  433: 
  434:     reasons.push(
  435:       "The paper is submitted but not yet fully marked, so attainment evidence is incomplete.",
  436:     );
  437:   }
  438: 
  439:   const weakness =
  440:     getStudentExamWeakness(
  441:       assignment,
  442:       submission,
  443:       fallbackTopic,
  444:     );
  445: 
  446:   if (
  447:     weakness.topic &&
  448:     weakness.successPercentage !== null &&
  449:     weakness.successPercentage < 50
  450:   ) {
  451:     if (priority === "none") {
  452:       priority = "monitor";
  453:     }
  454: 
  455:     reasons.push(
  456:       `Weakest marked exam evidence is ${weakness.topic} at ${weakness.successPercentage}% on Q${weakness.questionNumber}.`,
  457:     );
  458:   }
  459: 
  460:   return {
  461:     studentId:
  462:       submission.studentId,
  463:     studentName:
  464:       submission.studentName,
  465:     studentEmail:
  466:       submission.studentEmail,
  467:     status: submission.status,
  468:     percentage,
  469:     integrityIncidentCount:
  470:       submission.integrityIncidents.length,
  471:     integrityTerminated:
  472:       submission.integrityTerminated,
  473:     priority,
  474:     reasons,
  475:     weakestExamTopic:
  476:       weakness.topic,
  477:     weakestQuestionNumber:
  478:       weakness.questionNumber,
  479:     weakestQuestionSuccessPercentage:
  480:       weakness.successPercentage,
  481:   };
  482: }
  483: 
  484: function buildQuestionIntelligence(
  485:   assignment: ExamAssignment,
  486:   marked: ExamSubmission[],
  487:   fallbackTopic: string,
  488: ): ExamQuestionIntelligence[] {
  489:   return assignment.questionSetSnapshot
  490:     .questions.map((question) => {
  491:       let attemptedStudents = 0;
  492:       let omittedStudents = 0;
  493:       let zeroMarkStudents = 0;
  494:       let fullMarkStudents = 0;
  495:       let totalAwardedMarks = 0;
  496: 
  497:       for (const submission of marked) {
  498:         const answer =
  499:           submission.answers.find(
  500:             (item) =>
  501:               item.questionId ===
  502:               question.id,
  503:           );
  504: 
  505:         const response =
  506:           answer?.response?.trim() || "";
  507: 
  508:         if (response) {
  509:           attemptedStudents += 1;
  510:         } else {
  511:           omittedStudents += 1;
  512:         }
  513: 
  514:         const awarded =
  515:           answer?.awardedMarks ?? 0;
  516: 
  517:         totalAwardedMarks += awarded;
  518: 
  519:         if (awarded === 0) {
  520:           zeroMarkStudents += 1;
  521:         }
  522: 
  523:         if (
  524:           question.marks > 0 &&
  525:           awarded >= question.marks
  526:         ) {
  527:           fullMarkStudents += 1;
  528:         }
  529:       }
  530: 
  531:       const markedStudents =
  532:         marked.length;
  533: 
  534:       const totalPossibleClassMarks =
  535:         question.marks *
  536:         markedStudents;
  537: 
  538:       const averageAwardedMarks =
  539:         markedStudents > 0
  540:           ? round(
  541:               totalAwardedMarks /
  542:                 markedStudents,
  543:             )
  544:           : null;
  545: 
  546:       const successPercentage =
  547:         totalPossibleClassMarks > 0
  548:           ? round(
  549:               (totalAwardedMarks /
  550:                 totalPossibleClassMarks) *
  551:                 100,
  552:             )
  553:           : null;
  554: 
  555:       const marksLost =
  556:         Math.max(
  557:           0,
  558:           totalPossibleClassMarks -
  559:             totalAwardedMarks,
  560:         );
  561: 
  562:       const discriminationIndex =
  563:         questionDiscrimination({
  564:           questionId: question.id,
  565:           availableMarks:
  566:             question.marks,
  567:           marked,
  568:         });
  569: 
  570:       return {
  571:         questionId: question.id,
  572:         questionNumber:
  573:           question.questionNumber,
  574:         questionText:
  575:           question.question,
  576:         topic: questionTopic(
  577:           question,
  578:           fallbackTopic,
  579:         ),
  580:         assessmentObjective:
  581:           assessmentObjective(question),
  582:         commandWord:
  583:           commandWord(question),
  584: 
  585:         availableMarks:
  586:           question.marks,
  587:         markedStudents,
  588:         attemptedStudents,
  589:         omittedStudents,
  590:         zeroMarkStudents,
  591:         fullMarkStudents,
  592: 
  593:         totalAwardedMarks,
  594:         totalPossibleClassMarks,
  595:         marksLost,
  596: 
  597:         averageAwardedMarks,
  598:         successPercentage,
  599: 
  600:         attemptPercentage:
  601:           markedStudents > 0
  602:             ? round(
  603:                 (attemptedStudents /
  604:                   markedStudents) *
  605:                   100,
  606:               )
  607:             : null,
  608: 
  609:         omissionPercentage:
  610:           markedStudents > 0
  611:             ? round(
  612:                 (omittedStudents /
  613:                   markedStudents) *
  614:                   100,
  615:               )
  616:             : null,
  617: 
  618:         zeroMarkPercentage:
  619:           markedStudents > 0
  620:             ? round(
  621:                 (zeroMarkStudents /
  622:                   markedStudents) *
  623:                   100,
  624:               )
  625:             : null,
  626: 
  627:         fullMarkPercentage:
  628:           markedStudents > 0
  629:             ? round(
  630:                 (fullMarkStudents /
  631:                   markedStudents) *
  632:                   100,
  633:               )
  634:             : null,
  635: 
  636:         marksLostPercentage:
  637:           totalPossibleClassMarks > 0
  638:             ? round(
  639:                 (marksLost /
  640:                   totalPossibleClassMarks) *
  641:                   100,
  642:               )
  643:             : null,
  644: 
  645:         discriminationIndex,
  646:         discriminationLabel:
  647:           discriminationLabel(
  648:             discriminationIndex,
  649:           ),
  650: 
  651:         difficulty:
  652:           questionDifficulty(
  653:             successPercentage,
  654:             markedStudents,
  655:           ),
  656:       };
  657:     });
  658: }
  659: 
  660: function buildTopicIntelligence(
  661:   questions: ExamQuestionIntelligence[],
  662: ): ExamTopicIntelligence[] {
  663:   const map = new Map<
  664:     string,
  665:     ExamQuestionIntelligence[]
  666:   >();
  667: 
  668:   for (const question of questions) {
  669:     const current =
  670:       map.get(question.topic) || [];
  671: 
  672:     current.push(question);
  673:     map.set(question.topic, current);
  674:   }
  675: 
  676:   return Array.from(map.entries())
  677:     .map(
  678:       (
  679:         [topic, items],
  680:       ): ExamTopicIntelligence => {
  681:         const awardedMarks =
  682:           items.reduce(
  683:             (sum, item) =>
  684:               sum +
  685:               item.totalAwardedMarks,
  686:             0,
  687:           );
  688: 
  689:         const possibleMarks =
  690:           items.reduce(
  691:             (sum, item) =>
  692:               sum +
  693:               item.totalPossibleClassMarks,
  694:             0,
  695:           );
  696: 
  697:         const marksLost =
  698:           Math.max(
  699:             0,
  700:             possibleMarks -
  701:               awardedMarks,
  702:           );
  703: 
  704:         const averageSuccessPercentage =
  705:           possibleMarks > 0
  706:             ? round(
  707:                 (awardedMarks /
  708:                   possibleMarks) *
  709:                   100,
  710:               )
  711:             : null;
  712: 
  713:         return {
  714:           topic,
  715:           questionCount:
  716:             items.length,
  717:           availableMarks:
  718:             items.reduce(
  719:               (sum, item) =>
  720:                 sum +
  721:                 item.availableMarks,
  722:               0,
  723:             ),
  724:           averageSuccessPercentage,
  725:           awardedMarks,
  726:           possibleMarks,
  727:           marksLost,
  728:           marksLostPercentage:
  729:             possibleMarks > 0
  730:               ? round(
  731:                   (marksLost /
  732:                     possibleMarks) *
  733:                     100,
  734:                 )
  735:               : null,
  736:           priority:
  737:             priorityFromPercentage(
  738:               averageSuccessPercentage,
  739:             ),
  740:         };
  741:       },
  742:     )
  743:     .sort((first, second) => {
  744:       if (
  745:         first.averageSuccessPercentage ===
  746:         null
  747:       ) {
  748:         return 1;
  749:       }
  750: 
  751:       if (
  752:         second.averageSuccessPercentage ===
  753:         null
  754:       ) {
  755:         return -1;
  756:       }
  757: 
  758:       return (
  759:         first.averageSuccessPercentage -
  760:         second.averageSuccessPercentage
  761:       );
  762:     });
  763: }
  764: 
  765: function buildAssessmentObjectiveIntelligence(
  766:   questions: ExamQuestionIntelligence[],
  767: ): ExamAssessmentObjectiveIntelligence[] {
  768:   const objectives: (
  769:     | "AO1"
  770:     | "AO2"
  771:     | "AO3"
  772:   )[] = ["AO1", "AO2", "AO3"];
  773: 
  774:   return objectives
  775:     .map((objective) => {
  776:       const items =
  777:         questions.filter(
  778:           (question) =>
  779:             question.assessmentObjective ===
  780:             objective,
  781:         );
  782: 
  783:       if (!items.length) {
  784:         return null;
  785:       }
  786: 
  787:       const awardedMarks =
  788:         items.reduce(
  789:           (sum, item) =>
  790:             sum +
  791:             item.totalAwardedMarks,
  792:           0,
  793:         );
  794: 
  795:       const possibleMarks =
  796:         items.reduce(
  797:           (sum, item) =>
  798:             sum +
  799:             item.totalPossibleClassMarks,
  800:           0,
  801:         );
  802: 
  803:       const marksLost =
  804:         Math.max(
  805:           0,
  806:           possibleMarks -
  807:             awardedMarks,
  808:         );
  809: 
  810:       const averageSuccessPercentage =
  811:         possibleMarks > 0
  812:           ? round(
  813:               (awardedMarks /
  814:                 possibleMarks) *
  815:                 100,
  816:             )
  817:           : null;
  818: 
  819:       return {
  820:         assessmentObjective:
  821:           objective,
  822:         questionCount:
  823:           items.length,
  824:         availableMarks:
  825:           items.reduce(
  826:             (sum, item) =>
  827:               sum +
  828:               item.availableMarks,
  829:             0,
  830:           ),
  831:         awardedMarks,
  832:         possibleMarks,
  833:         averageSuccessPercentage,
  834:         marksLost,
  835:         marksLostPercentage:
  836:           possibleMarks > 0
  837:             ? round(
  838:                 (marksLost /
  839:                   possibleMarks) *
  840:                   100,
  841:               )
  842:             : null,
  843:         priority:
  844:           priorityFromPercentage(
  845:             averageSuccessPercentage,
  846:           ),
  847:       } satisfies ExamAssessmentObjectiveIntelligence;
  848:     })
  849:     .filter(
  850:       (
  851:         value,
  852:       ): value is ExamAssessmentObjectiveIntelligence =>
  853:         Boolean(value),
  854:     )
  855:     .sort((a, b) => {
  856:       if (
  857:         a.averageSuccessPercentage ===
  858:         null
  859:       ) {
  860:         return 1;
  861:       }
  862: 
  863:       if (
  864:         b.averageSuccessPercentage ===
  865:         null
  866:       ) {
  867:         return -1;
  868:       }
  869: 
  870:       return (
  871:         a.averageSuccessPercentage -
  872:         b.averageSuccessPercentage
  873:       );
  874:     });
  875: }
  876: 
  877: export function buildExamClassIntelligence(
  878:   assignment: ExamAssignment,
  879:   submissions: ExamSubmission[],
  880: ): ExamClassIntelligence {
  881:   const submitted =
  882:     submissions.filter(
  883:       (submission) =>
  884:         [
  885:           "submitted",
  886:           "marking",
  887:           "marked",
  888:         ].includes(
  889:           submission.status,
  890:         ),
  891:     );
  892: 
  893:   const marked =
  894:     submissions.filter(
  895:       (submission) =>
  896:         submission.status === "marked",
  897:     );
  898: 
  899:   const started =
  900:     submissions.filter(
  901:       (submission) =>
  902:         submission.status !==
  903:         "not_started",
  904:     );
  905: 
  906:   const markedPercentages =
  907:     marked.map(
  908:       (submission) =>
  909:         submission.percentage,
  910:     );
  911: 
  912:   const classAverage =
  913:     markedPercentages.length > 0
  914:       ? round(
  915:           markedPercentages.reduce(
  916:             (sum, value) =>
  917:               sum + value,
  918:             0,
  919:           ) /
  920:             markedPercentages.length,
  921:         )
  922:       : null;
  923: 
  924:   const highestPercentage =
  925:     markedPercentages.length > 0
  926:       ? Math.max(...markedPercentages)
  927:       : null;
  928: 
  929:   const lowestPercentage =
  930:     markedPercentages.length > 0
  931:       ? Math.min(...markedPercentages)
  932:       : null;
  933: 
  934:   const medianPercentage =
  935:     median(markedPercentages);
  936: 
  937:   const confidence =
  938:     analysisConfidence(
  939:       marked.length,
  940:     );
  941: 
  942:   const fallbackTopic =
  943:     assignment.questionSetSnapshot
  944:       .topic ||
  945:     assignment.questionSetTitle ||
  946:     "General Computer Science";
  947: 
  948:   const questionIntelligence =
  949:     buildQuestionIntelligence(
  950:       assignment,
  951:       marked,
  952:       fallbackTopic,
  953:     );
  954: 
  955:   const topicIntelligence =
  956:     buildTopicIntelligence(
  957:       questionIntelligence,
  958:     );
  959: 
  960:   const assessmentObjectiveIntelligence =
  961:     buildAssessmentObjectiveIntelligence(
  962:       questionIntelligence,
  963:     );
  964: 
  965:   const assessedQuestions =
  966:     questionIntelligence
  967:       .filter(
  968:         (question) =>
  969:           question.successPercentage !==
  970:           null,
  971:       )
  972:       .sort(
  973:         (first, second) =>
  974:           (first.successPercentage || 0) -
  975:           (second.successPercentage || 0),
  976:       );
  977: 
  978:   const integrityByType =
  979:     Object.fromEntries(
  980:       INCIDENT_TYPES.map(
  981:         (type) => [type, 0],
  982:       ),
  983:     ) as Record<
  984:       ExamIntegrityIncidentType,
  985:       number
  986:     >;
  987: 
  988:   let totalIncidents = 0;
  989: 
  990:   for (const submission of submissions) {
  991:     for (
  992:       const incident of
  993:       submission.integrityIncidents
  994:     ) {
  995:       integrityByType[
  996:         incident.type
  997:       ] += 1;
  998: 
  999:       totalIncidents += 1;
 1000:     }
 1001:   }
 1002: 
 1003:   const studentPriorities =
 1004:     submissions
 1005:       .map((submission) =>
 1006:         buildStudentPriority(
 1007:           assignment,
 1008:           submission,
 1009:           classAverage,
 1010:           fallbackTopic,
 1011:         ),
 1012:       )
 1013:       .sort((first, second) => {
 1014:         const order = {
 1015:           high: 0,
 1016:           medium: 1,
 1017:           monitor: 2,
 1018:           none: 3,
 1019:         };
 1020: 
 1021:         return (
 1022:           order[first.priority] -
 1023:           order[second.priority]
 1024:         );
 1025:       });
 1026: 
 1027:   const gradeIntelligence =
 1028:     buildExamGradeIntelligence(
 1029:       assignment,
 1030:       submissions,
 1031:     );
 1032: 
 1033:   const analysisWarnings = [
 1034:     ...confidence.warnings,
 1035:   ];
 1036: 
 1037:   if (
 1038:     gradeIntelligence.boundarySource ===
 1039:     "indicative"
 1040:   ) {
 1041:     analysisWarnings.push(
 1042:       "The assessment grade uses CS Master indicative boundaries, not an official exam-series boundary set.",
 1043:     );
 1044:   }
 1045: 
 1046:   if (
 1047:     questionIntelligence.every(
 1048:       (question) =>
 1049:         question.assessmentObjective ===
 1050:         null,
 1051:     )
 1052:   ) {
 1053:     analysisWarnings.push(
 1054:       "Assessment-objective metadata is unavailable for this paper, so AO analysis cannot be produced.",
 1055:     );
 1056:   }
 1057: 
 1058:   return {
 1059:     studentCount:
 1060:       assignment.studentIds.length,
 1061:     startedCount: started.length,
 1062:     submittedCount:
 1063:       submitted.length,
 1064:     markedCount: marked.length,
 1065: 
 1066:     submissionPercentage:
 1067:       assignment.studentIds.length > 0
 1068:         ? Math.round(
 1069:             (submitted.length /
 1070:               assignment.studentIds.length) *
 1071:               100,
 1072:           )
 1073:         : 0,
 1074: 
 1075:     markingPercentage:
 1076:       submitted.length > 0
 1077:         ? Math.round(
 1078:             (marked.length /
 1079:               submitted.length) *
 1080:               100,
 1081:           )
 1082:         : 0,
 1083: 
 1084:     classAverage,
 1085:     highestPercentage,
 1086:     lowestPercentage,
 1087:     medianPercentage,
 1088: 
 1089:     analysisConfidence:
 1090:       confidence.confidence,
 1091:     analysisWarnings,
 1092: 
 1093:     questionIntelligence,
 1094:     topicIntelligence,
 1095:     assessmentObjectiveIntelligence,
 1096:     studentPriorities,
 1097: 
 1098:     gradeIntelligence,
 1099: 
 1100:     integrity: {
 1101:       cleanSubmissionCount:
 1102:         submitted.filter(
 1103:           (submission) =>
 1104:             submission.integrityIncidents.length === 0 &&
 1105:             !submission.integrityTerminated,
 1106:         ).length,
 1107: 
 1108:       submissionsWithIncidents:
 1109:         submitted.filter(
 1110:           (submission) =>
 1111:             submission.integrityIncidents.length > 0,
 1112:         ).length,
 1113: 
 1114:       integrityTerminatedCount:
 1115:         submitted.filter(
 1116:           (submission) =>
 1117:             submission.integrityTerminated,
 1118:         ).length,
 1119: 
 1120:       totalIncidents,
 1121:       incidentsByType:
 1122:         integrityByType,
 1123:     },
 1124: 
 1125:     hardestQuestion:
 1126:       assessedQuestions[0] || null,
 1127: 
 1128:     easiestQuestion:
 1129:       assessedQuestions.length > 0
 1130:         ? assessedQuestions[
 1131:             assessedQuestions.length - 1
 1132:           ]
 1133:         : null,
 1134: 
 1135:     weakestTopic:
 1136:       topicIntelligence.find(
 1137:         (topic) =>
 1138:           topic.averageSuccessPercentage !==
 1139:           null,
 1140:       ) || null,
 1141: 
 1142:     strongestTopic:
 1143:       [...topicIntelligence]
 1144:         .reverse()
 1145:         .find(
 1146:           (topic) =>
 1147:             topic.averageSuccessPercentage !==
 1148:             null,
 1149:         ) || null,
 1150: 
 1151:     weakestAssessmentObjective:
 1152:       assessmentObjectiveIntelligence.find(
 1153:         (item) =>
 1154:           item.averageSuccessPercentage !==
 1155:           null,
 1156:       ) || null,
 1157: 
 1158:     strongestAssessmentObjective:
 1159:       [
 1160:         ...assessmentObjectiveIntelligence,
 1161:       ]
 1162:         .reverse()
 1163:         .find(
 1164:           (item) =>
 1165:             item.averageSuccessPercentage !==
 1166:             null,
 1167:         ) || null,
 1168:   };
 1169: }

FILE: services\analytics\writtenExamQuestionEvidenceService.ts

    1: import { ANALYTICS_EVIDENCE_WEIGHTS } from "@/data/analytics/analyticsConfig";
    2: import { getStudentExamAssignments } from "@/services/examAssignmentService";
    3: import { getExamSubmission } from "@/services/examSubmissionService";
    4: import type { AnalyticsEvidence } from "@/types/analytics";
    5: 
    6: function safeTopic(question: unknown, fallback: string): string {
    7:   if (!question || typeof question !== "object") return fallback;
    8: 
    9:   const data = question as Record<string, unknown>;
   10:   const candidates = [
   11:     data.topic,
   12:     data.topicFocus,
   13:     data.subtopic,
   14:     data.curriculumTopic,
   15:   ];
   16: 
   17:   for (const candidate of candidates) {
   18:     if (typeof candidate === "string" && candidate.trim()) {
   19:       return candidate.trim();
   20:     }
   21:   }
   22: 
   23:   return fallback;
   24: }
   25: 
   26: function safeMarks(value: unknown): number {
   27:   return typeof value === "number" &&
   28:     Number.isFinite(value) &&
   29:     value > 0
   30:     ? value
   31:     : 0;
   32: }
   33: 
   34: export async function getWrittenExamQuestionEvidence(
   35:   studentId: string,
   36: ): Promise<AnalyticsEvidence[]> {
   37:   const id = studentId.trim();
   38:   if (!id) return [];
   39: 
   40:   const assignments = await getStudentExamAssignments(id);
   41: 
   42:   const submissions = await Promise.all(
   43:     assignments.map((assignment) =>
   44:       getExamSubmission(assignment.id, id).catch(() => null),
   45:     ),
   46:   );
   47: 
   48:   const evidence: AnalyticsEvidence[] = [];
   49: 
   50:   assignments.forEach((assignment, assignmentIndex) => {
   51:     const submission = submissions[assignmentIndex];
   52: 
   53:     if (!submission || submission.status !== "marked") return;
   54: 
   55:     const fallbackTopic =
   56:       assignment.questionSetSnapshot.topic ||
   57:       assignment.questionSetTitle ||
   58:       assignment.title ||
   59:       "General Computer Science";
   60: 
   61:     const paperMarks =
   62:       assignment.totalMarks > 0
   63:         ? assignment.totalMarks
   64:         : submission.totalAvailableMarks;
   65: 
   66:     assignment.questionSetSnapshot.questions.forEach((question) => {
   67:       const answer = submission.answers.find(
   68:         (item) => item.questionId === question.id,
   69:       );
   70: 
   71:       if (!answer || answer.awardedMarks === null) return;
   72: 
   73:       const availableMarks = safeMarks(question.marks);
   74:       if (availableMarks <= 0) return;
   75: 
   76:       const awardedMarks = Math.max(
   77:         0,
   78:         Math.min(availableMarks, answer.awardedMarks),
   79:       );
   80: 
   81:       const weightShare =
   82:         paperMarks > 0
   83:           ? availableMarks / paperMarks
   84:           : 1 / Math.max(1, assignment.questionCount);
   85: 
   86:       evidence.push({
   87:         id: `written-exam-question-${assignment.id}-${question.id}`,
   88:         type: "written_exam",
   89:         title: `${assignment.title} Â· Q${question.questionNumber}`,
   90:         topic: safeTopic(question, fallbackTopic),
   91:         percentage: Math.round(
   92:           (awardedMarks / availableMarks) * 100,
   93:         ),
   94:         rawScore: awardedMarks,
   95:         totalMarks: availableMarks,
   96:         completedAt:
   97:           submission.markedAt || submission.submittedAt || null,
   98:         dueDate: assignment.dueDate,
   99:         weight:
  100:           ANALYTICS_EVIDENCE_WEIGHTS.written_exam * weightShare,
  101:         graded: true,
  102:         sourceAssignmentId: assignment.id,
  103:         sourceAssessmentId: assignment.questionSetId,
  104:         sourceQuestionId: question.id,
  105:         sourceQuestionNumber: question.questionNumber,
  106:         sourceLabel: "Teacher-marked written exam question",
  107:       });
  108:     });
  109:   });
  110: 
  111:   return evidence;
  112: }

   45: function normaliseIntegrityPolicy(
   46:   value?: Partial<ExamIntegrityPolicy> | null,
   47: ): ExamIntegrityPolicy {
   48:   return {
   49:     enabled:
   50:       typeof value?.enabled === "boolean"
   51:         ? value.enabled

   58: 
   59:     /*
   60:      * The five-second fullscreen rule is deliberately fixed.

   72:   };

   75: function convertAssignment(
   76:   id: string,
   77:   data: FirestoreExamAssignment,
   78: ): ExamAssignment {
   79:   return {
   80:     id,
   81:     teacherId: data.teacherId,
   82:     teacherName: data.teacherName || "Teacher",
   83:     classId: data.classId,
   84:     className: data.className,
   85:     studentIds: data.studentIds || [],
   86:     questionSetId: data.questionSetId,
   87:     questionSetTitle: data.questionSetTitle,
   88:     questionSetSnapshot: data.questionSetSnapshot,
   89:     title: data.title,
   90:     instructions: data.instructions || "",
   91:     dueDate: toDate(data.dueDate),
   92:     status: data.status || "active",
   93:     totalMarks: data.totalMarks || 0,
   94:     questionCount: data.questionCount || 0,
   95:     submittedCount: data.submittedCount || 0,
   96:     markedCount: data.markedCount || 0,
   97:     integrityPolicy: normaliseIntegrityPolicy(
   98:       data.integrityPolicy,
   99:     ),
  100:     createdAt: toDate(data.createdAt),
  101:     updatedAt: toDate(data.updatedAt),
  102:   };
  103: }
  104: 
  105: function validateInput(input: CreateExamAssignmentInput) {
  106:   if (!input.teacherId.trim()) {
  107:     throw new Error("A teacher account is required.");
  108:   }
  109: 
  110:   if (!input.classId.trim()) {
  111:     throw new Error("Select a class.");
  112:   }
  113: 
  114:   if (!input.questionSetId.trim()) {
  115:     throw new Error("A question set is required.");
  116:   }
  117: 
  118:   if (!input.title.trim()) {
  119:     throw new Error("Enter an assignment title.");
  120:   }
  121: 
  122:   if (
  123:     !(input.dueDate instanceof Date) ||
  124:     Number.isNaN(input.dueDate.getTime())
  125:   ) {
  126:     throw new Error("Select a valid due date.");
  127:   }
  128: 
  129:   if (input.studentIds.length === 0) {
  130:     throw new Error(
  131:       "The selected class has no enrolled students.",
  132:     );
  133:   }
  134: }
  135: 
  136: function sameCalendarDay(
  137:   first: Date | null,
  138:   second: Date,
  139: ): boolean {
  140:   if (!first) {
  141:     return false;
  142:   }
  143: 
  144:   return (
  145:     first.getFullYear() === second.getFullYear() &&
  146:     first.getMonth() === second.getMonth() &&
  147:     first.getDate() === second.getDate()
  148:   );
  149: }
  150: 
  151: async function findExistingExamAssignment(
  152:   input: CreateExamAssignmentInput,
  153: ): Promise<ExamAssignment | null> {
  154:   const snapshot = await getDocs(
  155:     query(
  156:       collection(db, "examAssignments"),
  157:       where(
  158:         "teacherId",
  159:         "==",
  160:         input.teacherId.trim(),
  161:       ),
  162:     ),
  163:   );
  164: 
  165:   const assignments = snapshot.docs.map(
  166:     (document) =>
  167:       convertAssignment(
  168:         document.id,
  169:         document.data() as FirestoreExamAssignment,
  170:       ),
  171:   );
  172: 
  173:   return (
  174:     assignments.find(
  175:       (assignment) =>
  176:         assignment.status === "active" &&
  177:         assignment.classId === input.classId.trim() &&
  178:         assignment.questionSetId ===
  179:           input.questionSetId.trim() &&
  180:         sameCalendarDay(
  181:           assignment.dueDate,
  182:           input.dueDate,
  183:         ),
  184:     ) || null
  185:   );
  186: }
  187: 
  188: export async function createExamAssignment(
  189:   input: CreateExamAssignmentInput,
  190: ): Promise<string> {
  191:   validateInput(input);
  192: 
  193:   const existing =
  194:     await findExistingExamAssignment(input);
  195: 
  196:   if (existing) {
  197:     throw new Error(
  198:       "This paper is already assigned to that class with the same due date.",
  199:     );
  200:   }
  201: 
  202:   const studentIds = Array.from(
  203:     new Set(
  204:       input.studentIds
  205:         .map((id) => id.trim())
  206:         .filter(Boolean),
  207:     ),
  208:   );
  209: 
  210:   const reference = await addDoc(
  211:     collection(db, "examAssignments"),
  212:     {
  213:       teacherId: input.teacherId.trim(),
  214:       teacherName:
  215:         input.teacherName?.trim() || "Teacher",
  216:       classId: input.classId.trim(),
  217:       className: input.className.trim(),
  218:       studentIds,
  219:       questionSetId: input.questionSetId.trim(),
  220:       questionSetTitle:
  221:         input.questionSetTitle.trim(),
  222:       questionSetSnapshot:
  223:         input.questionSetSnapshot,
  224:       title: input.title.trim(),
  225:       instructions:
  226:         input.instructions?.trim() || "",
  227:       dueDate: Timestamp.fromDate(input.dueDate),
  228:       status: "active",
  229:       totalMarks:
  230:         input.questionSetSnapshot.totalMarks,
  231:       questionCount:
  232:         input.questionSetSnapshot.questionCount,
  233:       submittedCount: 0,
  234:       markedCount: 0,
  235:       integrityPolicy:
  236:         normaliseIntegrityPolicy(
  237:           input.integrityPolicy,
  238:         ),
  239:       createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
  240:       updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  241:     },
  242:   );
  243: 
  244:   return reference.id;
  245: }
  246: 
  247: export async function getExamAssignmentById(
  248:   assignmentId: string,
  249: ): Promise<ExamAssignment | null> {
  250:   if (!assignmentId.trim()) {
  251:     return null;
  252:   }
  253: 
  254:   const snapshot = await getDoc(
  255:     doc(
  256:       db,
  257:       "examAssignments",
  258:       assignmentId,
  259:     ),
  260:   );
  261: 
  262:   if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  263:     return null;
  264:   }
  265: 
  266:   return convertAssignment(
  267:     snapshot.id,
  268:     snapshot.data() as FirestoreExamAssignment,
  269:   );
  270: }
  271: 
  272: export async function getTeacherExamAssignments(
  273:   teacherId: string,
  274: ): Promise<ExamAssignment[]> {
  275:   if (!teacherId.trim()) {
  276:     return [];
  277:   }
  278: 
  279:   const snapshot = await getDocs(
  280:     query(
  281:       collection(db, "examAssignments"),
  282:       where("teacherId", "==", teacherId),
  283:       orderBy("createdAt", "desc"),
  284:     ),
  285:   );
  286: 
  287:   return snapshot.docs.map(
  288:     (document) =>
  289:       convertAssignment(
  290:         document.id,
  291:         document.data() as FirestoreExamAssignment,
  292:       ),
  293:   );
  294: }
  295: 
  296: export async function getStudentExamAssignments(
  297:   studentId: string,
  298: ): Promise<ExamAssignment[]> {
  299:   if (!studentId.trim()) {
  300:     return [];
  301:   }
  302: 
  303:   const snapshot = await getDocs(
  304:     query(
  305:       collection(db, "examAssignments"),
  306:       where(
  307:         "studentIds",
  308:         "array-contains",
  309:         studentId,
  310:       ),
  311:       orderBy("dueDate", "asc"),
  312:     ),
  313:   );
  314: 
  315:   return snapshot.docs.map(
  316:     (document) =>
  317:       convertAssignment(
  318:         document.id,
  319:         document.data() as FirestoreExamAssignment,
  320:       ),
  321:   );
  322: }
  323: 
  324: export async function updateExamAssignmentStatus(
  325:   assignmentId: string,
  326:   status: ExamAssignmentStatus,
  327: ): Promise<void> {
  328:   await updateDoc(
  329:     doc(
  330:       db,
  331:       "examAssignments",
  332:       assignmentId,
  333:     ),
  334:     {
  335:       status,
  336:       updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  337:     },
  338:   );
  339: }
  340: 
  341: export async function updateExamIntegrityPolicy({
  342:   assignmentId,
  343:   teacherId,
  344:   policy,
  345: }: {
  346:   assignmentId: string;
  347:   teacherId: string;
  348:   policy: ExamIntegrityPolicy;
  349: }): Promise<ExamIntegrityPolicy> {
  350:   const reference = doc(
  351:     db,
  352:     "examAssignments",
  353:     assignmentId,
  354:   );
  355: 
  356:   const snapshot = await getDoc(reference);
  357: 
  358:   if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  359:     throw new Error(
  360:       "Exam assignment not found.",
  361:     );
  362:   }
  363: 
  364:   if (
  365:     snapshot.data().teacherId !==
  366:     teacherId.trim()
  367:   ) {
  368:     throw new Error(
  369:       "You do not have permission to change this exam.",
  370:     );
  371:   }
  372: 
  373:   const cleaned =
  374:     normaliseIntegrityPolicy(policy);
  375: 
  376:   await updateDoc(reference, {
  377:     integrityPolicy: cleaned,
  378:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  379:   });
  380: 
  381:   return cleaned;
  382: }
  383: 
  384: export async function deleteExamAssignment(
  385:   assignmentId: string,
  386: ): Promise<void> {
  387:   await deleteDoc(
  388:     doc(
  389:       db,
  390:       "examAssignments",
  391:       assignmentId,
  392:     ),
  393:   );
  394: }

   19: export type SavedExamQuestionSet = {
   20:   id: string;
   21:   teacherId: string;
   22:   title: string;
   23:   topic: string;
   24:   qualification: string;
   25:   examBoard: string;
   26:   difficulty: string;
   27:   questionCount: number;
   28:   totalMarks: number;
   29:   content: GeneratedExamQuestionSet;
   30:   status: "draft" | "published" | "archived";
   31:   createdAt: Date | null;
   32:   updatedAt: Date | null;
   33: };
   34: 
   35: type FirestoreExamQuestionSet = Omit<
   36:   SavedExamQuestionSet,
   37:   "id" | "createdAt" | "updatedAt"
   38: > & {
   39:   createdAt?: Timestamp;
   40:   updatedAt?: Timestamp;
   41: };
   42: 
   43: const COLLECTION_NAME = "examQuestionSets";
   44: 
   45: function timestampToDate(value: unknown): Date | null {
   46:   return value instanceof Timestamp ? value.toDate() : null;
   47: }
   48: 
   49: export async function saveExamQuestionSet(
   50:   teacherId: string,
   51:   questionSet: GeneratedExamQuestionSet,
   52: ): Promise<string> {
   53:   if (!teacherId.trim()) {
   54:     throw new Error("A signed-in teacher is required.");
   55:   }
   56: 
   57:   const documentReference = await addDoc(collection(db, COLLECTION_NAME), {
   58:     teacherId,
   59:     title: questionSet.title,
   60:     topic: questionSet.topic,
   61:     qualification: questionSet.qualification,
   62:     examBoard: questionSet.examBoard,
   63:     difficulty: questionSet.difficulty,
   64:     questionCount: questionSet.questionCount,
   65:     totalMarks: questionSet.totalMarks,
   66:     content: questionSet,
   67:     status: "draft",
   68:     createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
   69:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
   70:   });
   71: 
   72:   return documentReference.id;
   73: }
   74: 
   75: export async function getTeacherExamQuestionSets(
   76:   teacherId: string,
   77: ): Promise<SavedExamQuestionSet[]> {
   78:   if (!teacherId.trim()) {
   79:     return [];
   80:   }
   81: 
   82:   const snapshot = await getDocs(
   83:     query(
   84:       collection(db, COLLECTION_NAME),
   85:       where("teacherId", "==", teacherId),
   86:       orderBy("createdAt", "desc"),
   87:     ),
   88:   );
   89: 
   90:   return snapshot.docs.map((questionSetDocument) => {
   91:     const data = questionSetDocument.data() as FirestoreExamQuestionSet;
   92: 
   93:     return {
   94:       id: questionSetDocument.id,
   95:       teacherId: data.teacherId,
   96:       title: data.title,
   97:       topic: data.topic,
   98:       qualification: data.qualification,
   99:       examBoard: data.examBoard,
  100:       difficulty: data.difficulty,
  101:       questionCount: data.questionCount,
  102:       totalMarks: data.totalMarks,
  103:       content: data.content,
  104:       status: data.status,
  105:       createdAt: timestampToDate(data.createdAt),
  106:       updatedAt: timestampToDate(data.updatedAt),
  107:     };
  108:   });
  109: }
  110: 
  111: export async function getExamQuestionSetById(
  112:   questionSetId: string,
  113: ): Promise<SavedExamQuestionSet | null> {
  114:   if (!questionSetId.trim()) {
  115:     return null;
  116:   }
  117: 
  118:   const snapshot = await getDoc(doc(db, COLLECTION_NAME, questionSetId));
  119: 
  120:   if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  121:     return null;
  122:   }
  123: 
  124:   const data = snapshot.data() as FirestoreExamQuestionSet;
  125: 
  126:   return {
  127:     id: snapshot.id,
  128:     teacherId: data.teacherId,
  129:     title: data.title,
  130:     topic: data.topic,
  131:     qualification: data.qualification,
  132:     examBoard: data.examBoard,
  133:     difficulty: data.difficulty,
  134:     questionCount: data.questionCount,
  135:     totalMarks: data.totalMarks,
  136:     content: data.content,
  137:     status: data.status,
  138:     createdAt: timestampToDate(data.createdAt),
  139:     updatedAt: timestampToDate(data.updatedAt),
  140:   };
  141: }
  142: 
  143: export async function updateExamQuestionSetStatus(
  144:   questionSetId: string,
  145:   status: "draft" | "published" | "archived",
  146: ): Promise<void> {
  147:   await updateDoc(doc(db, COLLECTION_NAME, questionSetId), {
  148:     status,
  149:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  150:   });
  151: }
  152: 
  153: export async function duplicateExamQuestionSet(
  154:   questionSetId: string,
  155:   teacherId: string,
  156: ): Promise<string> {
  157:   const source = await getExamQuestionSetById(questionSetId);
  158: 
  159:   if (!source) {
  160:     throw new Error("The question set could not be found.");
  161:   }
  162: 
  163:   if (source.teacherId !== teacherId.trim()) {
  164:     throw new Error("You cannot duplicate another teacher's question set.");
  165:   }
  166: 
  167:   const reference = await addDoc(collection(db, COLLECTION_NAME), {
  168:     teacherId: source.teacherId,
  169:     title: `${source.title} (Copy)`,
  170:     topic: source.topic,
  171:     qualification: source.qualification,
  172:     examBoard: source.examBoard,
  173:     difficulty: source.difficulty,
  174:     questionCount: source.questionCount,
  175:     totalMarks: source.totalMarks,
  176:     content: { ...source.content, title: `${source.title} (Copy)` },
  177:     status: "draft",
  178:     createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
  179:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  180:   });
  181: 
  182:   return reference.id;
  183: }
  184: 
  185: export async function deleteExamQuestionSet(
  186:   questionSetId: string,
  187: ): Promise<void> {
  188:   await deleteDoc(doc(db, COLLECTION_NAME, questionSetId));
  189: }

    1: import {

   18: 
   19: type FirestoreIntegrityIncident = Omit<
   20:   ExamIntegrityIncident,
   21:   "occurredAt"
   22: > & {
   23:   occurredAt?: Timestamp;
   24: };
   25: 
   26: type FirestoreExamSubmission = Omit<
   27:   ExamSubmission,
   28:   | "id"
   29:   | "startedAt"
   30:   | "submittedAt"
   31:   | "markedAt"
   32:   | "updatedAt"

   44: function toDate(

   48:     ? value.toDate()
   49:     : null;
   50: }

   52: function convertIncident(
   53:   value: FirestoreIntegrityIncident,
   54: ): ExamIntegrityIncident {
   55:   return {
   56:     id: value.id,
   57:     type: value.type,
   58:     occurredAt: toDate(value.occurredAt),
   59:     questionNumber:
   60:       typeof value.questionNumber ===
   61:       "number"
   62:         ? value.questionNumber
   63:         : null,

   65:   };
   66: }

   68: export function createSubmissionId(
   69:   assignmentId: string,
   70:   studentId: string,

   72:   return `${assignmentId}_${studentId}`;
   73: }
   74: 
   75: function createAnswers(
   76:   assignment: ExamAssignment,
   77: ): StudentExamAnswer[] {
   78:   return assignment.questionSetSnapshot.questions.map(
   79:     (question) => ({
   80:       questionId: question.id,
   81:       questionNumber:
   82:         question.questionNumber,
   83:       response: "",
   84:       awardedMarks: null,

   90: function convertSubmission(
   91:   id: string,
   92:   data: FirestoreExamSubmission,
   93: ): ExamSubmission {
   94:   return {
   95:     id,
   96:     assignmentId: data.assignmentId,
   97:     studentId: data.studentId,
   98:     studentName:
   99:       data.studentName || "Student",
  100:     studentEmail:
  101:       data.studentEmail || "",
  102:     teacherId: data.teacherId,
  103:     classId: data.classId,
  104:     status:
  105:       data.status || "not_started",
  106:     answers: data.answers || [],
  107:     totalAwardedMarks:
  108:       data.totalAwardedMarks || 0,
  109:     totalAvailableMarks:
  110:       data.totalAvailableMarks || 0,
  111:     percentage: data.percentage || 0,
  112:     overallFeedback:
  113:       data.overallFeedback || "",
  114: 
  115:     integrityPolicySnapshot:
  116:       data.integrityPolicySnapshot || null,
  117: 
  118:     integrityIncidents:
  119:       Array.isArray(
  120:         data.integrityIncidents,
  121:       )
  122:         ? data.integrityIncidents.map(
  123:             convertIncident,
  124:           )
  125:         : [],
  126: 
  127:     integrityTerminated:
  128:       data.integrityTerminated === true,
  129: 
  130:     integrityTerminationReason:
  131:       data.integrityTerminationReason || "",
  132: 
  133:     integritySessionStartedAt:
  134:       toDate(
  135:         data.integritySessionStartedAt,
  136:       ),
  137: 
  138:     integrityLastQuestionNumber:
  139:       typeof data.integrityLastQuestionNumber ===
  140:       "number"
  141:         ? data.integrityLastQuestionNumber
  142:         : null,
  143: 
  144:     startedAt: toDate(data.startedAt),
  145:     submittedAt:
  146:       toDate(data.submittedAt),
  147:     markedAt: toDate(data.markedAt),
  148:     updatedAt: toDate(data.updatedAt),
  149:   };
  150: }
  151: 
  152: export async function getOrCreateExamSubmission({
  153:   assignment,
  154:   studentId,
  155:   studentName,
  156:   studentEmail,
  157: }: {
  158:   assignment: ExamAssignment;

  163:   const submissionId =

  176:     await getDoc(reference);

  179:     return convertSubmission(

  182:     );
  183:   }
  184: 
  185:   const answers =
  186:     createAnswers(assignment);
  187:

  189:     assignmentId: assignment.id,
  190:     studentId,
  191:     studentName:
  192:       studentName?.trim() || "Student",
  193:     studentEmail:
  194:       studentEmail?.trim() || "",
  195:     teacherId: assignment.teacherId,
  196:     classId: assignment.classId,
  197:     status: "in_progress",
  198:     answers,
  199:     totalAwardedMarks: 0,
  200:     totalAvailableMarks:
  201:       assignment.totalMarks,
  202:     percentage: 0,
  203:     overallFeedback: "",
  204: 
  205:     integrityPolicySnapshot: null,
  206:     integrityIncidents: [],
  207:     integrityTerminated: false,
  208:     integrityTerminationReason: "",
  209:     integritySessionStartedAt: null,
  210:     integrityLastQuestionNumber: null,
  211: 
  212:     startedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  213:     submittedAt: null,
  214:     markedAt: null,
  215:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  216:   });
  217: 
  218:   return {
  219:     id: submissionId,
  220:     assignmentId: assignment.id,
  221:     studentId,
  222:     studentName:
  223:       studentName?.trim() || "Student",
  224:     studentEmail:
  225:       studentEmail?.trim() || "",
  226:     teacherId: assignment.teacherId,
  227:     classId: assignment.classId,
  228:     status: "in_progress",
  229:     answers,
  230:     totalAwardedMarks: 0,
  231:     totalAvailableMarks:
  232:       assignment.totalMarks,
  233:     percentage: 0,
  234:     overallFeedback: "",
  235: 
  236:     integrityPolicySnapshot: null,
  237:     integrityIncidents: [],
  238:     integrityTerminated: false,
  239:     integrityTerminationReason: "",
  240:     integritySessionStartedAt: null,
  241:     integrityLastQuestionNumber: null,
  242: 
  243:     startedAt: new Date(),
  244:     submittedAt: null,
  245:     markedAt: null,
  246:     updatedAt: new Date(),
  247:   };
  248: }
  249: 
  250: export async function getExamSubmission(
  251:   assignmentId: string,
  252:   studentId: string,
  253: ): Promise<ExamSubmission | null> {
  254:   const snapshot = await getDoc(
  255:     doc(
  256:       db,
  257:       "examSubmissions",
  258:       createSubmissionId(
  259:         assignmentId,
  260:         studentId,
  261:       ),
  262:     ),
  263:   );
  264: 
  265:   if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  266:     return null;
  267:   }
  268: 
  269:   return convertSubmission(
  270:     snapshot.id,
  271:     snapshot.data() as FirestoreExamSubmission,
  272:   );
  273: }
  274: 
  275: export async function startExamIntegritySession({
  276:   assignment,
  277:   studentId,
  278: }: {
  279:   assignment: ExamAssignment;
  280:   studentId: string;
  281: }): Promise<void> {
  282:   const reference = doc(
  283:     db,
  284:     "examSubmissions",
  285:     createSubmissionId(
  286:       assignment.id,
  287:       studentId,
  288:     ),
  289:   );
  290: 
  291:   const snapshot =
  292:     await getDoc(reference);
  293: 
  294:   if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  295:     throw new Error(
  296:       "Submission not found.",
  297:     );
  298:   }
  299: 
  300:   const data = snapshot.data();
  301: 
  302:   if (
  303:     ["submitted", "marking", "marked"].includes(
  304:       data.status,
  305:     )
  306:   ) {
  307:     return;
  308:   }
  309: 
  310:   const updates: Record<
  311:     string,
  312:     unknown
  313:   > = {
  314:     integrityPolicySnapshot:
  315:       assignment.integrityPolicy,
  316:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  317:   };
  318: 
  319:   if (
  320:     !data.integritySessionStartedAt
  321:   ) {
  322:     updates.integritySessionStartedAt =
  323:       serverTimestamp();
  324:   }
  325: 
  326:   await updateDoc(
  327:     reference,
  328:     updates,
  329:   );
  330: }

  332: function incidentId(
  333:   type: ExamIntegrityIncidentType,
  334: ): string {
  335:   return `${type}-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()
  336:     .toString(36)
  337:     .slice(2, 8)}`;
  338: }

  340: export async function recordExamIntegrityIncident({
  341:   assignmentId,
  342:   studentId,
  343:   type,
  344:   questionNumber,
  345:   detail,
  346: }: {
  347:   assignmentId: string;
  348:   studentId: string;
  349:   type: ExamIntegrityIncidentType;
  350:   questionNumber: number | null;
  351:   detail: string;
  352: }): Promise<void> {
  353:   const reference = doc(
  354:     db,
  355:     "examSubmissions",
  356:     createSubmissionId(
  357:       assignmentId,
  358:       studentId,
  359:     ),
  360:   );
  361: 
  362:   const incident = {
  363:     id: incidentId(type),
  364:     type,
  365:     occurredAt: Timestamp.now(),
  366:     questionNumber:
  367:       questionNumber ?? null,
  368:     detail: detail.trim(),
  369:   };
  370: 
  371:   await updateDoc(reference, {
  372:     integrityIncidents:
  373:       arrayUnion(incident),
  374:     integrityLastQuestionNumber:
  375:       questionNumber ?? null,
  376:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  377:   });
  378: }

  380: export async function updateExamCurrentQuestion({
  381:   assignmentId,

  384: }: {
  385:   assignmentId: string;

  390:     doc(

  393:       createSubmissionId(
  394:         assignmentId,
  395:         studentId,
  396:       ),

  406: export async function terminateExamForIntegrity({
  407:   assignmentId,
  408:   studentId,
  409:   answers,
  410:   questionNumber,
  411:   reason,
  412: }: {
  413:   assignmentId: string;
  414:   studentId: string;
  415:   answers: StudentExamAnswer[];
  416:   questionNumber: number | null;
  417:   reason: string;
  418: }): Promise<void> {
  419:   const reference = doc(
  420:     db,
  421:     "examSubmissions",
  422:     createSubmissionId(
  423:       assignmentId,
  424:       studentId,
  425:     ),
  426:   );
  427: 
  428:   const terminationIncident = {
  429:     id: incidentId(
  430:       "integrity_termination",
  431:     ),
  432:     type:
  433:       "integrity_termination" as const,
  434:     occurredAt: Timestamp.now(),
  435:     questionNumber:
  436:       questionNumber ?? null,
  437:     detail: reason.trim(),
  438:   };
  439: 
  440:   await runTransaction(
  441:     db,
  442:     async (transaction) => {
  443:       const snapshot =
  444:         await transaction.get(reference);
  445: 
  446:       if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  447:         throw new Error(
  448:           "Submission not found.",
  449:         );
  450:       }
  451: 
  452:       const status =
  453:         snapshot.data().status;
  454: 
  455:       if (
  456:         ["submitted", "marking", "marked"].includes(
  457:           status,
  458:         )
  459:       ) {
  460:         return;
  461:       }
  462: 
  463:       const currentIncidents =
  464:         Array.isArray(
  465:           snapshot.data()
  466:             .integrityIncidents,
  467:         )
  468:           ? snapshot.data()
  469:               .integrityIncidents
  470:           : [];
  471: 
  472:       transaction.update(
  473:         reference,
  474:         {
  475:           answers,
  476:           status: "submitted",
  477:           submittedAt:
  478:             serverTimestamp(),
  479: 
  480:           integrityTerminated: true,
  481:           integrityTerminationReason:
  482:             reason.trim(),
  483: 
  484:           integrityLastQuestionNumber:
  485:             questionNumber ?? null,
  486: 
  487:           integrityIncidents: [
  488:             ...currentIncidents,
  489:             terminationIncident,
  490:           ],
  491: 
  492:           updatedAt:
  493:             serverTimestamp(),
  494:         },
  495:       );
  496:     },
  497:   );
  498: }
  499: 
  500: export async function autosaveExamAnswers(
  501:   assignmentId: string,
  502:   studentId: string,
  503:   answers: StudentExamAnswer[],
  504: ): Promise<void> {
  505:   const reference = doc(
  506:     db,
  507:     "examSubmissions",
  508:     createSubmissionId(
  509:       assignmentId,
  510:       studentId,
  511:     ),
  512:   );
  513: 
  514:   const snapshot =
  515:     await getDoc(reference);
  516: 
  517:   if (
  518:     snapshot.exists() &&
  519:     ["submitted", "marking", "marked"].includes(
  520:       snapshot.data().status,
  521:     )
  522:   ) {
  523:     throw new Error(
  524:       "This submission is locked.",
  525:     );
  526:   }
  527: 
  528:   await updateDoc(reference, {
  529:     answers,
  530:     status: "in_progress",
  531:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  532:   });
  533: }

  535: export async function submitExamSubmission(
  536:   assignmentId: string,
  537:   studentId: string,
  538: ): Promise<void> {
  539:   const submissionReference = doc(
  540:     db,
  541:     "examSubmissions",
  542:     createSubmissionId(
  543:       assignmentId,
  544:       studentId,
  545:     ),
  546:   );
  547: 
  548:   const snapshot =
  549:     await getDoc(
  550:       submissionReference,
  551:     );
  552: 
  553:   if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  554:     throw new Error(
  555:       "Submission not found.",
  556:     );
  557:   }
  558: 
  559:   const status =
  560:     snapshot.data().status;
  561: 
  562:   if (
  563:     status === "submitted" ||
  564:     status === "marking" ||
  565:     status === "marked"
  566:   ) {
  567:     return;
  568:   }
  569: 
  570:   await updateDoc(
  571:     submissionReference,
  572:     {
  573:       status: "submitted",
  574:       submittedAt:
  575:         serverTimestamp(),
  576:       updatedAt:
  577:         serverTimestamp(),
  578:     },
  579:   );
  580: }
  581: 
  582: export async function getAssignmentSubmissions(
  583:   assignmentId: string,
  584:   teacherId: string,
  585: ): Promise<ExamSubmission[]> {
  586:   const cleanedAssignmentId =
  587:     assignmentId.trim();
  588: 
  589:   const cleanedTeacherId =
  590:     teacherId.trim();
  591: 
  592:   if (
  593:     !cleanedAssignmentId ||
  594:     !cleanedTeacherId
  595:   ) {
  596:     return [];
  597:   }
  598: 
  599:   const snapshot = await getDocs(
  600:     query(
  601:       collection(
  602:         db,
  603:         "examSubmissions",
  604:       ),
  605:       where(
  606:         "assignmentId",
  607:         "==",
  608:         cleanedAssignmentId,
  609:       ),
  610:       where(
  611:         "teacherId",
  612:         "==",
  613:         cleanedTeacherId,
  614:       ),
  615:     ),
  616:   );
  617: 
  618:   return snapshot.docs
  619:     .map((document) =>
  620:       convertSubmission(
  621:         document.id,
  622:         document.data() as FirestoreExamSubmission,
  623:       ),
  624:     )
  625:     .sort((first, second) =>
  626:       first.studentName.localeCompare(
  627:         second.studentName,
  628:         "en-GB",
  629:         {
  630:           sensitivity: "base",
  631:         },
  632:       ),
  633:     );
  634: }
  635: 
  636: export async function saveDraftMarking({
  637:   assignmentId,
  638:   studentId,
  639:   answers,
  640:   overallFeedback,
  641: }: {
  642:   assignmentId: string;
  643:   studentId: string;
  644:   answers: StudentExamAnswer[];
  645:   overallFeedback: string;
  646: }): Promise<void> {
  647:   const totalAwardedMarks =
  648:     answers.reduce(
  649:       (sum, answer) =>
  650:         sum +
  651:         (answer.awardedMarks || 0),
  652:       0,
  653:     );
  654: 
  655:   await updateDoc(
  656:     doc(
  657:       db,
  658:       "examSubmissions",
  659:       createSubmissionId(
  660:         assignmentId,
  661:         studentId,
  662:       ),
  663:     ),
  664:     {
  665:       answers,
  666:       overallFeedback:
  667:         overallFeedback.trim(),
  668:       totalAwardedMarks,
  669:       status: "marking",
  670:       updatedAt:
  671:         serverTimestamp(),
  672:     },
  673:   );
  674: }
  675: 
  676: export async function finaliseExamMarking({
  677:   assignmentId,
  678:   studentId,
  679:   answers,
  680:   overallFeedback,
  681:   totalAvailableMarks,
  682: }: {
  683:   assignmentId: string;
  684:   studentId: string;
  685:   answers: StudentExamAnswer[];
  686:   overallFeedback: string;
  687:   totalAvailableMarks: number;
  688: }): Promise<void> {
  689:   const assignmentReference = doc(
  690:     db,
  691:     "examAssignments",
  692:     assignmentId,
  693:   );
  694: 
  695:   const submissionReference = doc(
  696:     db,
  697:     "examSubmissions",
  698:     createSubmissionId(
  699:       assignmentId,
  700:       studentId,
  701:     ),
  702:   );
  703: 
  704:   const totalAwardedMarks =
  705:     answers.reduce(
  706:       (sum, answer) =>
  707:         sum +
  708:         (answer.awardedMarks ?? 0),
  709:       0,
  710:     );
  711: 
  712:   const safeAwardedMarks =
  713:     Math.min(
  714:       totalAvailableMarks,
  715:       Math.max(
  716:         0,
  717:         totalAwardedMarks,
  718:       ),
  719:     );
  720: 
  721:   const percentage =
  722:     totalAvailableMarks > 0
  723:       ? Math.round(
  724:           (safeAwardedMarks /
  725:             totalAvailableMarks) *
  726:             100,
  727:         )
  728:       : 0;
  729: 
  730:   await runTransaction(
  731:     db,
  732:     async (transaction) => {
  733:       const [
  734:         submissionSnapshot,
  735:         assignmentSnapshot,
  736:       ] = await Promise.all([
  737:         transaction.get(
  738:           submissionReference,
  739:         ),
  740:         transaction.get(
  741:           assignmentReference,
  742:         ),
  743:       ]);
  744: 
  745:       if (
  746:         !submissionSnapshot.exists()
  747:       ) {
  748:         throw new Error(
  749:           "Submission not found.",
  750:         );
  751:       }
  752: 
  753:       if (
  754:         !assignmentSnapshot.exists()
  755:       ) {
  756:         throw new Error(
  757:           "Assignment not found.",
  758:         );
  759:       }
  760: 
  761:       const submissionData =
  762:         submissionSnapshot.data();
  763: 
  764:       const assignmentData =
  765:         assignmentSnapshot.data();
  766: 
  767:       const alreadyMarked =
  768:         submissionData.status ===
  769:         "marked";
  770: 
  771:       transaction.update(
  772:         submissionReference,
  773:         {
  774:           answers,
  775:           overallFeedback:
  776:             overallFeedback.trim(),
  777:           totalAwardedMarks:
  778:             safeAwardedMarks,
  779:           totalAvailableMarks,
  780:           percentage,
  781:           status: "marked",
  782:           markedAt:
  783:             serverTimestamp(),
  784:           updatedAt:
  785:             serverTimestamp(),
  786:         },
  787:       );
  788: 
  789:       if (!alreadyMarked) {
  790:         const currentMarkedCount =
  791:           typeof assignmentData.markedCount ===
  792:           "number"
  793:             ? assignmentData.markedCount
  794:             : 0;
  795: 
  796:         const studentCount =
  797:           Array.isArray(
  798:             assignmentData.studentIds,
  799:           )
  800:             ? assignmentData
  801:                 .studentIds.length
  802:             : 0;
  803: 
  804:         transaction.update(
  805:           assignmentReference,
  806:           {
  807:             markedCount:
  808:               Math.min(
  809:                 currentMarkedCount +
  810:                   1,
  811:                 studentCount,
  812:               ),
  813:             updatedAt:
  814:               serverTimestamp(),
  815:           },
  816:         );
  817:       }
  818:     },
  819:   );
  820: }

==============================================================================================================
FILE: services\examTrainerAnalyticsService.ts
==============================================================================================================
    1: import { getExamTrainerHistory } from "@/services/examTrainerAttemptService";
    2: import type {
    3:   ExamTrainerDashboardSummary,
    4:   ExamTrainerHistoryItem,
    5: } from "@/types/examTrainer";
    6: 
    7: function average(values: number[]): number {
    8:   if (values.length === 0) return 0;
    9:   return Math.round(
   10:     values.reduce((total, value) => total + value, 0) / values.length,
   11:   );
   12: }
   13: 
   14: function gradeFromPercentage(value: number): string {
   15:   if (value >= 90) return "9";
   16:   if (value >= 80) return "8";
   17:   if (value >= 70) return "7";
   18:   if (value >= 60) return "6";
   19:   if (value >= 50) return "5";
   20:   if (value >= 40) return "4";
   21:   if (value >= 30) return "3";
   22:   if (value >= 20) return "2";
   23:   return "1";
   24: }
   25: 
   26: function topicFrequency(
   27:   history: ExamTrainerHistoryItem[],
   28:   key: "priorityTopics",
   29: ): Map<string, number> {
   30:   const counts = new Map<string, number>();
   31: 
   32:   history.forEach((attempt) => {
   33:     attempt[key].forEach((topic) => {
   34:       counts.set(topic, (counts.get(topic) ?? 0) + 1);
   35:     });
   36:   });
   37: 
   38:   return counts;
   39: }
   40: 
   41: export async function getExamTrainerDashboardSummary(
   42:   studentId: string,
   43: ): Promise<ExamTrainerDashboardSummary> {
   44:   const history = await getExamTrainerHistory(studentId);
   45:   const percentages = history.map((attempt) => attempt.percentage);
   46:   const recent = percentages.slice(0, 3);
   47:   const previous = percentages.slice(3, 6);
   48: 
   49:   const weakestCounts = topicFrequency(history, "priorityTopics");
   50:   const weakestTopic =
   51:     [...weakestCounts.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0]?.[0] ?? null;
   52: 
   53:   const topicPerformance = new Map<string, number[]>();
   54: 
   55:   history.forEach((attempt) => {
   56:     if (
   57:       attempt.selectedTopic !== "all" &&
   58:       !attempt.priorityTopics.includes(attempt.selectedTopic)
   59:     ) {
   60:       topicPerformance.set(attempt.selectedTopic, [
   61:         ...(topicPerformance.get(attempt.selectedTopic) ?? []),
   62:         attempt.percentage,
   63:       ]);
   64:     }
   65:   });
   66: 
   67:   const strongestTopic =
   68:     [...topicPerformance.entries()]
   69:       .map(([topic, scores]) => ({
   70:         topic,
   71:         average: average(scores),
   72:       }))
   73:       .sort((a, b) => b.average - a.average)[0]?.topic ?? null;
   74: 
   75:   const bestPercentage = percentages.length ? Math.max(...percentages) : 0;
   76: 
   77:   return {
   78:     completedAttempts: history.length,
   79:     averagePercentage: average(percentages),
   80:     bestPercentage,
   81:     bestGrade: gradeFromPercentage(bestPercentage),
   82:     latestPercentage: percentages[0] ?? 0,
   83:     weakestTopic,
   84:     strongestTopic,
   85:     recentTrend: average(recent) - average(previous),
   86:   };
   87: }

  132: export async function getExamTrainerAttempt(
  133:   attemptId: string,
  134: ): Promise<ExamTrainerAttempt | null> {
  135:   const snapshot = await getDoc(attemptReference(attemptId));
  136:   if (!snapshot.exists()) return null;
  137: 
  138:   const data = snapshot.data() as FirestoreAttempt;
  139: 
  140:   return {
  141:     ...data,
  142:     id: snapshot.id,
  143:     startedAt: toDate(data.startedAt) ?? new Date(),
  144:     updatedAt: toDate(data.updatedAt) ?? new Date(),
  145:     submittedAt: toDate(data.submittedAt),
  146:   };
  147: }
  148: 
  149: export async function getLatestInProgressExamTrainerAttempt(
  150:   studentId: string,
  151: ): Promise<ExamTrainerAttempt | null> {
  152:   const snapshot = await getDocs(
  153:     query(
  154:       collection(db, "examTrainerAttempts"),
  155:       where("studentId", "==", studentId),
  156:       where("status", "==", "in_progress"),
  157:       orderBy("updatedAt", "desc"),
  158:     ),
  159:   );

  161:   const first = snapshot.docs[0];
  162:   if (!first) return null;
  163: 
  164:   const data = first.data() as FirestoreAttempt;
  165:

  167:     ...data,
  168:     id: first.id,
  169:     startedAt: toDate(data.startedAt) ?? new Date(),
  170:     updatedAt: toDate(data.updatedAt) ?? new Date(),
  171:     submittedAt: toDate(data.submittedAt),
