==============================================================================================================
FILE: services\examTrainerEvidenceService.ts
==============================================================================================================
    1: import {
    2:   collection,
    3:   getDocs,
    4:   orderBy,
    5:   query,
    6:   Timestamp,
    7: } from "firebase/firestore";
    8: 
    9: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
   10: import type { AdaptiveEvidence } from "@/types/adaptiveLearning";
   11: 
   12: type ExamTrainerResultDocument = {
   13:   attemptId?: string;
   14:   topic?: string;
   15:   scorePercent?: number;
   16:   percentage?: number;
   17:   topicScores?: {
   18:     topic?: string;
   19:     percentage?: number;
   20:   }[];
   21:   completedAt?: Timestamp | null;
   22:   createdAt?: Timestamp | null;
   23: };
   24: 
   25: export async function getExamTrainerAdaptiveEvidence(
   26:   studentId: string,
   27: ): Promise<AdaptiveEvidence[]> {
   28:   const snapshot = await getDocs(
   29:     query(
   30:       collection(db, "users", studentId, "examTrainerResults"),
   31:       orderBy("createdAt", "desc"),
   32:     ),
   33:   );
   34: 
   35:   const evidence: AdaptiveEvidence[] = [];
   36: 
   37:   snapshot.docs.forEach((document) => {
   38:     const data = document.data() as ExamTrainerResultDocument;
   39:     const completedAt =
   40:       data.completedAt instanceof Timestamp
   41:         ? data.completedAt.toDate()
   42:         : data.createdAt instanceof Timestamp
   43:           ? data.createdAt.toDate()
   44:           : null;
   45: 
   46:     if (Array.isArray(data.topicScores) && data.topicScores.length > 0) {
   47:       data.topicScores.forEach((topicScore, index) => {
   48:         if (!topicScore.topic) return;
   49: 
   50:         evidence.push({
   51:           id: `exam-trainer-${document.id}-${index}`,
   52:           topic: topicScore.topic,
   53:           source: "exam",
   54:           score:
   55:             typeof topicScore.percentage === "number"
   56:               ? topicScore.percentage
   57:               : 0,
   58:           completedAt,
   59:           weight: 1.25,
   60:         });
   61:       });
   62: 
   63:       return;
   64:     }
   65: 
   66:     evidence.push({
   67:       id: `exam-trainer-${document.id}`,
   68:       topic: data.topic || "Mixed Topics",
   69:       source: "exam",
   70:       score:
   71:         typeof data.scorePercent === "number"
   72:           ? data.scorePercent
   73:           : typeof data.percentage === "number"
   74:             ? data.percentage
   75:             : 0,
   76:       completedAt,
   77:       weight: 1.25,
   78:     });
   79:   });
   80: 
   81:   return evidence;
   82: }

==============================================================================================================
FILE: services\examTrainerService.ts
==============================================================================================================
    1: import { indicativeGradeFromPercentage } from "@/services/qualificationGradeService";
    2: import type { Qualification } from "@/types/user";
    3: 
    4: import type {
    5:   ExamTrainerAnswer,
    6:   ExamTrainerDifficulty,
    7:   ExamTrainerMarkedAnswer,
    8:   ExamTrainerQuestion,
    9:   ExamTrainerReport,
   10: } from "@/types/examTrainer";
   11: 
   12: function normalise(value: string): string {
   13:   return value.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
   14: }
   15: 
   16: function automaticMark(
   17:   question: ExamTrainerQuestion,
   18:   response: string,
   19: ): ExamTrainerMarkedAnswer | null {
   20:   const accepted = [
   21:     question.correctAnswer,
   22:     ...(question.acceptedAnswers ?? []),
   23:   ].filter((value): value is string => Boolean(value));
   24: 
   25:   if (question.type !== "multiple-choice" && accepted.length === 0) {
   26:     return null;
   27:   }
   28: 
   29:   const correct = accepted.some(
   30:     (answer) => normalise(answer) === normalise(response),
   31:   );
   32: 
   33:   return {
   34:     questionId: question.id,
   35:     awardedMarks: correct ? question.marks : 0,
   36:     maximumMarks: question.marks,
   37:     percentage: correct ? 100 : 0,
   38:     feedback: correct
   39:       ? "Correct. You have matched the expected answer."
   40:       : "This answer does not match the expected response.",
   41:     matchedPoints: correct ? question.markScheme : [],
   42:     missingPoints: correct ? [] : question.markScheme,
   43:     improvedAnswer: question.modelAnswer,
   44:     mode: "automatic",
   45:   };
   46: }
   47: 
   48: async function markWrittenAnswer(
   49:   question: ExamTrainerQuestion,
   50:   response: string,
   51: ): Promise<ExamTrainerMarkedAnswer> {
   52:   const apiResponse = await fetch("/api/ai/mark-lesson-question", {
   53:     method: "POST",
   54:     headers: {
   55:       "Content-Type": "application/json",
   56:     },
   57:     body: JSON.stringify({
   58:       topic: question.topic,
   59:       lessonTitle: "Exam Question Trainer",
   60:       question: question.question,
   61:       maximumMarks: question.marks,
   62:       modelAnswer: question.modelAnswer,
   63:       markScheme: question.markScheme,
   64:       guidance: question.examinerGuidance ?? [],
   65:       studentResponse: response,
   66:     }),
   67:   });
   68: 
   69:   const result = (await apiResponse.json()) as {
   70:     awardedMarks?: number;
   71:     maximumMarks?: number;
   72:     percentage?: number;
   73:     feedback?: string;
   74:     matchedPoints?: string[];
   75:     missingPoints?: string[];
   76:     improvedAnswer?: string;
   77:     mode?: "ai" | "demo";
   78:     error?: string;
   79:   };
   80: 
   81:   if (!apiResponse.ok || typeof result.awardedMarks !== "number") {
   82:     throw new Error(result.error || "The written answer could not be marked.");
   83:   }
   84: 
   85:   return {
   86:     questionId: question.id,
   87:     awardedMarks: result.awardedMarks,
   88:     maximumMarks: result.maximumMarks ?? question.marks,
   89:     percentage:
   90:       result.percentage ??
   91:       Math.round((result.awardedMarks / question.marks) * 100),
   92:     feedback: result.feedback ?? "Your answer has been marked.",
   93:     matchedPoints: result.matchedPoints ?? [],
   94:     missingPoints: result.missingPoints ?? [],
   95:     improvedAnswer: result.improvedAnswer ?? question.modelAnswer,
   96:     mode: result.mode ?? "demo",
   97:   };
   98: }
   99: 
  100: export function buildExamQuestions({
  101:   topic,
  102:   difficulty,
  103:   count,
  104:   questionBank,
  105: }: {
  106:   topic: string;
  107:   difficulty: ExamTrainerDifficulty | "all";
  108:   count: number;
  109:   questionBank: ExamTrainerQuestion[];
  110: }): ExamTrainerQuestion[] {
  111:   const filtered = questionBank.filter((question) => {
  112:     const topicMatches = topic === "all" || question.topic === topic;
  113:     const difficultyMatches =
  114:       difficulty === "all" || question.difficulty === difficulty;
  115: 
  116:     return topicMatches && difficultyMatches;
  117:   });
  118: 
  119:   const shuffled = [...filtered].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
  120:   return shuffled.slice(0, Math.max(1, Math.min(count, shuffled.length)));
  121: }
  122: 
  123: export async function markExamTrainerAttempt({
  124:   questions,
  125:   answers,
  126:   qualification,
  127: }: {
  128:   questions: ExamTrainerQuestion[];
  129:   answers: ExamTrainerAnswer[];
  130:   qualification?: Qualification | "";
  131: }): Promise<ExamTrainerReport> {
  132:   const markedAnswers = await Promise.all(
  133:     questions.map(async (question) => {
  134:       const response =
  135:         answers.find((answer) => answer.questionId === question.id)?.response ??
  136:         "";
  137: 
  138:       if (!response.trim()) {
  139:         return {
  140:           questionId: question.id,
  141:           awardedMarks: 0,
  142:           maximumMarks: question.marks,
  143:           percentage: 0,
  144:           feedback: "No answer was provided.",
  145:           matchedPoints: [],
  146:           missingPoints: question.markScheme,
  147:           improvedAnswer: question.modelAnswer,
  148:           mode: "automatic" as const,
  149:         };
  150:       }
  151: 
  152:       const automatic = automaticMark(question, response);
  153: 
  154:       if (automatic) {
  155:         return automatic;
  156:       }
  157: 
  158:       try {
  159:         return await markWrittenAnswer(question, response);
  160:       } catch {
  161:         return {
  162:           questionId: question.id,
  163:           awardedMarks: 0,
  164:           maximumMarks: question.marks,
  165:           percentage: 0,
  166:           feedback:
  167:             "Automatic marking was unavailable. Review the model answer and mark scheme.",
  168:           matchedPoints: [],
  169:           missingPoints: question.markScheme,
  170:           improvedAnswer: question.modelAnswer,
  171:           mode: "demo" as const,
  172:         };
  173:       }
  174:     }),
  175:   );
  176: 
  177:   const totalAwardedMarks = markedAnswers.reduce(
  178:     (total, answer) => total + answer.awardedMarks,
  179:     0,
  180:   );
  181: 
  182:   const totalAvailableMarks = questions.reduce(
  183:     (total, question) => total + question.marks,
  184:     0,
  185:   );
  186: 
  187:   const percentage =
  188:     totalAvailableMarks > 0
  189:       ? Math.round((totalAwardedMarks / totalAvailableMarks) * 100)
  190:       : 0;
  191: 
  192:   const topicMap = new Map<
  193:     string,
  194:     { awardedMarks: number; availableMarks: number }
  195:   >();
  196: 
  197:   questions.forEach((question) => {
  198:     const marked = markedAnswers.find(
  199:       (answer) => answer.questionId === question.id,
  200:     );
  201: 
  202:     const existing = topicMap.get(question.topic) ?? {
  203:       awardedMarks: 0,
  204:       availableMarks: 0,
  205:     };
  206: 
  207:     topicMap.set(question.topic, {
  208:       awardedMarks: existing.awardedMarks + (marked?.awardedMarks ?? 0),
  209:       availableMarks: existing.availableMarks + question.marks,
  210:     });
  211:   });
  212: 
  213:   const topicScores = Array.from(topicMap.entries())
  214:     .map(([topic, values]) => ({
  215:       topic,
  216:       awardedMarks: values.awardedMarks,
  217:       availableMarks: values.availableMarks,
  218:       percentage:
  219:         values.availableMarks > 0
  220:           ? Math.round((values.awardedMarks / values.availableMarks) * 100)
  221:           : 0,
  222:     }))
  223:     .sort((first, second) => second.percentage - first.percentage);
  224: 
  225: 
  226:   return {
  227:     totalAwardedMarks,
  228:     totalAvailableMarks,
  229:     percentage,
  230:     grade: indicativeGradeFromPercentage(percentage, qualification),
  231:     topicScores,
  232:     strongestTopics: topicScores
  233:       .filter((topic) => topic.percentage >= 70)
  234:       .map((topic) => topic.topic),
  235:     priorityTopics: topicScores
  236:       .filter((topic) => topic.percentage < 60)
  237:       .map((topic) => topic.topic),
  238:     markedAnswers,
  239:   };
  240: }

FILE: services\quizAssignmentService.ts
==============================================================================================================
    1: import {
    2:   collection,
    3:   doc,
    4:   getDoc,
    5:   getDocs,
    6:   query,
    7:   Timestamp,
    8:   where,
    9: } from "firebase/firestore";
   10: 
   11: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
   12: 
   13: export type QuizAssignmentStatus = "not_started" | "completed";
   14: export type QuizAssignmentSource = "built-in" | "ai-generated";
   15: 
   16: export type StudentQuizAssignment = {
   17:   id: string;
   18:   teacherId: string;
   19:   classId: string;
   20:   title: string;
   21:   description: string;
   22:   resourceId: string;
   23:   type: "quiz";
   24:   quizSource: QuizAssignmentSource;
   25:   dueDate: Date | null;
   26:   createdAt: Date | null;
   27:   status: string;
   28:   className: string;
   29:   teacherName: string;
   30:   resultStatus: QuizAssignmentStatus;
   31:   score: number;
   32:   totalQuestions: number;
   33:   percentage: number;
   34:   earnedXP: number;
   35:   timeTakenSeconds: number;
   36:   completedAt: Date | null;
   37: };
   38: 
   39: type FirestoreDate = Timestamp | Date | string | null | undefined;
   40: 
   41: function convertDate(value: FirestoreDate): Date | null {
   42:   if (!value) return null;
   43:   if (value instanceof Date) return value;
   44:   if (value instanceof Timestamp) return value.toDate();
   45: 
   46:   if (typeof value === "string") {
   47:     const parsedDate = new Date(value);
   48:     return Number.isNaN(parsedDate.getTime()) ? null : parsedDate;
   49:   }
   50: 
   51:   return null;
   52: }
   53: 
   54: function removeDuplicateIds(values: string[]): string[] {
   55:   return Array.from(
   56:     new Set(values.map((value) => value.trim()).filter(Boolean)),
   57:   );
   58: }
   59: 
   60: function normaliseQuizSource(value: unknown): QuizAssignmentSource {
   61:   return value === "ai-generated" ? "ai-generated" : "built-in";
   62: }
   63: 
   64: async function getStudentClassIds(studentId: string): Promise<string[]> {
   65:   const userSnapshot = await getDoc(doc(db, "users", studentId));
   66: 
   67:   if (!userSnapshot.exists()) return [];
   68: 
   69:   const data = userSnapshot.data();
   70: 
   71:   if (!Array.isArray(data.classIds)) return [];
   72: 
   73:   return removeDuplicateIds(
   74:     data.classIds.filter((value): value is string => typeof value === "string"),
   75:   );
   76: }
   77: 
   78: function splitIntoChunks(values: string[], chunkSize: number): string[][] {
   79:   const chunks: string[][] = [];
   80: 
   81:   for (let index = 0; index < values.length; index += chunkSize) {
   82:     chunks.push(values.slice(index, index + chunkSize));
   83:   }
   84: 
   85:   return chunks;
   86: }
   87: 
   88: export async function getStudentQuizAssignments(
   89:   studentId: string,
   90: ): Promise<StudentQuizAssignment[]> {
   91:   const cleanedStudentId = studentId.trim();
   92: 
   93:   if (!cleanedStudentId) return [];
   94: 
   95:   const classIds = await getStudentClassIds(cleanedStudentId);
   96: 
   97:   if (classIds.length === 0) return [];
   98: 
   99:   const classIdChunks = splitIntoChunks(classIds, 30);
  100: 
  101:   const assignmentSnapshots = await Promise.all(
  102:     classIdChunks.map((chunk) =>
  103:       getDocs(
  104:         query(
  105:           collection(db, "assignments"),
  106:           where("classId", "in", chunk),
  107:           where("type", "==", "quiz"),
  108:         ),
  109:       ),
  110:     ),
  111:   );
  112: 
  113:   const assignmentDocuments = assignmentSnapshots.flatMap(
  114:     (snapshot) => snapshot.docs,
  115:   );
  116: 
  117:   const uniqueAssignmentDocuments = Array.from(
  118:     new Map(
  119:       assignmentDocuments.map((assignmentDocument) => [
  120:         assignmentDocument.id,
  121:         assignmentDocument,
  122:       ]),
  123:     ).values(),
  124:   );
  125: 
  126:   const loadedAssignments = await Promise.all(
  127:     uniqueAssignmentDocuments.map(async (assignmentDocument) => {
  128:       const assignment = assignmentDocument.data();
  129:       const assignmentId = assignmentDocument.id;
  130:       const resultId = `${assignmentId}_${cleanedStudentId}`;
  131:       const resultReference = doc(db, "assignmentResults", resultId);
  132: 
  133:       const classReference =
  134:         typeof assignment.classId === "string" && assignment.classId.trim()
  135:           ? doc(db, "classes", assignment.classId)
  136:           : null;
  137: 
  138:       const [resultSnapshot, classSnapshot] = await Promise.all([
  139:         getDoc(resultReference),
  140:         classReference ? getDoc(classReference) : Promise.resolve(null),
  141:       ]);
  142: 
  143:       const result = resultSnapshot.exists() ? resultSnapshot.data() : null;
  144:       const classData = classSnapshot?.exists() ? classSnapshot.data() : null;
  145:       const completed = result?.status === "completed";
  146: 
  147:       return {
  148:         id: assignmentId,
  149:         teacherId:
  150:           typeof assignment.teacherId === "string" ? assignment.teacherId : "",
  151:         classId:
  152:           typeof assignment.classId === "string" ? assignment.classId : "",
  153:         title:
  154:           typeof assignment.title === "string" && assignment.title.trim()
  155:             ? assignment.title
  156:             : "Untitled Quiz",
  157:         description:
  158:           typeof assignment.description === "string"
  159:             ? assignment.description
  160:             : "",
  161:         resourceId:
  162:           typeof assignment.resourceId === "string"
  163:             ? assignment.resourceId
  164:             : "",
  165:         type: "quiz" as const,
  166:         quizSource: normaliseQuizSource(assignment.quizSource),
  167:         dueDate: convertDate(assignment.dueDate),
  168:         createdAt: convertDate(assignment.createdAt),
  169:         status:
  170:           typeof assignment.status === "string" ? assignment.status : "active",
  171:         className:
  172:           typeof classData?.name === "string" && classData.name.trim()
  173:             ? classData.name
  174:             : "Assigned class",
  175:         teacherName: "Teacher",
  176:         resultStatus: completed ? "completed" : "not_started",
  177:         score: typeof result?.score === "number" ? result.score : 0,
  178:         totalQuestions:
  179:           typeof result?.totalQuestions === "number"
  180:             ? result.totalQuestions
  181:             : 0,
  182:         percentage:
  183:           typeof result?.percentage === "number" ? result.percentage : 0,
  184:         earnedXP: typeof result?.earnedXP === "number" ? result.earnedXP : 0,
  185:         timeTakenSeconds:
  186:           typeof result?.timeTakenSeconds === "number"
  187:             ? result.timeTakenSeconds
  188:             : 0,
  189:         completedAt: convertDate(result?.completedAt),
  190:       } satisfies StudentQuizAssignment;
  191:     }),
  192:   );
  193: 
  194:   return loadedAssignments
  195:     .filter(
  196:       (assignment) =>
  197:         assignment.status !== "cancelled" &&
  198:         Boolean(assignment.resourceId.trim()),
  199:     )
  200:     .sort((first, second) => {
  201:       const firstDueDate = first.dueDate?.getTime() ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
  202:       const secondDueDate =
  203:         second.dueDate?.getTime() ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
  204: 
  205:       if (firstDueDate !== secondDueDate) return firstDueDate - secondDueDate;
  206: 
  207:       const firstCreatedAt = first.createdAt?.getTime() ?? 0;
  208:       const secondCreatedAt = second.createdAt?.getTime() ?? 0;
  209: 
  210:       return secondCreatedAt - firstCreatedAt;
  211:     });
  212: }

FILE: services\quizService.ts
==============================================================================================================
    1: import { doc, increment, serverTimestamp, writeBatch } from "firebase/firestore";
    2: 
    3: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
    4: 
    5: type SaveQuizResultParams = {
    6:   uid: string;
    7:   quizId: string;
    8:   topicId: string;
    9:   title: string;
   10:   scorePercent: number;
   11:   correctCount: number;
   12:   totalQuestions: number;
   13:   earnedXP: number;
   14: };
   15: 
   16: function createQuizResultId(quizId: string, uid: string): string {
   17:   const safeQuizId = quizId.trim().replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, "-");
   18: 
   19:   return `${safeQuizId}_${uid}`;
   20: }
   21: 
   22: export async function saveQuizResult({
   23:   uid,
   24:   quizId,
   25:   topicId,
   26:   title,
   27:   scorePercent,
   28:   correctCount,
   29:   totalQuestions,
   30:   earnedXP,
   31: }: SaveQuizResultParams): Promise<string> {
   32:   const cleanedUid = uid.trim();
   33:   const cleanedQuizId = quizId.trim();
   34: 
   35:   if (!cleanedUid) {
   36:     throw new Error("A valid student account is required.");
   37:   }
   38: 
   39:   if (!cleanedQuizId) {
   40:     throw new Error("A valid quiz is required.");
   41:   }
   42: 
   43:   const safeScorePercent = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(scorePercent)));
   44: 
   45:   const safeCorrectCount = Math.max(
   46:     0,
   47:     Math.min(Math.round(correctCount), Math.round(totalQuestions)),
   48:   );
   49: 
   50:   const safeTotalQuestions = Math.max(0, Math.round(totalQuestions));
   51: 
   52:   const safeEarnedXP = Math.max(0, Math.round(earnedXP));
   53: 
   54:   const resultId = createQuizResultId(cleanedQuizId, cleanedUid);
   55: 
   56:   const resultReference = doc(db, "users", cleanedUid, "quizResults", resultId);
   57: 
   58:   const userReference = doc(db, "users", cleanedUid);
   59: 
   60:   const batch = writeBatch(db);
   61: 
   62:   /*
   63:    * The uid field is required by the current
   64:    * Firestore security rules.
   65:    *
   66:    * A deterministic result ID prevents repeated
   67:    * page renders from creating duplicate quiz results.
   68:    */
   69:   batch.set(
   70:     resultReference,
   71:     {
   72:       uid: cleanedUid,
   73:       quizId: cleanedQuizId,
   74:       topicId: topicId.trim(),
   75:       title: title.trim() || "Untitled Quiz",
   76:       scorePercent: safeScorePercent,
   77:       correctCount: safeCorrectCount,
   78:       totalQuestions: safeTotalQuestions,
   79:       earnedXP: safeEarnedXP,
   80:       status: "completed",
   81:       completedAt: serverTimestamp(),
   82:       createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
   83:       updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
   84:     },
   85:     {
   86:       merge: true,
   87:     },
   88:   );
   89: 
   90:   /*
   91:    * Award XP once for this save operation.
   92:    */
   93:   batch.update(userReference, {
   94:     xp: increment(safeEarnedXP),
   95:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
   96:   });
   97: 
   98:   await batch.commit();
   99: 
  100:   return resultId;
  101: }

  274: export function downloadExamQuestionLevelAnalysisCsv({
  275:   assignment,
  276:   intelligence,
  277: }: {
  278:   assignment: ExamAssignment;
  279:   intelligence: ExamClassIntelligence;
  280: }): void {
  281:   if (typeof window === "undefined") {
  282:     return;
  283:   }
  284: 
  285:   const csv =
  286:     buildExamQuestionLevelAnalysisCsv({
  287:       assignment,
  288:       intelligence,
  289:     });
  290: 
  291:   const blob = new Blob(
  292:     ["\uFEFF", csv],
  293:     {
  294:       type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8;",
  295:     },
  296:   );
  297: 
  298:   const url =
  299:     URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  300: 
  301:   const link =
  302:     document.createElement("a");
  303: 
  304:   link.href = url;
  305:   link.download =
  306:     `${safeFileName(assignment.className)}-` +
  307:     `${safeFileName(assignment.title)}-final-qla.csv`;
  308: 
  309:   document.body.appendChild(link);
  310:   link.click();
  311:   link.remove();
  312: 
  313:   window.setTimeout(() => {
  314:     URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  315:   }, 0);
  316: }

  118: function convertDate(value: FirestoreDate): Date | null {
  119:   if (!value) return null;
  120:   if (value instanceof Date) return value;
  121:   if (value instanceof Timestamp) return value.toDate();
  122: 
  123:   if (typeof value === "string") {
  124:     const parsed = new Date(value);
  125:     return Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime()) ? null : parsed;
  126:   }
  127: 
  128:   return null;
  129: }
  130: 
  131: function normaliseStatus(value: unknown): QuizAssignmentStatus {
  132:   return value === "closed" || value === "cancelled" ? value : "active";
  133: }
  134: 
  135: function normaliseDeliveryMode(value: unknown): QuizDeliveryMode {
  136:   return value === "assessment" ? "assessment" : "practice";
  137: }
  138: 
  139: function safeNumber(value: unknown): number {
  140:   return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : 0;
  141: }
  142: 
  143: function normaliseIncident(
  144:   value: FirestoreIntegrityIncident,
  145: ): TeacherQuizIntegrityIncident {
  146:   const allowedTypes = new Set<QuizIntegrityIncidentType>([
  147:     "fullscreen_exit",
  148:     "fullscreen_restored",
  149:     "page_hidden",
  150:     "page_visible",
  151:     "auto_submit",
  152:   ]);
  153: 
  154:   const type =
  155:     typeof value.type === "string" &&
  156:     allowedTypes.has(value.type as QuizIntegrityIncidentType)
  157:       ? (value.type as QuizIntegrityIncidentType)
  158:       : "page_hidden";
  159: 
  160:   return {
  161:     id: typeof value.id === "string" ? value.id : "incident",
  162:     type,
  163:     occurredAt: convertDate(value.occurredAt as FirestoreDate),
  164:     questionNumber:
  165:       typeof value.questionNumber === "number" &&
  166:       Number.isFinite(value.questionNumber)
  167:         ? value.questionNumber
  168:         : null,
  169:     detail: typeof value.detail === "string" ? value.detail : "Integrity event recorded.",
  170:   };
  171: }
  172: 
  173: function calculateCompletionPercentage(completedCount: number, studentCount: number) {
  174:   return studentCount <= 0 ? 0 : Math.round((completedCount / studentCount) * 100);
  175: }
  176: 
  177: function calculateAveragePercentage(results: FirestoreResult[]) {
  178:   if (results.length === 0) return 0;
  179:   return Math.round(
  180:     results.reduce((sum, result) => sum + safeNumber(result.percentage), 0) /
  181:       results.length,
  182:   );
  183: }
  184: 
  185: async function getQuizAssignmentResults(
  186:   assignmentId: string,
  187:   teacherId: string,
  188: ): Promise<FirestoreResult[]> {
  189:   const cleanedAssignmentId = assignmentId.trim();
  190:   const cleanedTeacherId = teacherId.trim();
  191: 
  192:   if (!cleanedAssignmentId || !cleanedTeacherId) return [];
  193: 
  194:   const snapshot = await getDocs(
  195:     query(
  196:       collection(db, "assignmentResults"),
  197:       where("assignmentId", "==", cleanedAssignmentId),
  198:       where("teacherId", "==", cleanedTeacherId),
  199:     ),
  200:   );
  201: 
  202:   return snapshot.docs
  203:     .map((document) => document.data() as FirestoreResult)
  204:     .filter(
  205:       (result) =>
  206:         result.assignmentType === "quiz" &&
  207:         result.status === "completed" &&
  208:         result.teacherId === cleanedTeacherId &&
  209:         result.assignmentId === cleanedAssignmentId,
  210:     );
  211: }
  212: 
  213: async function getClassInformation(classId: string): Promise<{
  214:   className: string;
  215:   studentIds: string[];
  216: }> {
  217:   const cleanedClassId = classId.trim();
  218: 
  219:   if (!cleanedClassId) {
  220:     return { className: "Unknown class", studentIds: [] };
  221:   }
  222: 
  223:   const snapshot = await getDoc(doc(db, "classes", cleanedClassId));
  224: 
  225:   if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  226:     return { className: "Unknown class", studentIds: [] };
  227:   }
  228: 
  229:   const data = snapshot.data();
  230:   const studentIds = Array.isArray(data.studentIds)
  231:     ? data.studentIds.filter(
  232:         (value): value is string => typeof value === "string" && Boolean(value.trim()),
  233:       )
  234:     : [];
  235: 
  236:   return {
  237:     className:
  238:       typeof data.name === "string" && data.name.trim()
  239:         ? data.name
  240:         : "Untitled class",
  241:     studentIds: Array.from(new Set(studentIds.map((id) => id.trim()))),
  242:   };
  243: }
  244: 
  245: export async function getTeacherQuizAssignments(
  246:   teacherId: string,
  247: ): Promise<TeacherQuizAssignmentSummary[]> {
  248:   const cleanedTeacherId = teacherId.trim();
  249:   if (!cleanedTeacherId) return [];
  250: 
  251:   const assignmentsSnapshot = await getDocs(
  252:     query(collection(db, "assignments"), where("teacherId", "==", cleanedTeacherId)),
  253:   );
  254: 
  255:   const quizDocuments = assignmentsSnapshot.docs.filter((document) => {
  256:     const data = document.data();
  257:     return data.type === "quiz" && data.teacherId === cleanedTeacherId;
  258:   });
  259: 
  260:   const summaries = await Promise.all(
  261:     quizDocuments.map(async (assignmentDocument) => {
  262:       const assignment = assignmentDocument.data() as FirestoreAssignment;
  263:       const classId = assignment.classId || "";
  264:       const [classInformation, results] = await Promise.all([
  265:         getClassInformation(classId),
  266:         getQuizAssignmentResults(assignmentDocument.id, cleanedTeacherId),
  267:       ]);
  268: 
  269:       const completedCount = new Set(
  270:         results.map((result) => result.studentId || "").filter(Boolean),
  271:       ).size;
  272: 
  273:       return {
  274:         id: assignmentDocument.id,
  275:         teacherId: assignment.teacherId || "",
  276:         classId,
  277:         className: classInformation.className,
  278:         title: assignment.title || "Untitled Quiz",
  279:         description: assignment.description || "",
  280:         resourceId: assignment.resourceId || "",
  281:         dueDate: convertDate(assignment.dueDate),
  282:         createdAt: convertDate(assignment.createdAt),
  283:         status: normaliseStatus(assignment.status),
  284:         deliveryMode: normaliseDeliveryMode(assignment.deliveryMode),
  285:         studentCount: classInformation.studentIds.length,
  286:         completedCount,
  287:         completionPercentage: calculateCompletionPercentage(
  288:           completedCount,
  289:           classInformation.studentIds.length,
  290:         ),
  291:         averagePercentage: calculateAveragePercentage(results),
  292:         integrityTerminatedCount: results.filter(
  293:           (result) => result.integrityTerminated === true,
  294:         ).length,
  295:       } satisfies TeacherQuizAssignmentSummary;
  296:     }),
  297:   );
  298: 
  299:   return summaries.sort(
  300:     (a, b) => (b.createdAt?.getTime() ?? 0) - (a.createdAt?.getTime() ?? 0),
  301:   );
  302: }
  303: 
  304: export async function getTeacherQuizAssignmentDetail(
  305:   assignmentId: string,
  306:   teacherId: string,
  307: ): Promise<TeacherQuizAssignmentDetail | null> {
  308:   const cleanedAssignmentId = assignmentId.trim();
  309:   const cleanedTeacherId = teacherId.trim();
  310: 
  311:   if (!cleanedAssignmentId || !cleanedTeacherId) return null;
  312: 
  313:   const assignmentSnapshot = await getDoc(
  314:     doc(db, "assignments", cleanedAssignmentId),
  315:   );
  316: 
  317:   if (!assignmentSnapshot.exists()) return null;
  318: 
  319:   const assignment = assignmentSnapshot.data() as FirestoreAssignment;
  320: 
  321:   if (assignment.type !== "quiz" || assignment.teacherId !== cleanedTeacherId) {
  322:     return null;
  323:   }
  324: 
  325:   const classId = assignment.classId || "";
  326:   const [classInformation, results] = await Promise.all([
  327:     getClassInformation(classId),
  328:     getQuizAssignmentResults(cleanedAssignmentId, cleanedTeacherId),
  329:   ]);
  330: 
  331:   const resultByStudentId = new Map<string, FirestoreResult>();
  332:   results.forEach((result) => {
  333:     if (result.studentId) resultByStudentId.set(result.studentId, result);
  334:   });
  335: 
  336:   const students = await Promise.all(
  337:     classInformation.studentIds.map(async (studentId) => {
  338:       const profileSnapshot = await getDoc(doc(db, "users", studentId));
  339:       const profile = profileSnapshot.exists() ? profileSnapshot.data() : null;
  340:       const result = resultByStudentId.get(studentId);
  341: 
  342:       return {
  343:         studentId,
  344:         studentName:
  345:           typeof profile?.name === "string" && profile.name.trim()
  346:             ? profile.name
  347:             : "Student",
  348:         studentEmail:
  349:           typeof profile?.email === "string" && profile.email.trim()
  350:             ? profile.email
  351:             : "No email available",
  352:         status: result ? "completed" : "not_started",
  353:         score: safeNumber(result?.score),
  354:         totalQuestions: safeNumber(result?.totalQuestions),
  355:         percentage: safeNumber(result?.percentage),
  356: 
  357:         /*
  358:          * Integrity-terminated assessments never surface XP in the markbook.
  359:          * This also corrects older records created before the zero-XP rule
  360:          * was enforced at write time.
  361:          */
  362:         earnedXP:
  363:           result?.integrityTerminated === true
  364:             ? 0
  365:             : safeNumber(result?.earnedXP),
  366: 
  367:         timeTakenSeconds: safeNumber(result?.timeTakenSeconds),
  368:         completedAt: convertDate(result?.completedAt),
  369:         deliveryMode: normaliseDeliveryMode(result?.deliveryMode ?? assignment.deliveryMode),
  370:         integritySessionStartedAt: convertDate(result?.integritySessionStartedAt),
  371:         integrityIncidents: Array.isArray(result?.integrityIncidents)
  372:           ? result.integrityIncidents.map(normaliseIncident)
  373:           : [],
  374:         integrityTerminated: result?.integrityTerminated === true,
  375:         integrityTerminationReason:
  376:           typeof result?.integrityTerminationReason === "string"
  377:             ? result.integrityTerminationReason
  378:             : "",
  379:       } satisfies TeacherQuizStudentResult;
  380:     }),
  381:   );
  382: 
  383:   students.sort((a, b) => a.studentName.localeCompare(b.studentName));
  384: 
  385:   const completedCount = students.filter((student) => student.status === "completed").length;
  386:   const studentCount = students.length;
  387: 
  388:   return {
  389:     assignment: {
  390:       id: assignmentSnapshot.id,
  391:       teacherId: assignment.teacherId || "",
  392:       classId,
  393:       className: classInformation.className,
  394:       title: assignment.title || "Untitled Quiz",
  395:       description: assignment.description || "",
  396:       resourceId: assignment.resourceId || "",
  397:       dueDate: convertDate(assignment.dueDate),
  398:       createdAt: convertDate(assignment.createdAt),
  399:       status: normaliseStatus(assignment.status),
  400:       deliveryMode: normaliseDeliveryMode(assignment.deliveryMode),
  401:       studentCount,
  402:       completedCount,
  403:       completionPercentage: calculateCompletionPercentage(completedCount, studentCount),
  404:       averagePercentage: calculateAveragePercentage(results),
  405:       integrityTerminatedCount: students.filter((student) => student.integrityTerminated).length,
  406:     },
  407:     students,
  408:   };
  409: }
